* No listed object toggles or is hit in the merged database.

[cols="2,3",options="header"]
|===
|File (`cov/config/vcs/`) |Excludes
|`sep_regblock_exclusions.el` |PeakRDL regblock literal ties: no-stall, no-error decode and response, `load_next`, reserved and width-cast bits, and the response ports that carry them
|`sep_unreachable_exclusions.el` |Tied SEP DMA inputs: upper `lsio_trigger` bits, the CTN response and the alert ping
|`sep_master_tie_exclusions.el` |Request fields on ports that one internal master drives with a constant: VeeR IFU, LSU (pre-remap), debug, secure DMA and the KM picorv32 CPU
|`sep_xbar_tie_exclusions.el` |Crossbar and interconnect bits that copy a listed master tie or that a target or bridge ties
|`sep_system_tie_exclusions.el` |Ties in the CPU wrapper, reset, IO/SPI, watchdog, DMA, system peripherals, memory interfaces, entropy FIFO and the integration shims
|`sep_crypto_tie_exclusions.el` |Ties in `sep_crypto`, the TRNG, the key manager and the eFuse command path
|`sep_fsm_unreachable_exclusions.el` |`axi_lite_to_tlul` FSM transitions that `ACK_ZERO_STROBE_WRITE` disables
|`sep_cond_branch_exclusions.el` |Condition and branch terms that literal ties and elaboration parameters hold constant
|===

===== PeakRDL register blocks

`cov/config/vcs/sep_regblock_exclusions.el` excludes generated register-block
signals that a literal in the generated RTL holds constant:

* No-stall: a block whose cpuif hardwires `cpuif_req_stall_rd` and
  `cpuif_req_stall_wr` to `'0`, for example
  `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/sv/blocks/sep_scratch_reg.sv`. A block with external
  registers drives the stall from `external_pending` (`efuse_mmr_reg`,
  `entropy_source_reg`, `km_kpv_reg`), so its stall stays graded.
* No-error: a register space that decodes no error holds `is_valid_addr` and
  `is_valid_rw` high and every error net low, so its AXI-Lite `bresp`/`rresp`
  and its APB `pslverr` hold OKAY. Two entries carry this response one level
  up: `efuse_token_processing` `apb_resp_o.pslverr` and the B/R response
  outputs of the eFuse interface shim. The copies of the shim response inside
  `u_sep` arrive through an integrator input port and stay graded.
* Other literals: `sep_cpu_ctrl_reg` and `filter_ctrl_reg` `load_next` nets
  that the generator assigns `'1` in both arms, the reserved
  `SEP_NMI_VEC.rsvd` field, and the `sep_scratch_reg` address bits [2:0] and
  data bits [63:32].

The wrapper-key register blocks and the KM `km_csr`, `km_drbg_sampler` and
`km_mailbox_*` blocks decode errors, so their error responses stay graded.
The fabric SLVERR paths (the AXI-Lite demux error slave, the `axi_filter`
datapath and the crossbar decode error slave) stay graded.

===== SEP DMA tied inputs

`cov/config/vcs/sep_unreachable_exclusions.el` contains instance-scoped blocks
on `sep_uvm_top.u_dut.u_sep.u_sep_dma_wrap`:

* `hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep.sv` ties `lsio_trigger[10:1]` low. Bit 0 carries the SPI
  request and stays graded.
* `hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_dma_wrap.sv` drives the CTN TL-UL response
  (`ctn_tl_d2h`) to constants and ties the alert receiver `ping_req_i` low.
  The DMA host path and the alert request and acknowledge stay graded.

===== Single-master AXI ties

`cov/config/vcs/sep_master_tie_exclusions.el` excludes request fields on ports
that exactly one internal master drives, where that master's RTL assigns the
field a constant and every module on the path passes it unchanged:

* VeeR IFU: the pre-remap AR address bits [2:0]
  (`vendor/chipsalliance/Cores-VeeR-EL2/upstream/design/ifu/el2_ifu_mem_ctl.sv`).
* VeeR LSU: AxSIZE bit 2 and `rready` on the pre-remap `lsu_axi_req_raw` only.
  The no_cpu bench force drives the LSU request after the window remap, so
  every post-remap LSU field stays graded.
* VeeR debug system-bus master: its ID, AxPROT, AxCACHE, length, burst, QoS,
  lock and last ties.
* Secure DMA host port: the burst, ID, length, lock, QoS, region, atop, user
  and cache ties of `axi_lite_to_axi` and `hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_dma_wrap.sv`.
* KM picorv32 CPU: address bits [1:0] and the AxPROT ties, from the CPU through
  the KM AXI-Lite crossbar to each KM target and the key CSR blocks of the
  crypto wrappers. The KM CPU is the only initiator on that bus
  (`hw/ip/key_manager/rtl/key_manager.sv`).

A port that the inbound SoC port, the JTAG bridge or a second master can reach
is not listed. The IFU never writes, so its raw write address is 0. After the
IFU window remap that address is 0 or `0x1000_0000`:
`hw/ip/axi_window_remap/rtl/axi_window_remap.sv` moves a raw address of 0 to
`0x1000_0000` when `SEP_LOCAL_BASE_ADDR[31:0]` is 0, and software writes that
CSR (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl/sep_cpu_ctrl.rdl`). Bit 28 of those
`aw.addr` fields stays graded; the other bits are listed.

===== Crossbar and interconnect ties

`cov/config/vcs/sep_xbar_tie_exclusions.el` excludes bits in
`sep_local_axi_xbar`, `sep_system_peripherals_xbar` and
`sep_crypto_axi_interconnect` that take no second value in any legal system: a
direct copy of an IFU, debug or DMA field that the master list excludes, a
`cpu_tcm` target field that the DMA ties, the DMA AxPROT, the `cpu_tcm`
response `rlast` and USER, the `reset_ctrl` read data bits [63:7], mailbox
response bit 0, the external ID bit that `axi_id_remap` holds at 0, the tied
`test_i` input and the OTBN AxCACHE bit that the interconnect forces to 1.
A field that the inbound SoC port, an integrator-driven target, the no_cpu
bench force on the LSU port or a free CPU or DMA field can reach stays graded.
An address bit that only a decode range bounds stays graded.

===== System logic ties

`cov/config/vcs/sep_system_tie_exclusions.el` excludes toggle bits, condition
vectors, branch arms and FSM transitions in the SEP system logic. Its main tie
families are:

* Error-slave ties: the request and response literals inside the AXI-Lite
  error slaves, and the read side of the per-entry filter and remap lock
  demuxes, whose read select
  `hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_system_peripherals/rtl/sep_system_csr.sv` ties to 0.
* Filter and remap ties: the traffic-filter data-width and group-ID
  parameters, the outbound-filter skip input, the output-remap address bit 3
  and USER override, and the constant region size and base of the CPU alias
  remaps (`hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_cpu.sv`).
* CPU wrapper ties: the IFU and debug request literals on the wrapper nets,
  the IFU decode-error slave response, the lockstep and trace ties, and a
  non-secure entropy FIFO whose error output is a constant.
* Bridge and memory-interface ties: `axi_lite_to_tlul` and `tlul_to_axi_lite`
  literals, integrity generation that SEP enables, DMA TL-UL size and
  alignment, memory-interface CSR and no-error ties, and the ROM, SRAM and
  eFuse interface shims of the reference integration.

The response echo fields `d_source` and `d_size` stay graded. A net that a
master, the inbound SoC port, JTAG, an integrator port or a software-writable
CSR can move stays graded.

===== sep_crypto ties

`cov/config/vcs/sep_crypto_tie_exclusions.el` excludes bits and one block that
a literal tie, an elaborated parameter, a localparam or a width cast in SEP RTL
fixes. Its main tie families are:

* eFuse ties: the field map, which
  `hw/sys/sep/rtl/efuse/sep_efuse_wrapper.sv` ties to the localparam
  `EfuseFieldMap` of `hw/sys/sep/rtl/efuse/sep_efuse_pkg.sv`, the command
  literals, the security-disable token rails and the ID-converter pad bits.
* Key manager ties: the OTP error slave, the scramblers, the mailbox depth
  pad, the virtual-ROM decode, the unused key-bus read data and the response
  codes that never return EXOKAY.
* TRNG ties: entropy-source FIFO inputs and no-error responses, DRBG strobe
  and FIFO ties, and the pool EDN cancel.
* Wrapper ties: the key-vault shim defaults and write response, alert ping
  lines, AHB control, OTBN OTP key and memory error, ABR memory address pad,
  and the unused response bit 0 of bridges that return only OKAY or SLVERR.

Key storage, key transfer, alert handshakes and every error response that a
test can provoke stay graded.

===== Unreachable FSM transitions

`cov/config/vcs/sep_fsm_unreachable_exclusions.el` excludes eight
`axi_lite_to_tlul` transitions that depend on `ACK_ZERO_STROBE_WRITE`
(`hw/common/axi/axi_lite_to_tlul.sv`), which is off in every SEP instance
except the SPI host wrapper (`hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_ot_spi_wrap.sv`), whose
transitions stay graded. Transitions on a reset with a transfer outstanding,
error transitions and abort transitions stay graded.

===== Condition and branch ties

`cov/config/vcs/sep_cond_branch_exclusions.el` excludes condition and branch
terms that a literal tie or an elaboration parameter holds constant: the stall
terms of no-stall PeakRDL regblocks, the error operand and error branches of
regblocks that decode no error, `load_next` arms that the generator assigns
`'1`, the KM CPU virtual-ROM decode and the KM ROM base compare, branches
inside elaboration-only constant functions, the else arms of the integrity
generation, `ACK_ZERO_STROBE_WRITE` and `HAS_LC_STATE` parameters, literal
input ties inside SEP (entropy-source FIFO, pool EDN cancel, outbound-filter
skip, SPI `err_clr`, output-remap address bit 3, IFU write address) and the
constant eFuse `sw_lock` bits.

[[sep-fcov-waiver-list]]
==== Waiver list

None. The policy names no `role = "waive"` file, so no point leaves the
denominator for a reason other than the scope and the exclusion lists above.

==== Adding a bin

Add a Phase 1 bin only when an `all`-group test already walks that cell, or
when a new test is added in the same change. A cell the suite does not walk
belongs in <<sep-fcov-planned>>. Keep covergroup instance names stable
when a module is added: the URG Group report matches them by hierarchy name.

[[sep-fcov-traceability]]
=== VPLAN to FCOV Traceability

Each row names a section of the Scenario-Expanded Test Summary in
<<sep-dv-verification-plan,SEP_VPLAN>> and the Phase 1 covergroups of
`cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` that record the behaviour of that section. A covergroup
is listed against a section when one of its owning tests (<<sep-fcov-owners>>,
<<sep-fcov-rand>>) is a row of that section's table, so one group can appear
in more than one row. The mapping is the plan's. Which leaf fills each bin is a
property of a run, and the URG Group pages of that run show it. A test whose
own contract has no Phase 1 bin is listed under <<sep-fcov-owners>>.

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|VPLAN section |Coverage
|0. Smoke |`sep_axi_decode_cg`, `sep_sram_access_cg`, `sep_boot_rom_cg`, `sep_cpu_boot_cg`, `sep_otbn_execute_cg`, `sep_spi_flash_cg`, `sep_efuse_sense_cg`
|Phase 1 representatives (areas 1–7) |`sep_axi_decode_cg`, `sep_kmac_mode_cg`, `sep_esrc_edn_flow_cg`, `sep_crypto_isolate_cg`, `sep_dma_copy_hash_cg`, `sep_spi_flash_cg`, `sep_efuse_sense_cg`, `sep_lc_state_cg`, `sep_dma_completion_route_cg`, `sep_feat_ctrl_cg`, `sep_mailbox_pic_cg`, `sep_mailbox_channel_cg`, `sep_wdt_bark_cg`
|Crypto and entropy |`sep_smn_inbound_cg`, `sep_aes_mode_cg`, `sep_hmac_mode_cg`, `sep_kmac_mode_cg`, `sep_otbn_execute_cg`, `sep_abr_keygen_cg`, `sep_abr_sign_cg`, `sep_abr_mlkem_cg`, `sep_esrc_edn_flow_cg`, `sep_crypto_isolate_cg`
|Fabric and remap |`sep_axi_decode_cg`, `sep_inbound_filter_allow_cg`
|Mailbox, IRQ, PIC |`sep_mailbox_pic_cg`, `sep_mbox_rand_cg`, `sep_spi_flash_cg`, `sep_spi_host_rand_cg`
|SPI and DMA |`sep_dma_copy_hash_cg`, `sep_spi_flash_cg`, `sep_dma_completion_route_cg`, `sep_dma_rand_cg`
|Memory, boot, reset, WDT, clock |`sep_sram_access_cg`, `sep_boot_rom_cg`, `sep_wdt_bark_cg`, `sep_scratch_reset_cg`
|eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field |`sep_efuse_program_lock_cg`, `sep_efuse_fail_closed_cg`, `sep_lc_transition_cg`, `sep_feat_ctrl_cg`, `sep_sec_dis_boot_cg`, `sep_lc_demote_cg`
|Key Manager vault |`sep_km_generate_cg`, `sep_km_command_sideload_cg`, `sep_km_host_cmd_cg`, `sep_km_wipe_cg`, `sep_km_sw_reset_cg`, `sep_crypto_isolate_cg`, `sep_mailbox_pic_cg`
|===

[[sep-fcov-collection]]
=== Collection Strategy

`sep_fcov` is a passive TB-side sampler. It drives nothing.

Its CSR side reads the post-remap CPU-LSU AXI bus, wired from the same node
`tb_top` already mirrors to its flat `s_axi_*` ports and force-splices on the
no-CPU builds -- not a new probe, and not a Python path into the fabric
(`sep_public_scope.vlt` keeps public marks off it). The flat `s_axi_*` request
ports carry TB stimulus only, so under `+cpu_boot` the EL2 owns the bus and a
port-side sampler would score nothing on any firmware test. Everything else is
the real `m_axi_*` DUT port or a `tb_top` probe port.

The sampler also reads two DUT nets directly, read-only like the LSU bus: the
WDT bark interrupt that drives the CPU NMI (`intr_wdog_timer_bark`) and the LSU
read `ARSIZE`. Through existing `tb_top` probe ports it reads the HMAC and ABR
host isolate requests, `sep_reset_n` and the SPI `sd[0]` pad.

Register data bins are gated on exactly ONE outstanding AXI transaction in
that direction, and that transaction must be a single beat, so the address
that labels a bin is unambiguously the address that carried the data. A burst
would pair its start address with a later beat's data, and a W beat accepted
before its own AW belongs to the next transaction; both are skipped. A
pipelined burst of firmware traffic is therefore sampled only where it is
serial: a missed sample is a missed hit, never a false one.

The LSU demux sits inside `sep_cpu`, which resets on `sep_cpu_reset_n`, so the
outstanding counts restart when that reset asserts, as well as on the cold
reset. A warm reset that drops a transaction in flight would otherwise leave a
stale count and gate off every bus-decoded bin for the rest of the run.
Counting goes on while the reset is held, because the no-CPU bus still
completes transactions then (the SEC_DIS leaves read `LC_STATE` with
`sep_cpu_reset_n` low), and a response with no count does not decrement. The
SMN inbound counts restart when `sep_reset_n` asserts, because the inbound
filter sits in `sep_system_peripherals`, which resets on it.

A covergroup bin is an interface event. It is not a substitute for a VPLAN
checker that compares a golden (digest, banner text, shadow image).

Collect with `--tool vcs --cov --target default` and read the URG Group pages
for `u_sep_fcov`. The code-coverage number (line/cond/toggle/FSM/branch) is a
different number, and the sampler is not part of it:
`cov/config/vcs/sep_cov_scope.hier` removes `sep_uvm_top.u_sep_fcov` from code
coverage, so only its covergroups are graded, as the Group number. The URG
headline SCORE averages both.

`OCAH_FCOV_COVER` cover-property points are unused.

[[sep-fcov-exclusions]]
=== Exclusions and Limitations

[cols="1,2",options="header"]
|===
|Item |Reason
|SV covergroups under Verilator |Unsupported. Phase 1 groups are VCS-only (`ifndef VERILATOR`)
|Verilator cover-property FCOV |Not used. Phase 1 is covergroups only; no `OCAH_FCOV_COVER` points are defined
|Toggle coverage under Verilator |Database size; commercial flow only
|`ocah_axi_cov_if` |Commercial-flow asset; not on the Verilator filelist
|Python probes of DUT internals |No route; public scope stays on `sep_uvm_top`
|`rom_fw` class |Other owner; not in `all`
|`sep_abr_absent_decerr_test` |`tools = ["verilator"]`: it needs the `lsu_stub_abr_absent` elaboration, and a VCS coverage run builds `--target default`, which contains ABR. No covergroup samples ABR-absent behaviour
|`CMD_ABR_SK_TRANSFER` consume |Graded by the `sep_km_abr_mlkem_sideload_test` checkers after a live ABR capture. No covergroup samples the handshake
|===

The exclusion lists of the code-coverage report are in
<<sep-fcov-exclusion-lists>>.

One limitation of the code-coverage figure is stated where it applies and is
repeated here, so that no reader credits the figure with more than it covers.
Only `[coverage.vcs]` applies the compile-time scope: a
`--cov --tool verilator` run grades an unscoped denominator, and its figure is
not this one.

[[sep-fcov-planned]]
=== Phase 2 Planned Groups

The Phase 2 groups are the functional coverage of the Phase 3 tests of
<<sep-dv-verification-plan,SEP_VPLAN>> (<<sep-vplan-phase3>>). The VPLAN tier
is Phase 3 and the FCOV tier is Phase 2. Both name the same work, and the
owning tests of a Phase 2 group are Phase 3 tests.

A Phase 2 group earns credit when the sampler collects it and its owning test
is in `all`. From then on the fill rules of <<sep-fcov-rand-phase2>> apply: a
bin that is not seed-dependent fills in one VCS `all --cov` regression, and a
seed-dependent bin closes over a merge of regressions. Each group defines the bins of a VCS covergroup in
`cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` under `ifndef VERILATOR`. The group `sep_fabric_port_silence_cp`
is a set of cover properties in the same sampler. Verilator compiles no
covergroup, so a Verilator run of an owning test grades by checker only. Every
coverpoint samples a DUT signal. No coverpoint reads log text or a scoreboard
count. Bins come from the specification and the register description. A bin
is an interface event and not a golden comparison. A group stays in this
section only when at least one Phase 3 test reaches a bin that the group
defines (<<sep-fcov-p2-dropped>>). A bin that no stimulus reaches is not counted
as coverage. The signal paths that do not resolve in the testbench are in
<<sep-fcov-p2-unresolved>>.

[[sep-fcov-parked]]
*Class words.* The class words PROPOSED, Blocked, Executable and Parked, and
the terms observation tap, added hook, existing bench hook and preload, have the meanings that
<<sep-vplan-credit-rules,SEP_VPLAN>> defines. For the Phase 2 groups:

* *PROPOSED*: a drive, force or deposit of a net or a state inside the `sep`
module for a purpose other than error injection, that is not in
`hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` and not in the closed set of
`docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` (<<sep-vplan-phase3-proposed-drives,SEP_VPLAN>>).
* *Observation tap*: a testbench variable, an XMR probe port, an observation
`bind` or a hierarchical read reference of a DUT net. It reads only and is not
PROPOSED. A tap, probe or sampler reference that `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` does
not list is an observation tap the owning test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH
observation-probe list. A bin that samples it is not blocked by it.
* *Added hook*: an error inject (an inject or a force that puts a fault or an
illegal condition into the DUT, inside the `sep` module or outside it) or a
top-port drive (a drive of a port of the `sep` module by the bench, including a
stream input, a strap, an SMC, JTAG, DMI, DFT or debug port, a memory-macro
response port, and a port value that a plusarg sets) that
`docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` does not list and `hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` does not
have. The owning test adds an error inject to
the inject exceptions and a top-port drive to the HDL Top port table of
`docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` (<<sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks,SEP_VPLAN>>). It is not
PROPOSED, and a bin whose stimulus needs it is not blocked by it.
* *Existing bench hook*: a drive, a force, an inject or a time-0 plusarg that
`hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` has. It is existing DV infrastructure and is
approved, whatever net it drives: it is not PROPOSED and not an added hook, and
a bin whose stimulus needs it is not blocked by it
(<<sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks,SEP_VPLAN>>).
* *Preload*: the content of a memory macro outside `sep` that the approved
image load `tb_backdoor_mem` of `hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` puts in place at
t=0 (a Key Manager ROM image, its parameter block or a `rom_main` build, loaded
through `+km_rom_hex`). It is part of the approved t=0 image load, it is not a
hook and not PROPOSED, and a bin whose stimulus needs it is not blocked by it
(<<sep-vplan-km-proposed,SEP_VPLAN>>).
* *Blocked*: a bin whose stimulus needs a PROPOSED drive, that needs a value that
the specification does not state (<<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked>>), or whose every
owner that fills it is a parked test or a blocked check. The area sections write
`Blocked: <item or value>` at the bin, for example "Blocked: the cells need the
PROPOSED drive `x`".
* *Executable*: a bin that is not blocked, so that an executable check of an
executable owning test fills it. A coverpoint or group is *executable* when
every bin is executable, and *executable in part* when some bins are executable
and some are blocked.
* *Parked*: a coverpoint or group with no executable bin. An area section writes
"parked: <item or value>" for such a coverpoint, for example "parked: needs the
PROPOSED drive `x`".

The class cell of every coverpoint row starts with `executable`, `executable in
part. Blocked: <bins> <item>` or `parked: <item>`, and every group states its
class in a `*Class*:` line.

A blocked bin and a parked coverpoint or group are not in an executable total
and do not count toward closure. A bin that only the parked Phase 3 tests
(<<sep-vplan-phase3-parked>>) fill is blocked.

[[sep-fcov-graded-window]]
*Graded window.* Every bin of a Phase 2 group samples only inside the graded
window of one of its owning tests. The sampler has one gate input for this,
`graded_owner`: an integer code that names one Phase 3 entry, from one code table
that the sampler and the leaves share, with 0 for "no window open". It is a
variable of the Phase 2 sampler that the leaf writes through the testbench handle
of `u_sep_fcov`. `tb_top` builds `u_sep_fcov` only on VCS (Verilator compiles no
covergroup), so the leaf writes the gate only when that handle exists and skips
every gate write on a Verilator leaf; the checks of a leaf do not depend on the
gate. The gate is a monitor-only testbench variable that only the coverage sampler reads: it drives nothing and touches no DUT net, so it is an observation tap and blocks no bin. The rules are:

* The leaf writes the code of its own entry at the first step of its graded
stimulus. It writes 0 before each control leg, before each bring-up or clean-up
step, before each reset sequence that is not itself the graded stimulus, and at
the end of the leaf. A leaf never writes the code of another entry.
* A coverpoint samples only when `graded_owner` holds the code of a test that its
"Owning tests" cell names. Where the cell names an owner per bin, a bin samples
only for the code of its own owner. Traffic of other leaves in a merged
regression, of earlier tests in the same simulation, of the bring-up reset, of a
control leg and of a reset sequence between legs therefore fills no Phase 2 bin.
* Where the graded stimulus is a reset, a reset edge or a fault, the window is
open across it, and a reset-edge coverpoint samples the edge only inside the
window. The power-on reset falls before any leaf writes a code, so it fills no
bin.
* An extension leg that runs in the testlist entry of an open test writes the
code of its own Phase 3 entry for its leg only. The steps of the open test run
with the window closed.
* The gate is not a bin dimension, a checker verdict or a scoreboard count. A bin
still samples a DUT signal, and the gate only enables the sample.
* The Phase 1 groups do not read the gate and keep their own sampling.

An area rule below that says a coverpoint is "ungated" means that the coverpoint
does not use the `in_reset` gate of Phase 1. The graded window still applies.

[[sep-fcov-p2-crypto-entropy]]
==== Crypto and entropy groups

Each group below has owning tests and a sampled DUT signal. Every group is a VCS
covergroup in `cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` under `ifndef VERILATOR`.

===== Sampling rules

. Every coverpoint samples a DUT signal. No coverpoint samples a log line or a
scoreboard count.
. Every coverpoint is a VCS covergroup in the sampler under `ifndef VERILATOR`.
The graded VCS coverage run builds `--target default`. In `no_cpu` mode the
LSU master is spliced onto the CPU bus of that build.
. Bins follow the spec and the RDL. A value that only the leaf draws (message
bytes, key bytes, gap length) has no bin. A control window of a test has no
bin: the graded window of <<sep-fcov-graded-window>> is closed in it. A window
length that the spec does not state is not a bin.
. A Phase 1 group is not edited. A cell that extends a Phase 1 group is a
coverpoint of a Phase 2 group below (see <<sep-fcov-crypto-phase1-map>>).
. `@clk` is every `posedge clk_i`. `CSR wr` and `CSR rd` are the completed
single-beat OKAY accesses that the sampler decodes on the CPU-LSU bus. A leaf
that sends its CSR traffic through the `m_axi` master fills no `CSR` bin.
. An armed bin needs a first sample that proves the "before" state (a
non-reset value, a set status bit). The second sample proves the "after"
state. A simulator that is off, or a register that never held the value, fills
nothing.
. An illegal bin is a state that the spec forbids. A hit is a DUT failure that
the leaf must also report. An illegal bin never counts toward closure. Every
illegal bin has the legal bin of the same window as its control.
. A stored-value bin reads the register output of the DUT, never the bus write
alone. A refused write shows as an unchanged stored value.
. A `CSR` bin needs an OKAY response. A write that the DUT refuses with SLVERR
or DECERR fills no bin.
. The sampler gates every data bin on exactly one outstanding single-beat
transaction. A concurrent leaf loses some samples. A lost sample is a missed
hit and never a false hit.
. No bin takes its cell from a seed index or from a testlist entry that pins
the cell: regression seeds are random per leaf and a leaf does not know its run
index. An owner runs a cell set on every seed only when the set is small and
cheap (stimulus status "Walk"). A coverpoint whose cell the owner draws from the
seed has stimulus status "Seed-dependent" and is graded on a
merge of regressions, as for the groups of <<sep-fcov-rand>>, and one
regression never claims it. Every other coverpoint of the group fills in one
VCS `all --cov`. The cell of a seed-dependent coverpoint states the bins that fill on
every seed and the expected number of regressions to closure.
. The mapping of an alert kind to one receiver index of its block is not
stated: `crypto.adoc` lists the block ranges only. The groups of this area sample
the aggregate source 33 and the alert pair of a receiver (`alert_tx`). The block
range of the receiver pulses belongs to the groups of the mailbox, interrupt and
PIC area. No group samples which index of the pair pulses.
. A bin whose stimulus needs a PROPOSED drive, or a value that the
specification does not state, is blocked (<<sep-fcov-parked>>). A tap that the
table of sampled signals marks "Added tap" and a drive that it marks "Added
hook" block no bin. A blocked bin is not counted. The Class and stimulus cell
of a coverpoint starts with its class (<<sep-fcov-parked>>): `executable`,
`executable in part` with the blocked bins and the reason, or `parked: <item>`
with the reason that blocks every bin. The stimulus status follows.

===== Class and stimulus status

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Status |Meaning
|Defined |The owning test defines stimulus for the bin as written
|Walk |The bin fills when the owning test runs the cell set on every seed. The set is small and cheap. The seed picks data and order only
|Seed-dependent |The owning test draws the cell from the seed. The coverpoint is graded on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand>>), never in one regression. The cell names the bins that fill on every seed and the expected closure
|Partial |Some bins of the coverpoint have no stimulus. The cell names the open bins
|executable |Every sampled input is approved, an added tap or an added hook, and no bin is blocked, so every bin is executable
|executable in part |Some bins are blocked (<<sep-fcov-parked>>). The cell names those bins and the reason. The other bins are executable
|parked: <item> |Every bin is blocked (<<sep-fcov-parked>>). The cell names the reason. The coverpoint is parked and is not counted
|===

===== Sampled signals

Hierarchy aliases: `$CORE` = `u_dut.u_sep`, `$CRY` = `$CORE.u_sep_crypto`,
`$ESRC` = `$CRY.u_sep_trng.u_entropy_source_s3c_scan`,
`$DRBG` = `$CRY.u_sep_trng.u_drbg_s3c_scan`, `$HMAC` =
`$CRY.u_hmac_wrapper_s3c_scan.u_tt_hmac`, `$AES` =
`$CRY.u_aes_wrapper_s3c_scan.u_tt_aes`, `$KMAC` =
`$CRY.u_kmac_wrapper_s3c_scan.u_tt_kmac`, `$OTBN` =
`$CRY.u_sep_crypto_otbn_wrapper_s3c_scan.u_otbn`, `$CSRNG` = `$DRBG.u_csrng`,
`$EDN` = `$DRBG.u_edn`. Every path resolves in the tree. A tap is a read-only
reference. No tap forces a signal.

Disposition: "At sampler" = the sampler has the input (approved). "Wire" = the
`tb_top` probe exists and is approved; the sampler needs an input. "Added tap"
= the signal is internal, and a read-only hierarchical reference in `tb_top`
taps it; the owning test adds the tap to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe
list. "Added hook" = the bins need the external-stream driver, a top-port drive
of the `sep` inputs that the owning test adds to the port table of the HDL Top
section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`; the response monitor on the same port is a
read-only tap. "Hole" = no stimulus can reach it.

[cols="1,4,1",options="header"]
|===
|Tap |DUT signal |Disposition
|`csr_lsu` |CPU-LSU AXI request and response (address, data, strobe, response) and the block decode of the OTBN, AES, HMAC, KMAC, CSRNG, EDN, ESRC, ABR, POOL, CPU_CTRL and RESET_CTRL blocks. Register addresses are the `localparam` symbols of `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/svh/sep_reg.svh` |At sampler
|`intr_vec` |`sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o`, PIC source N on bit N-1 |Wire: bits 7:0, 8 and 14 reach the sampler; the other bits need sampler inputs
|`edn_ack` |`crypto_edn_ack_o[i]`, client 0 AES, 1 KMAC, 2 OTBN RND, 3 OTBN URND |At sampler
|`edn_req` |`crypto_edn_req_o[i]`, same client order |Wire
|`edn_fips` |`crypto_edn_fips_o[i]`, the `edn_fips` bit of each crypto client |Wire
|`km_stream` |`km_entropy_tvalid_o`, `km_entropy_tready_o`, `km_entropy_tdata_o`, stream 0 after the mux |At sampler (`tvalid`, `tready`); Wire (`tdata`)
|`crypto_stream` |`axis1_tvalid_o`, `axis1_tready_o`, `axis1_tdata_o`, stream 1 after the mux |At sampler (`tvalid`, `tready`); Wire (`tdata`)

|`pool_edn` |`pool_edn_req_o`, `pool_edn_ack_o`, `pool_edn_bus_o`, `pool_edn_fips_o`, the native EDN client of the pool |Wire

|`eng_idle` |Idle nets of HMAC (`$CRY.u_hmac_wrapper_s3c_scan.idle_o`), AES (`$CRY.u_aes_wrapper_s3c_scan.idle_o`), KMAC (`$CRY.u_kmac_wrapper_s3c_scan.idle_o`) and OTBN (`$OTBN.idle_o`) |Added tap: the parent leaves each port unconnected
|`alert_pulse` |`$CRY.crypto_alert_pulse[10:0]`: [0] HMAC, [2:1] OTBN, [4:3] AES, [6:5] KMAC, [8:7] CSRNG, [10:9] EDN |Added tap. No group of this area samples it
|`alert_tx` |`$CRY.crypto_alert_tx[10:0]`, the `alert_p` and `alert_n` pair of each receiver, same index order |Added tap
|`drbg_int` |`$CRY.drbg_int_axis_req[i]`, `$CRY.drbg_int_axis_rsp[i]`, the internal DRBG endpoint of stream i |Added tap
|`ext_stream` |`$CORE.ext_trng_axis_req_i[i]`, `$CORE.ext_trng_axis_rsp_o[i]` at the `sep` boundary |Added hook (top-port drive of `ext_trng_axis_req_i`); the response is a read-only tap

Spec documents of this section (paths relative to the repository root).
`SPEC` = `hw/sys/sep/doc/`. `OT` =
`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/<ip>/regs/gen/adoc/<ip>.adoc` for
`hmac`, `kmac`, `aes`, `otbn`, `csrng` and `edn`. `ESRC_RDL` =
`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc`. `ESRC_ARCH` =
`hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc`. `BLK` =
`hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/` (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `sep_reset_ctrl.adoc`,
`sep_entropy_pool.adoc`). `ABR_RDL` = `integration/rdl/sep/abr_reg.rdl`.

===== `sep_crypto_bus_access_cg`

*Description*: Engine concurrency on the crypto bus. The engines are separate
blocks with separate resets (`SPEC` `crypto.adoc`). The other coverpoints of
this group belong to the fabric response matrix.

*Owning tests*: `sep_crypto_edn_multisink_arbitration_test` (extension leg). *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Class and stimulus
|`cp_engine_pair` |Pair of engines busy on the same `clk_i`: hmac_aes, hmac_kmac, hmac_otbn, aes_kmac, aes_otbn, kmac_otbn. 6 bins. Busy means the engine idle net is low |`eng_idle` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_busy_count` |Number of busy engines on one `clk_i`: 2, 3, 4. 3 bins |`eng_idle` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_bystander_read` |`CSR rd` of an HMAC `DIGEST` word with response OKAY while a peer is busy. Peer class: aes, kmac, otbn. 3 bins |`csr_lsu` (`HMAC_DIGEST_0_`), `eng_idle` |executable. Stimulus: Partial: concurrent coroutines can break the one-outstanding gate on some reads, so a sample is missed and never invented
|===

===== `sep_crypto_reset_cg`

*Description*: TRNG reset window and reset evidence. `SPEC` `trng.adoc`,
`reset_controller.adoc`, `entropy_pool.adoc`; `BLK` `sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`,
`sep_entropy_pool.adoc`; `ESRC_RDL` (`FIPS_LOCK`); `OT` csrng and edn.

*Owning tests*: `sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test`,
`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`, `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`,
`sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`, `sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test`.
Tests of other areas contribute to the same group. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

[cols="1,4,2,1,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`cp_clear_ext_sel` |At the rise of `trng_reset_active_probe_o`: stream {crypto, pool} x select {external}. 2 cells. The Key Manager stream has no consumer without Key Manager firmware |`trng_rst`, `ext_sel` |`sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_xfer_phase` |Per stream {crypto, pool}: before_clear (an external handshake completes with the window low), in_clear (illegal: a handshake completes with the window high), resume (the first handshake after the window falls). 6 bins, 2 illegal |`ext_stream`, `trng_rst` |`sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_pool_in_clear` |`CSR rd` of the pool STATUS issued and answered inside the window with `fifo_level` field 0. 1 bin. The OKAY response of a pool read in the window belongs to `cp_trng_nonmember` |`csr_lsu` (`ENTROPY_POOL_STATUS`), `trng_rst` |`sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_pool_scrub` |Window start with a non-zero pool level, and the level is 0 at the window end. 1 bin. A window start with an empty pool is not a bin: no owner grades the scrub of an empty pool, and the TRNG reset of any leg would fill it |`pool_state`, `trng_rst` |`sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_client_in_clear` |Client {aes, kmac, otbn_rnd, otbn_urnd} x {request_pending_in_clear (`edn_req` high, no `edn_ack` in the window), ack_after_release}. 8 bins. Illegal: `edn_ack` high in the window |`edn_req`, `edn_ack`, `trng_rst` |`sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the leaf starts a pending request from each client before the reset
|`cp_reset_evidence` |Six armed bins. After a TRNG reset the first read returns the reset value, and the value before the reset differed: csrng_err_code (0 on the field bits), edn_err_code (0 on the field bits), csrng_regwen (1), edn_regwen (1), esrc_regs (every read-write ESRC field outside the `FIPS_LOCK` list and outside `CTRL` at its RDL reset value; the hardware-updating registers are not compared), cpu_lock_survives (`EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK` still 1) |`csr_lsu` (`CSRNG_ERR_CODE`, `CSRNG_REGWEN`, `EDN_ERR_CODE`, `EDN_REGWEN`, ESRC block, `SEP_CPU_CTRL_EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL`, `SEP_CPU_CTRL_EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK`), `trng_rst` |`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`, `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`, `sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`, `sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

===== `sep_crypto_intr_cg`

*Description*: Interrupt line gating of the 13 crypto sources and the 8 ESRC
sources. `SPEC` `interrupts.adoc` (PIC sources 18 to 30); `OT` hmac and kmac
(`INTR_STATE`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`); `ESRC_RDL` (`INTR_STATUS`).

*Owning tests*: `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` (extension leg),
`sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` (the OTBN line cells), `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`. Hardware fills come from the tests listed per
coverpoint. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_line_gate` |13 sources x {state, enable} = 52 cells, less the cell (0, 0) of hmac_err, the four CSRNG sources and the two EDN sources, the cells (0, 0) and (0, 1) of the two `fifo_empty` sources, and the cell (1, 1) of hmac_err, cs_entropy_req and cs_hw_inst_exc = 38 cells (see Bins not graded). The cell (1, 1) of hmac_done, kmac_done, cs_cmd_req_done, cs_fatal_err, edn_cmd_req_done and edn_fatal_err fills only from a real event of a hardware-fill owner whose graded check reads the vector bit; in the extension leg it is a control. Sources: hmac_done, hmac_fifo_empty, hmac_err, kmac_done, kmac_fifo_empty, kmac_err, cs_cmd_req_done, cs_entropy_req, cs_hw_inst_exc, cs_fatal_err, edn_cmd_req_done, edn_fatal_err, otbn_done (vector bits 17 to 29). No illegal bin: the specification does not state the latency from state AND enable to the vector bit, so a per-clock compare can hit on a compliant registered path. The owners grade the line against state AND enable with the settle and hold windows of their checks |`hmac_intr`, `kmac_intr`, `otbn_done`, `csrng_intr`, `edn_intr`, `intr_vec` |`sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` (the 10 sources of the extension leg: the cells (0, 1) and (1, 0) of all 10, the cell (0, 0) of `hmac_done`, `kmac_done` and `kmac_err`, and the cell (1, 1) of `kmac_err`; the cell (1, 1) of the other nine sources and the cell (0, 0) of the seven sources that the open interrupt test walks are controls of the leg, because the open interrupt tests grade them); `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` (`otbn_done`, all four cells); `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` (the cells (1, 1) and (1, 0) of the two `fifo_empty` sources, with the status made by the FIFO); hardware fills of the state-1 cells, from the real events, by `sep_hmac_illegal_cfg_start_test`, `sep_hmac_fifo_stream_rand_test`, `sep_hmac_swap_context_rand_test`, `sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test`, `sep_kmac_unsupported_cfg_inject_test`, `sep_kmac_stream_cfg_rand_test`, `sep_kmac_entropy_mode_rand_test`, `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`, `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_w1c_clear` |The 11 clearable sources x enable {0, 1} at a state fall by a write-one, without the enable-1 clear of the nine sources that the open interrupt tests clear with the enable set (hmac_done, hmac_err, kmac_done, the four CSRNG sources and the two EDN sources; a control of the extension leg). 13 bins. The two `fifo_empty` bits are read-only. Illegal: a state fall with no write, outside the gated reset of the block and the TRNG reset window |same as `x_line_gate`, `csr_lsu`, `ip_rst`, `trng_rst` |`sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` (10 sources), `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` (`otbn_done`) |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_enable_flip` |The 11 clearable sources x {line rises when the enable is set with state 1, line falls when the enable is cleared with state 1}. 22 bins. The two `fifo_empty` bits are excluded |same as `x_line_gate` |`sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` (10 sources), `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` (`otbn_done`) |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_hw_raise` |The state bit rises in a cycle with no `INTR_TEST` strobe for that source, for the 10 sources that an owner raises: hmac_done, hmac_fifo_empty, hmac_err, kmac_done, kmac_err, cs_cmd_req_done, cs_fatal_err, edn_cmd_req_done, edn_fatal_err, otbn_done. 10 bins |same as `x_line_gate`, plus the `INTR_TEST` strobes |hmac_done: `sep_hmac_swap_context_rand_test`; kmac_done: `sep_kmac_stream_cfg_rand_test`; err: `sep_hmac_illegal_cfg_start_test`; fifo_empty: `sep_hmac_fifo_stream_rand_test`; kmac_err: `sep_kmac_unsupported_cfg_inject_test`, `sep_kmac_entropy_mode_rand_test`; otbn_done: `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test`; cs_fatal_err, edn_fatal_err: `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`; cs_cmd_req_done, edn_cmd_req_done: `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_aes_quiet` |Window: AES busy and no other engine busy. Bin: the window ends with no rise on any of the 41 vector bits other than bits 36 and 37 (the entropy pool low and fill-stall sources, which have no enable and follow the pool). Illegal: a rise on any of those 41 bits inside the window. AES has no interrupt (`OT` aes `STATUS`; `SPEC` `aes.adoc`) |`eng_idle`, `intr_vec` |`sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_hmac_fifo_empty_qual` |At each FIFO-empty rise: {empty_after_full: `fifo_empty` state sets}, {empty_not_full: state stays 0}. 2 bins. Illegal: state sets with no earlier full |`hmac_intr`, `hmac_status` |`sep_hmac_fifo_stream_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Partial: the unmet cells of the rule (second round after Process or Stop) have no stimulus
|`x_esrc_status_enable` |8 sources (`INTR_STATUS` bits 0, 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28) x {status, enable} = 32 cells, less the 8 cells (status 1, enable 0) = 24 cells. The specification does not state whether a status bit latches while its enable bit is clear, so the cells (status 1, enable 0) have no bin (see Bins not graded). No line check: the OR onto PIC source 16 is not observable |`esrc_reg_in`, `esrc_reg_out` (`INTR_STATUS`, `INTR_ENABLE`) |`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`; hardware fills by `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`, `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_esrc_raise_path` |Per source other than `FIFO_UNDERFLOW`: hw (the bit rises with no test write). 7 hw bins. The hardware raise of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` is not a bin: it is a control of `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`, and `sep_esrc_e2e_fifo_closed_test` grades it (see Bins not graded). The raise of each source by an `INTR_TEST` write and its write-one clear are graded by `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` (open), so the test raise has no bin. The `FIFO_ERROR` hw bin fills from the ESRC FIFO integrity inject (an added hook) of `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test` (CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY) |`esrc_reg_in`, `esrc_reg_out` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`, `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

===== `sep_crypto_invalid_encoding_cg`

*Description*: Illegal HMAC, AES and KMAC configurations and their outcome.
`OT` hmac (`CFG`, `CMD`, `INTR_STATE`), aes (`CTRL_SHADOWED`), kmac (`CFG_SHADOWED`,
`CMD`); `SPEC` `hmac.adoc`.

*Owning tests*: `sep_hmac_illegal_cfg_start_test`,
`sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test`, `sep_aes_encoding_inject_rand_test`,
`sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test`, `sep_kmac_unsupported_cfg_inject_test`.
*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_hmac_start_class` |At `hash_start`: class {blocked_none_256, blocked_none_384, blocked_none_512 (hmac_en 1 with Key_None), blocked_1024_256 (hmac_en 1, Key_1024, SHA2_256), blocked_keylen_invalid (hmac_en 1, several `key_length` bits set, SHA2_256), blocked_digest_none (hmac_en 0, `digest_size` all-zero), blocked_digest_invalid (hmac_en 0, several `digest_size` bits set)} x outcome {blocked, started}. 14 cells, 7 legal (blocked). Blocked: `hmac_err` state sets, no `hmac_done`. Started: `hmac_done` rises. `hmac_idle` is not part of the outcome, because the register text does not state its value after a blocked start. Illegal: a blocked class that starts. The legal classes (hmac_en 0 with any `key_length`, Key_1024 on SHA2_384 and SHA2_512) are controls of the owner and are not bins: the open HMAC test grades them |`hmac_cfg`, `hmac_cmd`, `hmac_intr` |`sep_hmac_illegal_cfg_start_test`, `sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_hmac_key_src` |Blocked start (Key_None, hmac_en 1) with {software key, delivered key valid}. 2 bins |`hmac_cfg`, `hmac_cmd`, `hmac_intr`, `hmac_key_valid` |software: `sep_hmac_illegal_cfg_start_test`; delivered: `sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`x_aes_invalid_map` |Field {KEY_LEN, OPERATION, MODE, PRNG_RESEED_RATE} x written class {all-zero, multi-hot}. 8 cells. At the commit the stored value is the mapped value: AES_256, AES_ENC, AES_NONE, PER_1. Illegal: any other stored value |`csr_lsu` (`AES_CTRL_SHADOWED`), `aes_ctrl`, `aes_ctrl_strobe` |KEY_LEN, OPERATION, MODE: `sep_aes_encoding_inject_rand_test`; PRNG_RESEED_RATE: `sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`x_kmac_mode_strength` |At `CMD.start`: mode {SHAKE, cSHAKE} x strength {128, 224, 256, 384, 512} x `en_unsupported_modestrength` {0, 1}. Bins: the 12 unsupported cells (strength 224, 384, 512), each with its outcome {blocked: `kmac_err` sets, no absorb, `sha3_idle` 1, with the bit clear; started: `sha3_absorb` 1, with the bit set}. 12 bins. Illegal: an unsupported cell with the bit clear that starts. The 8 supported cells are controls of the owner and are not bins. SHA3 cells are Phase 1. SHA3 with strength 128 is ignored (the spec states no rule) |`kmac_cfg`, `kmac_cmd`, `kmac_status`, `kmac_intr`, `csr_lsu` (`KMAC_CMD`) |`sep_kmac_unsupported_cfg_inject_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_kmac_err_recover` |`CMD.err_processed` written after a blocked start: `kmac_err` cleared, `sha3_idle` 1, next hash reaches `kmac_done`. 1 bin |`kmac_cmd`, `kmac_status`, `kmac_intr` |`sep_kmac_unsupported_cfg_inject_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

===== `sep_aes_cfg_cg`

*Description*: AES key source, reseed rate and clear triggers. `OT` aes
(`CTRL_SHADOWED`, `TRIGGER`); `SPEC` `aes.adoc`. The cell (SIDELOAD 1, no valid
delivered key) is ignored: the spec states no rule.

*Owning tests*: `sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test`,
`sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test`. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_key_source` |Stored `SIDELOAD` {0, 1} x delivered `KEY_VALID` {0, 1}. Cells: (0, 1), (1, 1). The cell (0, 1) is the software key with a valid delivered key. The cell (0, 0) is not driven by an owner. It extends `sep_aes_mode_cg` `cp_sideload` |`aes_ctrl` (`sideload_q`), `aes_key_valid` |`sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined (needs the Key Manager firmware image)
|`cp_reseed_rate` |Stored `PRNG_RESEED_RATE` {PER_1, PER_64, PER_8K} at the first block of a run of 129 blocks or more. 3 bins |`aes_ctrl` (`prng_reseed_rate_q`), `edn_ack` (client 0) |`sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_reseed_trigger` |AES idle with EDN enabled: a window after a `TRIGGER` write with `PRNG_RESEED` = 0 and no rise of `crypto_edn_ack_o[0]`. 1 bin. The write of `TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED` followed by an acknowledge is a control of the owner; the open tests grade it and it is not a bin. Illegal: a rise in the window with no write |`aes_trigger`, `aes_status`, `edn_ack` |`sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_clear_trigger` |`KEY_IV_DATA_IN_CLEAR` written while idle, then a reload of the key, IV and data words and an `OUTPUT_VALID` rise. 1 bin. The `DATA_OUT_CLEAR` cell and the register values after either clear are not bins: the specification states pseudo-random data and no value (VPLAN Blocked legs of tested features) |`aes_trigger`, `aes_status`, `csr_lsu` (`AES_KEY_SHARE0_0_`, `AES_IV_0_`, `AES_DATA_IN_0_` writes) |`sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

===== `sep_aes_op_status_cg`

*Description*: AES `STATUS` bits in manual and automatic mode. `OT` aes
(`CTRL_SHADOWED`, `TRIGGER`, `STATUS`). {manual, `STALL`} and {automatic,
`OUTPUT_LOST`} are ignored: not meaningful.

*Owning tests*: `sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test`,
`sep_aes_encoding_inject_rand_test`. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_mode_status` |{automatic, `STALL` 1}, {automatic, `STALL` 0 after the output read}, {manual, `OUTPUT_LOST` 1}. 3 bins. The cell {manual, `OUTPUT_LOST` 0 with the output read} is the control of the owner and is not a bin |`aes_ctrl` (`manual_operation_q`), `aes_status` |`sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_lost_clear` |`OUTPUT_LOST` falls with a `ctrl_we` strobe. 1 bin. Illegal: a fall with no strobe |`aes_status`, `aes_ctrl_strobe` |`sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_nonidle_cause` |`IDLE` 0 with {core running, `STALL` 1}. 2 bins |`aes_status` |`sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_start_suppress` |`TRIGGER.START` written in {manual with a legal mode: `IDLE` falls; manual with MODE = AES_NONE: `OUTPUT_VALID` stays 0 over the window of the owner; automatic: no fall}. 3 bins |`aes_trigger`, `aes_status`, `aes_ctrl` |manual: `sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test`; AES_NONE: `sep_aes_encoding_inject_rand_test`; automatic: `sep_aes_stream_status_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk (the automatic-mode START write is a leg of the owner)
|===

===== `sep_aes_fault_cg`

*Description*: AES shadow-register update error and reset
restore. `OT` aes (`CTRL_SHADOWED`, `CTRL_AUX_SHADOWED`, `CTRL_AUX_REGWEN`,
`STATUS`, `ALERT_TEST`); `SPEC` `aes.adoc`.

*Owning tests*: `sep_crypto_shadow_update_err_rand_test`. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Class and stimulus
|`x_shadow_pair_aes` |`CTRL_SHADOWED` x pair {matched: no pulse on vector bit 32 and the value commits, mismatched: update error 1 and a pulse on vector bit 32}. 2 cells. Illegal: a mismatched pair with update error 0. The `CTRL_AUX_SHADOWED` pairs are logged by the owner and are not bins (the text states the status bit for the Control Register only), and the update error 0 of a matched pair is graded by the open AES test |`csr_lsu` (`AES_CTRL_SHADOWED`), `aes_ctrl_strobe`, `aes_fault`, `intr_vec` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_update_err_recovery` |Update error 1, then a `CTRL_SHADOWED` rewrite clears it, then an AES block reaches `OUTPUT_VALID`. 1 bin |`aes_ctrl_strobe`, `aes_fault`, `aes_status` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_reset_restore` |After `aes_gated_rst_n` rises: {update error 0, armed by update error 1 before the reset; a new AUX pair lands, armed by `CTRL_AUX_REGWEN` 0 before the reset}. 2 bins. The AUX register value after the reset is logged by the owner and graded by another test, so it is not a bin |`ip_rst`, `aes_fault`, `aes_aux_regwen` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

===== `sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg`

*Description*: Register locks of AES, ESRC, CSRNG and EDN. `OT` aes
(`CTRL_AUX_REGWEN`), csrng (`REGWEN`), edn (`REGWEN`); `ESRC_RDL` (`FIPS_LOCK`,
`CTRL`, `HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE`). The ESRC locked-field set is the 39 rows of
the ESRC register map whose description states "FIPS_LOCK.LOCK prevents
software changes". The set includes `DEBUG_CTRL`, `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` and both `RAW_ENABLE` fields.
This group bins three fields of that set: the two `RAW_ENABLE` fields (`x_fips_field`)
and `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` (`cp_module_enable_locked`). The other 36 fields
are graded by an open test and have no bin here.

*Owning tests*: `sep_crypto_shadow_update_err_rand_test`,
`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test`,
`sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`cp_aux_regwen` |{post-lock AUX write refused (stored value unchanged), write of 1 to `CTRL_AUX_REGWEN` after 0 leaves 0}. 2 bins. The pre-lock AUX write is the control of the owner and is not a bin |`aes_aux_regwen`, `csr_lsu` (`AES_CTRL_AUX_SHADOWED`, `AES_CTRL_AUX_REGWEN`) |`sep_crypto_shadow_update_err_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`x_fips_field` |The two `RAW_ENABLE` fields (`NOISE_OBS_CTRL`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`) under `FIPS_LOCK` x {locked at 1: the observe `LEVEL` rises again after the drain at the lock, locked at 0: a write of 1 leaves the observe `LEVEL` at 0}. 4 cells. The stored value of the fields before and after the lock is graded by an open test and is a control of the owner, so it is not a bin |`esrc_reg_out` (field value and `FIPS_LOCK`), `esrc_biw_obs`, `esrc_noise_obs` |`sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_module_enable_locked` |After the lock a `MODULE_ENABLE` write of 0 is ignored and `main_sm_idle` stays 0. 1 bin. The unlocked write is the control trial of the owner and has no bin |`esrc_reg_out` (`CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE`), `esrc_sm` |`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`x_csrng_regwen` |Register {CTRL, FIPS_FORCE} x post-lock write refused. 2 bins. A write of 1 to `REGWEN` after 0 leaves 0 (1 bin). The pre-lock write is the control of the owner (`CTRL-REGWEN-PRE`) and is not a bin |`csrng_regwen`, `csr_lsu` (CSRNG block) |`sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`x_edn_regwen` |`CTRL` post-lock write refused. 1 bin. A write of 1 to `REGWEN` after 0 leaves 0 (1 bin). The pre-lock write is the control of the owner and is not a bin |`edn_regwen`, `csr_lsu` (EDN block) |`sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|===

===== `sep_crypto_alert_cg`

*Description*: CSRNG and EDN recoverable alert status, and the Crypto alert source for malformed
encodings (`SPEC` `crypto.adoc`, OpenTitan Alert Termination). The Crypto
alert for each `ALERT_TEST` bit belongs to the mailbox, interrupt and PIC area.
`OT` csrng (`RECOV_ALERT_STS`), edn (`RECOV_ALERT_STS`). The KMAC shadow
pairs are logged by the owner and have no bin (see Bins not graded). The
fabric fault tests contribute to the same group.

*Owning tests*: `sep_crypto_shadow_update_err_rand_test`,
`sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_alert_source` |Block {hmac, otbn, aes, kmac, csrng, edn} x a malformed encoding on the alert pair of one receiver of the block. 6 cells. Outcome: vector bit 32 rises within the bound that the owner derives from the `ALERT_TEST` delay of the block. The receiver index is not a bin. The `ALERT_TEST` cells belong to the mailbox, interrupt and PIC area. The malformed encoding comes from the alert sender inject (an added hook); CHK-ALERT-MALFORMED fills the cells |`intr_vec`, `alert_tx` |`sep_crypto_shadow_update_err_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk (every block on every seed)
|`cp_csrng_recov_seq` |`RECOV_ALERT_STS.CMD_STAGE_INVALID_CMD_SEQ_ALERT` {set by a repeated instantiate, cleared by a write of zero}. 2 bins. The clear convention is a write of zero |`csrng_recov`, `csrng_swcmd`, `csr_lsu` (CSRNG block) |`sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

|`x_len_corner` |Legal (mode, strength) combination {SHA3 224, 256, 384, 512; SHAKE 128, 256} x message length {rate - 1, rate, rate + 1}. 18 cells. The length is the count of message bytes written between `start` and `process`. The rate is the Keccak rate of the strength |`kmac_cfg`, `csr_lsu` (message window strobe) |executable. Stimulus: Walk

Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_wait_timer` |`wait_timer` {0, 1 to 8} x EDN response {withheld}: {0 never expires, 1 to 8 expires with `kmac_err`}. `prescaler` {0, 2 to 15}. 4 bins |`csr_lsu` (`KMAC_ENTROPY_PERIOD`), `kmac_timer`, `kmac_intr` |executable. Stimulus: Walk (prescaler 0 and a drawn prescaler from 2 to 15 on every seed; the owner grades the count with the prescaler above the count with prescaler 0)
|`cp_sw_seed_count` |`ENTROPY_SEED` writes before `CMD.start` {0: no absorb, 5: no absorb, 6: absorb}. 3 bins |`kmac_seed_strobe`, `kmac_status` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_hash_cnt` |`hash_cnt` {rises by one per hash, falls to 0 at `CMD.hash_cnt_clr`, falls to 0 at `CMD.entropy_req`}. 3 bins |`kmac_hash_cnt`, `kmac_cmd` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_entropy_req_ack` |`CMD.entropy_req` written while idle in EDN mode, then a rise of `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]`. 1 bin |`kmac_cmd`, `edn_ack` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_auto_reseed` |{threshold T from 2 to 4 with N keyed hashes of at least T + 2: `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` rises during the run or its settle window and `hash_cnt` reads below N after the last hash; threshold 0 with N keyed hashes: no rise and `hash_cnt` reads N}. 2 bins. Illegal: a rise with threshold 0 |`kmac_cfg`, `kmac_hash_cnt`, `edn_ack`, `csr_lsu` (`KMAC_ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED`) |executable. Stimulus: Walk (both thresholds on every seed)

===== `sep_abr_completion_cg`

*Description*: Adams Bridge operation protocol, combined commands and verify
outcome. `ABR_RDL` (`MLDSA_CTRL`, `MLDSA_STATUS`, `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES`,
`MLKEM_CTRL`, `MLKEM_STATUS`); `SPEC` `adams_bridge.adoc`. The ABR CSRs are
defined by the vendored package and are not in the generated address map.

*Owning tests*: `sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test`. *Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable. *Stimulus status*: as in the
coverpoint rows.

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_op_protocol` |Combined operation {DSA keygen+sign, KEM keygen+decaps} x state {held (`VALID` 1, `READY` 0), `READY` after zeroize, `READY` after reset} = 6 cells, and single-step operation {DSA keygen, signing, verifying; KEM keygen, encaps, decaps} x `READY` 0 at the end of the cell = 6 cells. 12 cells. The valid status and the re-arm of the single-step operations are graded by the open ABR tests and are controls of the owner, so they are not bins |`csr_lsu` (`ABR_MLDSA_CTRL`, `ABR_MLDSA_STATUS`, `ABR_MLKEM_CTRL`, `ABR_MLKEM_STATUS`), `ip_rst` |`sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: the two held cells fill on every seed. The owner draws one re-arm per combined operation per leaf, so each re-arm cell of an operation fills in a leaf with a probability of 1/2; it draws one single-step cell per leaf, so the six single-step cells close in about 6 * ln(6) / reseed regressions
|`cp_combined_cmd` |`CTRL` = 100 for KEYGEN+SIGN (DSA) and for KEYGEN+DECAPS (KEM). 2 bins |`csr_lsu` (`ABR_MLDSA_CTRL`, `ABR_MLKEM_CTRL`) |`sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_verify_outcome` |VERIFYING of a published ACVP external-mu sigVer reject case completes with `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` different from c~ of the signature as written (reject), one bin for each ACVP reason class of the reject cases: `modified message`, `modified signature - commitment`, `modified signature - hint`, `modified signature - z`. 4 bins. The accept of the ACVP accept case is the control of the owner and is graded by the open ABR test, so it is not a bin. The sampler holds the c~ words from the signature writes and takes the reason class from the vector-table row that the owner writes (a stimulus label). The row inside a reason class has no bin |`csr_lsu` (`ABR_MLDSA_VERIFY_RES_0_`, signature window) |`sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: the owner draws one reason class per leaf, so the four bins close in about 4 * ln(4) / reseed regressions
|===

===== `sep_esrc_noise_gen_cg`

*Description*: ESRC stored configuration and detune. `ESRC_RDL` (`CTRL`,
`RING_OSC_ENABLE`, `RING_OSC_TUNE`, `RING_OSC_CTRL`,
`GENERATOR_<n>_SAMPLE_CLK_CONFIG`); `ESRC_ARCH`. The bench forces the noise
after the oscillator, so the oscillator, its length, its source and the sample
clock have no observable effect. The stored values are graded by an open test and
have no bin here.

*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

|`cp_detune_mode` |Effective detune of a lane {follows `DETUNE` bit with `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` 0 for mask zero, follows it for mask non-zero, ignores it with `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` 1 and mask non-zero}. 3 bins |`esrc_detune`, `esrc_reg_out` |`sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_detune_lane` |Lane 0 to 11: the effective detune toggles at a rising failure edge with `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` 1. 12 bins |`esrc_detune`, `esrc_fail` |`sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: the owner draws one lane k per leaf, so the 12 bins close in about 12 * ln(12) / reseed regressions
|`cp_lane_pattern` |Pattern seen at `dcor.noise_i` of a lane over one window: {constant 0 (the whitener leg), biased high, biased low}. 3 bins. The class comes from the signal, not from the leaf label |`esrc_noise`, `esrc_reg_out` (`HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE`) |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk

===== `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg`

*Description*: ESRC startup gate, restart edge and health-tester sides.
`ESRC_RDL` (`COMPONENT_ID`, `CTRL`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`,
`HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE`, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `*_TOTAL_FAILS`,

`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`,
`sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`.
*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`cp_component_id` |`COMPONENT_ID` field bits unchanged after a write of its complement (the Reserved bits 23:16 have no stated read value and are not sampled). 1 bin. The value 0x0100_0001 is graded by an open test and has no bin |`csr_lsu` (`ENTROPY_SOURCE_COMPONENT_ID`) |`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`x_enable_boot` |`MODULE_ENABLE` x `BOOT_PHASE_DONE`: (1, 0), (1, 1), and (0) with `main_sm_idle` 1. 3 bins |`esrc_reg_out` (`CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE`), `esrc_sm` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_restart_edge` |`MODULE_ENABLE` rises: the five total-failure counters read 0 after the edge and `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` rises later. 2 bins. Armed on a non-zero `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` |`esrc_reg_out`, `esrc_reg_in`, `esrc_sm` |`sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_startup_fail_hold` |A failing startup window: `ht_fail_pulse` with `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` 0 and `MODULE_ENABLE` 1. 1 bin. Illegal: a push into the main FIFO, the BIW observe FIFO or the whitener while `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` is 0 |`esrc_sm`, `esrc_fail`, `esrc_biw_obs`, `esrc_fifo`, `esrc_compress` |`sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_toggle_moment` |`MODULE_ENABLE` falls with {`BOOT_PHASE_DONE` 0, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` 1, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` non-zero while the failing pattern runs}. 3 bins. `main_sm_idle` is 1 after each. The third bin uses the latched counter, which CHK-BOOT-TOGGLE reads before the fall and grades at 0 after the rise; `ht_fail_pulse` is one clock per failing window and is not a bin |`esrc_reg_out`, `esrc_reg_in` (`REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS.FAIL_COUNT.next`), `esrc_sm` |`sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_test_enable_set` |Stored `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` {6, 5}. 2 bins. The reset value 7 is present in every leg and is not a bin. Bit 0 repetition, bit 1 adaptive proportion, bit 2 Markov. The value with the Markov bit cleared and the values with two bits cleared are not driven |`esrc_reg_out` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_window_class` |Stored `HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE.SIZE` {below 1024, 1024, 2048 (reset), above 2048}. 4 bins |`esrc_reg_out` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: below 1024 (the persistent-alert windows), 1024 (the limit trials) and 2048 fill on every seed; above 2048 fills when a pattern trial draws the window 4096 (two of three windows per leaf)
|`x_tester_side` |Tester {repetition, adaptive-proportion high, adaptive-proportion low, Markov high, Markov low} x {trip: the lane status bit latches, pass: the window completes and no bit latches}. 8 cells: the five trip cells and the pass cells of the repetition, Markov high and Markov low testers. The repetition and Markov low trips come from limits beyond the exact run and alternation counts of a `square` stream on all lanes, and the Markov high trip from a limit below the exact pair alternation count of an `alternate` stream on all lanes. The pass cells of the two adaptive-proportion testers are not bins (they share status bit 3) |`esrc_reg_in` (`GENERATOR_<n>_HEALTH_STATUS`, `*_TOTAL_FAILS`), `esrc_reg_out` (limits, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE`) |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_test_level` |Trip at {the 8-bit per-generator instance (a lane status bit), the 32-bit top instance (`HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 3 or bit 5)}. 2 bins. Bit 0 of the top instance is a control of the owner (the open alert test grades the repetition failure) and is not a bin |`esrc_reg_in` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_top_failure_set` |Constant-zero 32-bit stream after a clean startup: `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bits 3 and 5 set (1 bin); `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` non-zero (1 bin); `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` set (1 bin); constant all-ones 32-bit stream: status bit 3 set with `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` non-zero (1 bin). 4 bins. Status bit 4 and `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` are not bins: a constant stream has no alternations and no window of the owner drives the 32-bit Markov high tester. Status bit 0 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` are controls of the owner and are not bins |`esrc_reg_in` (`HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `*_TOTAL_FAILS`, `INTR_STATUS`), `esrc_noise` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_disable_clears` |`HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` bit cleared while `REPETITION_COUNT` is non-zero: the count reads 0 at the next window. 1 bin. Armed |`esrc_reg_out`, `esrc_reg_in` |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_prestage_test` |`FIFO_RDATA` words drained with `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` 1 and every lane stuck at 0 after `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 sets. 1 bin. Bit 0 is the qualifier and a control; the owner grades the 2:1 count of stream words to whitener input words and logs the words |`esrc_reg_in`, `esrc_reg_out`, `csr_lsu` (`FIFO_RDATA` reads) |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|===

===== `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg`

*Description*: ESRC failure latches, total-failure counters, persistent
alert, autotune and FIFO flags. `ESRC_RDL` (`GENERATOR_<n>_HEALTH_STATUS`,
`*_TOTAL_FAILS`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`,
`MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `INTR_STATUS`, `FIFO_STATUS`); `ESRC_ARCH`.

*Owning tests*: `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`,
`sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`,
`sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`, `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`,
`sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test` (the subset clear of `cp_fifo_flags`).
*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

[cols="1,4,2,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owner |Class and stimulus
|`x_gen_latch` |Lane 0 to 11 x status bit 3 latched = 12 cells, and lane x {status bit 3 cleared by a write of 1} = 12 bins (the clean-up writes of the limit trials clear bits 0, 4 and 5 and are not sampled). Bits 0, 4 and 5 latched on every lane by one limit trial each = 3 bins. Bits 1, 2, 6, 7 have no assignment |`esrc_reg_in` (`GENERATOR_<n>_HEALTH_STATUS.STATUS.next`) |`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: the three limit-trial bins fill on every seed; the 24 lane bins fill for the two lanes that the pattern trials draw, so they close in about 12 * ln(12) / (2 * reseed) regressions
|`cp_total_fails` |`REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` cleared by a `MODULE_ENABLE` rise (armed). 1 bin. The clear is sampled for a counter that reads non-zero before the rise. The increment is the control of the owner and is graded by the open alert test, so it is not a bin. The APT and Markov totals of the constant-zero stream are bins of `cp_top_failure_set` |`esrc_reg_in` (`REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS.FAIL_COUNT.next`) |`sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_alert_threshold` |Stored `ALERT_THRESHOLD` {0, 4 (reset, read first), 5, 7}. 4 bins. The threshold 3 is the control of the owner and is graded by the open alert test, so it is not a bin |`esrc_reg_out` |`sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: 0 and 4 fill on every seed (threshold 0 runs on every seed, and CHK-PF-RESET reads the reset value 4); 5 and 7 each fill when the seed draws that threshold (one of three per leaf). The write of 7 in the reset leg is a control of the owner and fills no bin
|`cp_any_fail` |`ANY_FAIL_COUNT` {below the threshold, at or above the threshold, above 8 with threshold 0}, and {falls to 0 within two whole windows of clean noise after a burst}. 4 bins |`esrc_reg_in` (`ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS.ANY_FAIL_COUNT.next`) |`sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_alert_hang` |{entry: `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` set and `LEVEL` stops rising, no entry below the threshold, no entry with a clean window between bursts, no entry with threshold 0, a write of 1 clears both bits and the output stays halted, exit by `MODULE_ENABLE` 0 then 1, exit by TRNG reset}. 7 bins |`esrc_reg_in`, `esrc_sm`, `esrc_fail`, `esrc_fifo`, `esrc_compress` |`sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk (the exit by TRNG reset is a leg of the owner)
|`cp_autotune` |{`AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` 0 and a lane fails: `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` 0, detune unchanged; `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` 1 and a lane fails: `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` 1 and the lane detune toggles}, and burst number {1, 2, 3} (the probe bit changes at least once; the toggle count is not graded). 5 bins. Other lanes unchanged (illegal if they toggle) |`esrc_detune`, `esrc_fail`, `esrc_reg_in`, `esrc_reg_out` |`sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_fifo_flags` |`FIFO_OVERFLOW` set by a push while full; a write of 1 to a proper subset of the set `INTR_STATUS` bits that holds both `FIFO_OVERFLOW` and `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` clears only that subset and leaves the other set bits. 2 bins. The set of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` by an empty pop is graded by an open test and has no bin here |`esrc_fifo`, `esrc_reg_in`, `csr_lsu` (`ENTROPY_SOURCE_INTR_STATUS`) |`sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test` (overflow set), `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test` (subset clear that holds bits 8 and 12) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_fifo_integrity` |Main FIFO integrity: {a one-bit parity error in a stored word then a pop of that word: `FIFO_ERROR` rises; a one-bit error in one pointer copy: `FIFO_ERROR` rises; a write of 1 clears `FIFO_ERROR`}. 3 bins. Illegal: `FIFO_ERROR` rises with no inject and no `INTR_TEST` write, outside the interval from a pointer-copy inject to the next TRNG reset (a released pointer force can leave the mismatch in place). The errors come from the ESRC FIFO integrity inject (an added hook); CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY, CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR and CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C (parity trial) fill the three bins |`esrc_fifo` (`fifo_error`), `esrc_reg_in` (`INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR.next`), `csr_lsu` (`ENTROPY_SOURCE_INTR_STATUS`) |`sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|===

Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_unselected_drop` |Stream {crypto, pool} with select internal: an external `tvalid` completes with `tready` 1 and the post-mux `tvalid` stays 0. 2 bins |`ext_stream`, `crypto_stream`, `pool_stream` |`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` (CHK-EXT-DROP) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_ext_stall` |Stream {crypto, pool} with select external: `tready` falls while `tvalid` is 1, then rises again. 2 bins |`ext_stream`, `crypto_stream`, `pool_stream` |`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` (crypto: CHK-SEL-READY, the fall and the rise after an AES PRNG reseed; pool: CHK-EXT-BP) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_hold_internal` |Stream {crypto, pool} with select external: the internal endpoint holds `tvalid` 1 with `tready` 0 and `tdata` stable, then delivers the held word after the select returns. 2 bins |`drbg_int` |`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_pool_pop` |Pool `DATA` read OKAY at level {2 to 31, 32}, and a feed at level 32. 3 bins. Level 1 has no forced step |`pool_state`, `pool_edn`, `csr_lsu` (`ENTROPY_POOL_DATA`) |`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` (CHK-EXT-BP, CHK-POOL-DATA) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_ext_irq` |`ext_trng_irq_i` {rises, falls} with vector bit 16 following. 2 bins |`ext_irq`, `intr_vec` |`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` (CHK-EXT-IRQ) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_ext_tuser` |A word of an external beat that the consuming adapter accepts carries `edn_fips` 1 (the mux supplies `tuser` 1): stream {crypto, pool}. 2 bins. Illegal: `edn_fips` 0 |`ext_stream`, `edn_ack`, `edn_fips`, `pool_edn` |`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` (CHK-EXT-XFER) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_km_ext_stream` |Key Manager stream with `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` bit 0 set: {each beat completed at external port 0 appears at `km_entropy_*` with the same data and in order, the external `tready` equals `km_entropy_tready_o`}. 2 bins. Illegal: a clock with `tvalid` high at `axis1_*` or `axis2_*` whose word differs from the word of the internal DRBG endpoint of the same stream |`ext_stream`, `km_stream`, `crypto_stream`, `pool_stream`, `ext_sel`, `drbg_int` |`sep_drbg_sink_mix_rand_test` (CHK-MIX-EXT-KM) |executable. Stimulus: Defined
|`cp_active_sinks` |Sinks served in one run {km, aes, kmac, otbn_rnd, otbn_urnd, pool}: 6 bins |`km_stream`, `edn_ack`, `pool_edn` or `pool_stream` |`sep_drbg_sink_mix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: km fills on every seed; each other sink is in the drawn set of a leaf with a probability of 0.8 (needs the Key Manager firmware image)
|===

|Owning test |Phase 1 group |Phase 2 coverpoint
|`sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test` |`sep_aes_mode_cg` `cp_sideload` |`sep_aes_cfg_cg` `x_key_source` (cell: software key with a valid delivered key)
|`sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` |`sep_otbn_execute_cg` |`sep_otbn_op_cg` (all coverpoints)
|`sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test` |`sep_abr_keygen_cg`, `sep_abr_sign_cg`, `sep_abr_mlkem_cg` |`sep_abr_completion_cg` (all coverpoints)
|`sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test` |`sep_esrc_edn_flow_cg` `cp_pool_pop` |`sep_entropy_delivery_cg` `cp_pool_pop`
|`sep_drbg_sink_mix_rand_test` |`sep_esrc_edn_flow_cg` `cp_km`, `cp_edn_aes`, `cp_edn_kmac`, `cp_edn_otbn_rnd`, `cp_edn_otbn_urnd`, `cp_pool_pop` (one coverpoint per sink) |`sep_entropy_delivery_cg` `cp_active_sinks`
