===== Bins not graded

Each group bins only the cells that an owning test hits. The cells below have
no bin, for the reason given.

[cols="1,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Cells without a bin |Reason
|`sep_crypto_invalid_encoding_cg` |A `hash_continue` with a blocked configuration |No owner drives `hash_continue` with a blocked configuration
|`sep_crypto_intr_cg` |ESRC line check onto PIC source 16; ABR sources; HMAC and KMAC `fifo_empty` unmet conditions (second and third condition); hardware raise of kmac_fifo_empty, cs_entropy_req and cs_hw_inst_exc; enable flip and write-one clear of the two `fifo_empty` bits; the `INTR_TEST` raise of each ESRC source alone (graded by `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test`); the 8 ESRC cells (status 1, enable 0) of `x_esrc_status_enable`; the cell (0, 0) of hmac_err, the four CSRNG sources and the two EDN sources in `x_line_gate`; the cells (0, 0) and (0, 1) of the two `fifo_empty` sources in `x_line_gate`; the cell (1, 1) of hmac_err, cs_entropy_req and cs_hw_inst_exc in `x_line_gate`; the hardware raise of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` in `cp_esrc_raise_path` |The line is not observable. Whether an ESRC status bit latches while its enable bit is clear is a Blocked specification question (VPLAN Phase 3 Blocked features); the owner logs those status reads and every ESRC owner grades a status bit with its enable set. No test drives ABR errors. No stimulus for the unmet conditions or for the three hardware raises (an ignored command is not stated as a hardware exception). Retention of a forced read-only bit is blocked. The open interrupt test grades the line low with the state and the enable at 0 for the seven sources that it walks, so the extension leg runs that cell as a control. No owner drives a `fifo_empty` status of 0: the owner of the two `fifo_empty` sources makes the status 1 from the FIFO and grades the enable-1 and enable-0 cells of state 1 only. No hardware-fill owner raises hmac_err with its enable set, and no owner raises cs_entropy_req or cs_hw_inst_exc from hardware; the open interrupt tests grade the cell (1, 1) of these three sources with `INTR_TEST`. The hardware raise of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` is a control of `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`, and `sep_esrc_e2e_fifo_closed_test` grades it
|`sep_aes_cfg_cg` |GCM phases, `NUM_VALID_BYTES`, key length cells, mode x operation cells; the `DATA_OUT_CLEAR` cell of `cp_clear_trigger` and the `DATA_OUT` and `IV` values after a clear trigger |No Phase 3 test drives GCM. The open mode test fills the cipher cells. The register text states a clear with pseudo-random data and no value or status effect, so the owner logs the values and grades the encryption after the key clear only
|`sep_aes_op_status_cg` |`IDLE` causes "register clearing" and "PRNG reseeding"; register group completeness; busy writes; `OUTPUT_VALID` falling after a partial read of `DATA_OUT` |The owner checks the encrypt and stall causes only. The register text states no fall condition.
|`sep_aes_fault_cg` |`CTRL_GCM_SHADOWED`; secret register scrub on reset; `ALERT_FATAL_FAULT` stays 0 |No Phase 3 test drives GCM. The scrub values are not stated. The fatal input is tied off and has no control window
|`sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg` |CSRNG lock cells of `CMD_REQ`, `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE`, `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE_REGWEN`, `HW_EXC_STS`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`; 36 of the 39 locked ESRC fields; the `FIPS_LOCK` write-one, write-zero and reset behaviour; `LOCK` set with `HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE` of at least 1024 and the unlocked `MODULE_ENABLE` write (the lock set is graded by `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, no check grades the window size at the lock, and the unlocked write is a control of `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`) |The owner drives two CSRNG registers and one EDN register. An open test grades the locked fields and the lock behaviour.
|`sep_crypto_alert_cg` |The update error of the KMAC shadow pairs (`CFG_SHADOWED`, `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED`), which the owner logs and does not grade, because the text does not state its cause; the kind of each `ALERT_TEST` bit and the receiver index that it reaches; the receiver range of an alert; CSRNG and EDN field alerts; `CS_BUS_CMP_ALERT`; the other CSRNG and EDN status codes; EDN `CSRNG_ACK_ERR`, `HW_EXC_STS` bit 0, `CS_HW_INST_EXC` and `CMD_RDY` after an ignored instantiate |The map from an `ALERT_TEST` bit to a receiver index is not stated. No Phase 3 test drives the field alerts or the other cells. The multi-bit boolean codes are stated in the RDL (True = 6, False = 9), so the field alerts need a stimulus and not a value. The CSRNG text states that bit 14 sets and that the command is ignored, and no acknowledge or hardware-exception status.
|`sep_kmac_cfg_cmd_cg` |`msg_mask` x `entropy_fast_process` (they act only in KMAC mode; the keyed digest is graded by an open test); PREFIX, key length, key source, application interface, pass-through quirk, command x stage cells of `err_processed`, `hash_cnt_clr`, `entropy_req` |Open Phase 1 tests fill the PREFIX and key cells. No check for the rest
|`sep_kmac_entropy_cg` |Wait timer "large"; threshold {1, maximum}; the number of reseeds per run and the hash at which the first reseed occurs; EDN-timeout escape; the fourth mode encoding |The owner sets the threshold to 0, to 2 to 4 or to a value above the hash count, and a timer of 1 to 8. The specification states a reseed after each threshold of messages and no count at the boundary
|`sep_otbn_err_lock_cg` |The LOCKED entry by `software_errs_fatal`, the LOCKED hold and the unlock exit; the seven other software errors; the eight fatal bits except `fatal_software`; FIPS and repetition checks; system-reset exit; `lifecycle_escalation` stays 0 |The text does not state that the key-share error escalates to LOCKED, so no stimulus reaches LOCKED. The escalation input has no control window
|`sep_esrc_noise_gen_cg` |Oscillator length, source and synchronizer cells; `SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT`; external clock presence; `SAMPLE_CLK_DIVIDE` 6 to 31; stored values of the ring-oscillator, sample-clock, decorrelator and generator divide fields; the generator divide reset order |The bench forces the noise after the oscillator. An open test grades the stored values and the reset values.
|`sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` |Stream words while the FIFO is full; coupled divider axis; `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` bins; BIW structure; degenerate BIW inputs; all-clear mask; parity format; `INPUT_COUNT` at the block boundary; a churn push after fewer than 32 pushes (`cp_churn_age`) |The spec states no BIW coefficient. The register text gives the count of words accumulated and no value at the block boundary. The integrity checks are bins of `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` `cp_fifo_integrity`. The word of a churn push after fewer than 32 pushes depends on stale entries, which the specification does not define
|`sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` |Repetition limit bins 0, 1, 255; window 65535; APT and Markov limit bands; component-version bins beyond `COMPONENT_ID`; window synchronization; the pass cells of the two adaptive-proportion testers; the `ENABLE` value with the Markov bit cleared; the Markov high and adaptive-proportion high trips of the 32-bit instance |The limits are leaf draws. The limit values have no spec bin beyond the trip and pass sides. The 32-bit statistic of a lane pattern other than the constant is not stated. The adaptive-proportion pass cells share status bit 3 with the trip of the other adaptive-proportion tester, so they cannot be seen
|`sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` |`ALERT_FAIL_COUNTS`; `ALERT_CNTR_CLR_OK`; detune under lock; threshold 1 and maximum; `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` set by an empty pop; the APT and Markov total-failure counters under a generator-level trip; the contribution of a single generator failure to `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`; the number of probe toggles per failing window |No stimulus. An open test grades the underflow. The text does not say which instance (a generator or the 32-bit test) counts into a total-failure counter, that a failure of one generator counts into `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`, or that it gives one rising edge per failing window.
|`sep_drbg_err_inject_cg` |Type bits 28 to 30; the undefined selector class; `HW_EXC_STS` bits 1 to 15; the source-to-type map; `HW_EXC_STS` bit 0; the receiver range of a fatal alert |The owner never drives the type bits. The receiver range of an alert belongs to the mailbox, interrupt and PIC area. The number of hardware instances is not stated. The text does not state that an ignored command is a hardware exception.
|`sep_csrng_cmd_cg` |Commands other than instantiate; `clen`, `flag0`, `glen`; control fields; `GENBITS`; reseed interval; FIPS force |The spec states no numeric status code and no command rule for these cells
|`sep_edn_mode_cg` |Auto mode; boot command defaults; reseed rate; command FIFO reset; hardware command type |Auto mode needs the reseed word, which the spec does not state
|`sep_entropy_delivery_cg` |FIPS provenance cells for the native clients; the `rnd_fips_chk_fail` cell; pool write SLVERR; empty read; pool full and low flags beyond `pool_low`; `IRQ_CAUSE` mirror of the `STATUS` bits |The provenance tag is 1 on every path. The open pool test grades the access shape. The open pool aperture test grades `IRQ_CAUSE` against `STATUS`.
|===

[[sep-fcov-p2-fabric-remap]]
==== Fabric and remap groups

These groups extend the Phase 1 groups `sep_axi_decode_cg` and `sep_inbound_filter_allow_cg` with the dense legal cells of the fabric, the filters and the remap blocks. They are Phase 2 groups, named in <<sep-fcov-planned>>. The VPLAN calls the same tier Phase 3.

*Collection rules*

1. Every coverpoint samples a DUT signal. No coverpoint samples a log line or a scoreboard count.
2. Every coverpoint is VCS only. A covergroup sits in `cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` under `ifndef VERILATOR`. An absence point is an SV `cover property` in the same sampler.
3. Bins follow the spec and the RDL. A value that only changes an address or data inside a fixed contract has no bin (see <<sep-fcov-rand>>). A value that the spec leaves to the adopter or does not define has no bin.
4. An absence point needs two qualifiers. The initiator shows a completed response in the window (`sil_dma_not_connected` is the exception: the spec states no response for that class, so an accepted request stands in, and the done or error interrupt of the DMA run ends the window). The probed port shows at least one handshake since reset (`probe_alive`). A simulator that is off cannot fill the point. Each absence point has a paired control in the same leaf that drives the same probe. The sampler gates the silence points on one outstanding transaction of the initiator it watches.
5. Initiator rule. A beat on a shared port belongs to the initiator whose own request tap holds an accepted, unanswered request with the same address, after the alias rule. The sampler discards the sample when no initiator or two initiators match. The initiator taps are `lsu_req`, `dma_req` and `xbar_ext_req`.
6. Seed rule. Every bin fills on every seed of its owner, except the bins that this section marks seed-dependent. The groups of this section therefore carry no `_rand_cg` suffix. A seed-dependent bin records a configuration that the owner draws from its seed. It is graded on a merge of regressions, as a `_rand_cg` group is (<<sep-fcov-rand>>), and no single regression claims it. The seed-dependent bins are the SMC configuration bins of `sep_fabric_smc_route_test`: every cell of `cp_smc_cfg_class_dir`, both causes of `sil_out_on_smc_route` and the above-top bin of `sil_smc_outside_aperture`. The owner draws configuration A or B with equal weight on each seed, so one regression can miss a configuration (VPLAN `sep_fabric_smc_route_test`, Randomization). The stimulus conditions that this needs are listed under "Stimulus conditions".
7. Each group keeps only the bins that a test of this bucket hits. The other bins are listed under "Bins not graded".
8. Tap status. Each tap is approved or an added tap (see "Sampler taps"). A tap reads only and blocks no bin. BH-SMC, the drive of the SMC aperture inputs with the SMC responder, is an added hook (a top-port drive) and blocks no bin. A bin whose every owner is parked is blocked (<<sep-fcov-parked>>) and is not counted as hit.

*Sampler taps*

Each tap is a read-only reference. No tap forces a signal. Paths start at `sep_uvm_top`. Status "approved": the tap exists in `tb/tb_top.sv` and `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` lists it. Status "Added tap": a read-only reference that the owning test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. A hierarchical reference to an internal net and a testbench-only port are both added taps, and neither changes the DUT. The column "At sampler" says which fields the sampler carries.

[cols="2,5,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Tap |Path |At sampler |Status
|`in_req` |`m_axi_*` flat inputs that drive `u_dut.smn_inbound_axi_req_i` |Address: yes. `id`, `len`, `size`, `burst`, `prot`, `user`: no |approved for the address. Added tap for `id`, `len`, `size`, `burst`, `prot`, `user`
|`in_resp` |`u_dut.smn_inbound_axi_resp_o` (`m_axi_b*`, `m_axi_r*`) |`resp`, `last`: yes. `rdata`, `rid`: no |approved for `resp`, `last`. Added tap for `rdata`, `rid`
|`out_req` |`smn_outbound_req_w` = `u_dut.smn_outbound_axi_req_o` |No. The always-ready outbound responder answers OKAY and serves every capture cell |Added tap (PR-OUT)

|`xbar_ext_req` |`u_dut.u_sep.smn_inbound_to_sep_axi_req`, `_resp` |The existing `xbar_ext_in_*` testbench taps carry the low 32 address bits and the handshakes only |approved (PR-XEXT)

|`alias_out` |The input and output requests of `u_local_master_remap_wrap` of `sep_system_peripherals`: address and AxCACHE |No |Added tap (PR-ALIAS)

|`pre_out_filter` |`u_dut.u_sep.u_sep_system_peripherals.pre_outbound_filter_axi_req`, `_resp` |No |Added tap

*Build facts*

* The graded VCS coverage run uses `--target default` only: one full-CPU elaboration. A `no_cpu` test force-splices the LSU VIP onto the post-alias bus `lsu_axi_req`. The sampler is not compiled under Verilator, so no coverpoint of this section has a Verilator build.
* The default target defines `SEP_SMN_INBOUND_AXI_LIVE` and `SEP_JTAG_AXIL_LIVE`. The SMC route leaves need the bench hook BH-SMC of the VPLAN: the SMC responder enabled for their target, the aperture inputs driven from plusargs or from the leaf before reset release (they are tied to 0 otherwise), and the testbench SMC decode check widened or gated by a plusarg for the aperture. The PR-SMC counters are an added tap. The SMC route fires only in a run that drives the aperture. The hook is an added hook (a top-port drive) that the owning tests add to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`.
* The pre-alias net `lsu_axi_req_raw` exists in the real CPU wrapper only. The stub build has the post-alias net only.

*Stimulus conditions*

A reach entry of the form "when the owner satisfies X" names one condition below. Each condition is a contract on the owning test, so that the bin fills on every seed.

[cols="2,3,4",options="header"]
|===
|Condition |Owner |Contract
|`corner_dir_group` |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` |Each corner cell, the no-match cell included, runs once as a read and once as a write on each instance it applies to. The seed changes values, not which cell runs.
|`src_id_all_classes` |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` |Each seed runs, on each instance, a source-ID exact, mismatch and wildcard cell, each as a read and as a write. The inbound fall-through on a source-ID mismatch also runs as a read and as a write. The seed picks the source ID values. The outbound fall-through on a source-ID mismatch comes from `sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test`, which runs the AP and STEE stack as a read and as a write on every seed.
|`valid_clear_cacheable_f` |`sep_fabric_alias_remap_attr_rand_test` |The valid-clear run repeats the corner with `cacheable` 0xF.
|`row_matrix_every_seed` |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` |Every seed walks every (row, cell kind, direction, initiator) cell of the model inventory, the burst cells included. The seed picks offset, size, ID and order. Each OTBN leg runs from the LSU with the fixed loop over AxCACHE 0x0, 0x2, 0x3 and 0xF on each channel, so AxCACHE[1] = 0 and 1 both occur.
|`alias_all_classes` |`sep_cpu_lsu_alias_window_twin_test` |Every seed covers all five unit classes and the SRAM start and last words. The seed picks the static word values.
|`dest_class_both_dirs` |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` |Each destination class is probed in both directions, in the reset-state leg and in the match legs. The seed picks the address.
|`ge4g_both_dirs` |`sep_fabric_alias_remap_attr_rand_test` |The alias hit probes, which translate to an address at or above 0x1_0000_0000, run as a read and as a write.
|`dma_ext_read_leg` |`sep_fabric_dma_endpoint_matrix_test` |One directed leg reads from the extension port with the DMA.
|===

Reach values (<<sep-fcov-parked>>). "executable": the owner fills every bin as written. "executable in part": some cells are blocked; the cell names them and the PROPOSED item or the parked owner. "parked: ...": no bin is executable; the cell names the PROPOSED item or the parked owner, and no bin is counted. A condition that the cell names must also hold.

===== `sep_fabric_inbound_aperture_cg`

*Description*: The inbound aperture of `SEP_GLOBAL_BASE_ADDR` and `SEP_REGION_SIZE` classifies each System Interface request: in-window rebase, outside-window pass-through, the rebased limit, the destination-class verdict and the burst region response.

*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="2,6,3,3,3,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Reach
|`cp_in_ap_class` |`target_sram`, `target_scratch` (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology). Response OKAY. `bottom_word`, `top_word`, `last_byte`: the `in_req` address minus the base equals 0, the window size minus 8 and the window size minus 1. The response is not graded. |- |`in_req` address against `aperture`; `in_resp` response |`sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` |executable
|`cp_out_ap_class` |`one_byte_above_top`, `below_base`. Response DECERR. |- |`in_req` address against `aperture`; `in_resp` response |`sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` |executable
|`cp_translation` |`in_window_rebased` (`xbar_ext_req` address = `in_req` address minus base), `outside_window_unchanged` (`xbar_ext_req` address = `in_req` address). |- |`in_req`, `xbar_ext_req`, `aperture` |`sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` |executable
|`cp_rebased_limit` |`forwarded_below_0x4000_0000` (`xbar_ext_req` handshake), `decerr_at_or_above_0x4000_0000` (rebased or unchanged address, `in_resp` DECERR, no `xbar_ext_req` handshake) (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology). |- |`in_req`, `in_resp`, `xbar_ext_req`, `aperture` |`sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` |executable
|`cp_dest_class_verdict` |Class {`mailbox`, `system_csr`, `loopback`} x direction {R, W} x verdict {`blocked`}, where `blocked` means `in_resp` DECERR with no enabled entry of `in_filter` covering the address. 6 cells. Also `loopback` x {R, W} x {`admitted`}, where `admitted` means `in_resp` OKAY and an `xbar_ext_req` handshake. 2 cells. Also `unreachable_unit` x {R, W} x {`refused_with_entry`}, where `refused_with_entry` means `in_resp` DECERR with an enabled entry of `in_filter` covering the address. 2 cells. 10 cells in all. Class by the decode window of the peripheral crossbar, taken from the local address (the `in_req` address, or the `xbar_ext_req` address for a request inside the rebased window): `mailbox` 0x10A0_0000-0x10A0_FFFF, `system_csr` 0x10A1_0000-0x10A4_FFFF and 0x1080_2000-0x1080_20FF (the scratch window), `unreachable_unit` an address in the Boot ROM, Reset Control, AP region or STEE region row (`memory_map.adoc`, Units Unreachable from the Inbound Port), `loopback` every other address below 0x4000_0000 (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology; `sep-input-fabric.svg`). |- |`in_req`, `in_resp`, `in_filter`, `xbar_ext_req` |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test`, `sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` |executable. The `blocked` cells also need `dest_class_both_dirs`
|`cp_burst_region` |SI burst to the crypto region, with an enabled entry that admits it, x start kind {`in_extent_reg`, `hole`} x direction. 4 cells. (`crypto.adoc`, Single-Beat Access Only) |A crypto-region burst with a response other than DECERR |`in_req` length, burst, address; `in_filter`; `in_resp` response |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` |executable
|===

[cols="2,6,3,3,3,2",options="header"]

|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Reach

The `ge_4g` cells also need `ge4g_both_dirs`
|`cp_smu_aperture_cfg` |Configuration {reset 0x8000_0000/0x4000_0000} x probe {`first_word`, `last_word`}. 2 cells. The SMU aperture registers are not written and the route of an address outside every window is blocked by the spec conflict, both in Unreachable bins. (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `SMU_GLOBAL_BASE_ADDR` and `SMU_REGION_SIZE`) |- |`smu_aperture`; `out_req` address |`sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test` |executable
|`cp_smc_cfg_class_dir` |Configuration A (0x4000_0000/0x0100_0000) x class {`first_word`, `last_word`} x direction, and configuration B (0x9000_0000/0x0100_0000) x class {`first_word`, `last_word`} x direction. 8 cells, all with stimulus. Seed-dependent (rule 6): the owner draws one configuration per seed, so the cells of a configuration fill only on the seeds that draw it, and the coverpoint is graded on a merge. The word above the top has no bin here: the owner requires no `smc_req` handshake for it, and `sil_smc_outside_aperture` holds it. (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `SMC_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS`; `fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology) |- |`smc_aperture`; `smc_req` handshake; `lsu_req` address |`sep_fabric_smc_route_test` |executable
|`cp_smc_readiness` |`request_after_fuse_sense_done`: an `smc_req` handshake while `smc_fuse_sense_done_i` is 1. (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `SMC_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS`) |- |`smc_fuse_done`; `smc_req` |`sep_fabric_smc_route_test` |executable
|`cp_out_attr` |Route {`smu`, `ap`, `stee`} x `prot[1]` {0, 1}, with AxPROT equal to the issued value; route {`ap`, `stee`} with AxUSER 0 while the LSU drives a non-zero AxUSER; route {`smu`} with AxUSER equal to the issued non-zero value; DMA initiator on {`smu`} with AxUSER 0. (`fabric.adoc`, Transaction Routing) |- |`out_req` prot, user; `lsu_req` prot; `dma_req` for the DMA cells |`sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test`, `sep_fabric_dma_endpoint_matrix_test` |executable

The DMA alias cells (source or destination alias to SRAM, destination alias to ext top) take the form from `dma_req_raw` (an address in 0xD000_0000-0xFFFF_FFFF) and the translated address from `dma_req`. (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology; `memory_map.adoc`, Memory Map; `sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `SEP_LOCAL_BASE_ADDR`) |- |`lsu_req_raw`, `lsu_req`, `dma_req_raw`, `dma_req`, `local_base` |`sep_cpu_lsu_alias_window_twin_test` (LSU classes), `sep_fabric_dma_endpoint_matrix_test` (DMA cells) |executable. ===== `sep_filter_match_cg`

*Description*: The filter match rule: a failing condition falls through to a higher entry, the source ID classes, independent AW and AR decode and the winner class.

*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="2,6,3,3,3,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Reach
|`cp_fail_cond_fallthrough` |Instance x direction x failing condition on the lowest covering entry {`entry_disabled`, `ns_mismatch`, `src_mismatch`, `burst_mismatch`}. A higher entry admits. The lowest covering entry fails exactly one condition. `src_mismatch` compares `src_id` with AxUSER[3:0]: inbound probes carry a drawn AxUSER[3:0], outbound probes carry OTHERS or the LSU bench master AxUSER. Legal cells: inbound 4 conditions x 2 directions = 8, outbound 3 conditions x 2 directions = 6, 14 in all. The inbound cells and the outbound `entry_disabled` and `ns_mismatch` cells come from `sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test`. The outbound `src_mismatch` cells come from the AP and STEE source-ID stack of `sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test`, where OTHERS does not match the lowest entry and a higher entry admits. (filter doc, Match, Then Permit; `smc-traffic-filters.svg`) |Outbound `burst_mismatch`: no local master issues a multi-beat burst (`fabric.adoc`, Top Level AXI4). Ignored, not a hole of the owner |`in_filter`, `out_filter` fields; `in_req` prot, len, user; `pre_out_filter` prot, user; `in_resp` or `pre_out_filter` verdict |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test`, `sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test` (outbound `src_mismatch`) |executable. Also needs `corner_dir_group` and `src_id_all_classes`
|`cp_src_id_class` |Instance {`in`, `out`} x class {`wildcard`, `exact`, `mismatch`}. 6 cells. `wildcard` is `src_id` 0. `exact` is a non-zero `src_id` equal to the probe AxUSER[3:0]. `mismatch` is a non-zero `src_id` different from the probe AxUSER[3:0], AxUSER[3:0] 0 included. Outbound `exact` and `mismatch` use the LSU bench master AxUSER. (`filter_ctrl.adoc`, `FILTER_CONFIG`; filter doc, Match, Then Permit; `smc-traffic-filters.svg`) |- |`in_filter`, `out_filter` src_id; `in_req` user[3:0]; `pre_out_filter` user[3:0] |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` |executable. Also needs `src_id_all_classes`
|`cp_ar_aw_independent` |Instance x {the AR winner index differs from the AW winner index for one address}. 2 cells. (filter doc, Match, Then Permit) |- |`in_filter`, `out_filter` fields; `in_req` and `pre_out_filter` prot |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_winner_class` |Instance x class {`lowest_allow`, `perm_block`, `perm_block_hides_allow`, `fallthrough_allow`, `no_match`}, without the cell (`in`, `perm_block`). 9 cells. The cell (`in`, `perm_block`) is a control of the owning test, because `sep_fabric_inbound_filter_rule_matrix_test` grades the inbound permission bits. `perm_block_hides_allow` means that a higher entry would admit. (filter doc, Match, Then Permit) |- |`in_filter`, `out_filter` fields; `in_req` and `pre_out_filter` request; `in_resp` and `pre_out_filter` verdict |`sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` |executable. Also needs `corner_dir_group`

===== `sep_aperture_reserved_access_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: The refused-access response codes of every address map row. This group owns those codes (see <<sep-fcov-planned>>). The coverpoints below extend it from three apertures to every address map row.

*Tool*: VCS. Home: another bucket section. This section adds the cells below.

[cols="2,6,3,3,3,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Reach
|`cp_row_cell` |Row x cell kind x response cell. Rows and cells come from the address map table: 8 Reserved rows by base address (0x1005_0000, 0x1080_4000, 0x1091_4000, 0x1092_1000, 0x1093_0600, 0x1096_0000, 0x10C0_0000, 0x1200_0000; DECERR; 0xBADCAB1E read data with DECERR); hole-in-extent cells (SLVERR 0xFFFFFFFF for OTBN, HMAC, KMAC; OKAY 0 for ESRC; OKAY 0 for a drawn ABR hole word at a 32-bit access from SI); past-extent cells (DECERR for DMA, WDT, reset control, OTBN, AES, CSRNG, EDN, ESRC, LC, KM mailbox); the live word (OKAY), a stable register of the extent with no read side effect. The response and data are graded at a 32-bit access (AxSIZE 2) in either half of the 64-bit data bus, from SI for every row except reset control, and from the LSU for the reset-control cells and the live words. AxSIZE 3 has no bin. |Forwarded rows (AP, STEE), adopter-defined cells (TRNG, SEP external), the PIC rows, the DMA hole cells, the mailbox-wrapper hole and past-extent cells, the SPI host past-extent cells, the entropy pool cells, the alias remap bank, filter bank and SEP CPU control cells, the eFuse control and token past-extent cells, the Reserved rows 0x10A1_0280, 0x10A1_0380, 0x10A2_0400, 0x10A2_1200 and 0x10A3_2000, the two scratch gap Reserved rows (0x1080_2040, 0x1080_20C0), and the ABR past-extent cells: not binned (graded by other tests) |`in_resp` response and read data; `lsu_resp`; `lsu_req` address |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` |executable. Also needs `row_matrix_every_seed`
|`cp_row_init_dir` |Initiator {`LSU`, `SI`} x direction {R, W}. 4 cells, crossed with `cp_row_cell` on the SI rows of `assets/sep_axi_connectivity.svg`. The LSU cells cross only the reset-control and live-word cells. |- |`in_req`; `lsu_req` |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` |executable. The cross follows `cp_row_cell`
|`cp_aperture_offset_dir` |Aperture {reset control} x class {`in_extent`, `immediately_above`, `far_inside_reserved`} x direction. 6 cells. |- |`lsu_req` address; `lsu_resp` response |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` |executable. Also needs `row_matrix_every_seed`
|`cp_non_aliasing` |A gap, tail or hole write followed by a read of the live reference register, for {reset control, HMAC, KMAC}. 3 cells. (`memory_map.adoc`, Memory Map) |- |`lsu_req` write and read sequence; `in_req` write address for the SI hole cells |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` |executable

===== `sep_fabric_port_silence_cp`

*Description*: A covergroup with one coverpoint per property and one bin per cause. Each point records one form: a request is accepted at initiator X for class Y, a response follows at X, no handshake occurs on probe P between the two, and `probe_alive` is true. The response is the one the spec states for the class. The point `sil_dma_not_connected` drops the response qualifier, because the spec states no response for a not-connected DMA target: it needs an accepted `dma_req` request and `probe_alive`, and its window ends at the done or error interrupt of the DMA run.

*Tool*: VCS. *Class*: executable.

[cols="2,6,3,3,2",options="header"]
|===
|Property |Bins |Probe and initiator |Owning tests |Reach
|`sil_out_local_target` |LSU access to {`scratch`, `csr`} completes OKAY. No `out_req` handshake. The access passes through the route demux of `sep_system_peripherals`. (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology) |`out_req`; `lsu_req` |`sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test` |executable
|`sil_out_filter_deny` |Outbound request refused by the filter because every covering entry is disabled. No `out_req` handshake. 1 bin. The NS mismatch refusal is a cell of `sep_filter_match_cg.cp_fail_cond_fallthrough`. (filter doc, Blocked Transactions) |`out_req`; `lsu_req` |`sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test` |executable
|`sil_smc_outside_aperture` |An access one byte above the supplied SMC aperture, and an access to the SMU window. No `smc_req` handshake. The above-top bin is seed-dependent (rule 6): it fills on a configuration A seed, graded on a merge. The SMU-window bin fills on every seed. (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology) |`smc_req`; `lsu_req` |`sep_fabric_smc_route_test` |executable
|`sil_out_on_smc_route` |An access that takes `smc_req`. No `out_req` handshake. Cause {`smc_only`, `smu_overlap`}. 2 bins. Seed-dependent (rule 6): `smc_only` fills on a configuration A seed and `smu_overlap` on a configuration B seed, graded on a merge. |`out_req`; `lsu_req` |`sep_fabric_smc_route_test` |executable
|`sil_ext_shim_window` |The first and last byte of the shim window. No `ext_req` handshake. 2 bins. (`memory_map.adoc`, External Window Reference Model) |`ext_req`; `lsu_req`, `in_req` |`sep_fabric_extension_port_window_test` |executable
|`sil_ext_filter_deny` |SI burst with the entry disabled: DECERR. No `xbar_ext_req` handshake. |`xbar_ext_req`; `in_req` |`sep_fabric_extension_port_window_test` |executable
|`sil_inbound_refused` |Inbound refusal with no forward. Cause {`filter_deny`, `rebase_ge_0x4000_0000`}. No `xbar_ext_req` or `sys_csr_req` handshake. 2 bins. (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology; filter doc, Blocked Transactions) |`xbar_ext_req`, `sys_csr_req`; `in_req` |`sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` (rebase), `sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test` (filter_deny) |executable
|`sil_dma_not_connected` |DMA request to {boot ROM base, a DMA CSR word that no CPU access touches during the DMA leg}, accepted at `dma_req` with no response required. No `rom_req` handshake and no `dma_csr_req` handshake from the response of the `CONTROL.GO` write to the done or error interrupt of the DMA run, while the CPU waits in `wfi`. 2 bins. (`fabric.adoc`, Top Level AXI4) |`rom_req`, `dma_csr_req`; `dma_req` |`sep_fabric_dma_endpoint_matrix_test` |executable
|===

*Unreachable bins*

[cols="2,1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Item |Class |Reason
|Extension class 0x2000_0000 on the port |Permanent hole |The shim window is diverted before the port (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology; `memory_map.adoc`, External Window Reference Model).
|Outbound filter `burst_mismatch` cells |Permanent hole |No local master issues a multi-beat burst (`fabric.adoc`, Top Level AXI4). The outbound path carries single beats.
|Source ID value of the CPU masters |Blocked |The numeric value of `SepSourceId` is not stated (`fabric.adoc`, Transaction Routing; `cpu.adoc`). The outbound `exact` cell uses the LSU bench master AxUSER, and no bin names the CPU value.
|Which inbound masters drive which AxUSER values |Blocked |The spec does not list the masters of `smn_inbound_axi_req_i` or their source ID values. The inbound cells use the bench master.
|Same-cycle filter write and request |Permanent hole |The spec states no ordering between a CSR write and the next transaction, and no observable shows the collision cycle.
|SMU aperture at a non-reset pair |Blocked |No address separates a programmed non-reset aperture from the reset one: the spec holds two conflicting rules for an address outside every window (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology and Address Remapping). No leaf writes the SMU aperture registers.
|CPU fault on an error response |Blocked |The spec states no CPU consequence of an error response on a load.
|SMN catch-all classes below 0x1_0000_0000 outside the named windows |Blocked |The spec holds two conflicting rules (`fabric.adoc`, Fabric Topology and Address Remapping). The word above the supplied SMC aperture top is one of these classes: only its absence from the SMC port is a bin (`sil_smc_outside_aperture`).
|CPU DBG cells (extension port, TCM refused, ROM refused) |No stimulus |No owning test of this section drives the CPU DBG system bus. See <<sep-fcov-p2-unresolved-fabric-remap,Unresolved signal paths>>.
|Cross-channel divergent verdict with both requests in flight |No stimulus |The owner sends the read and the write one after the other.
|DMA to the boot ROM as a legal endpoint |Not connected |`fabric.adoc` (Top Level AXI4) and `assets/sep_axi_connectivity.svg` show the DMA has no boot ROM path. The fabric endpoint matrix grades it as not connected.
|===

*Bins not graded*

No owning test of this section grades the bins below. Each row states why.

[cols="2,3,4",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Bin |Reason
|`sep_fabric_dedicated_port_cg` |Boot ROM refused initiator for CPU DBG |No owning test of this section drives the CPU DBG system bus.
|`sep_fabric_dedicated_port_cg` |Boot ROM refused initiator System Interface, read and write |The open `sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test` (CHK-DBG-UNREACHABLE) drives the System Interface read and write of the first and last Boot ROM word. No coverpoint of this section samples them.
|`sep_fabric_dedicated_port_cg` |CPU TCM refused initiators, 11 of the 16 cells |`sep_cpu_demux_unmapped_fetch_test` drives the other 5 cells (CPU IFU read of ICCM, CPU LSU read and write of ICCM, CPU LSU read and write of DCCM) under `cp_tcm_refused_initiator` of the memory bucket. No owning test of this section drives the other 11.
|`sep_fabric_dedicated_port_cg` |AXI Extension port attributes (AxSIZE, AxID, AxUSER values) |The values only change data or an address inside a fixed contract (<<sep-fcov-rand>>).
|`sep_fabric_dedicated_port_cg` |Response of the extension port at each initiator (tie-off response) |The port response is the adopter's value. The bench drives a constant, so the bin cannot show DUT behaviour.
|`sep_fabric_dedicated_port_cg` |Extension address class 0x2000_0000 |Permanent hole: the literal region base never appears on the port.
|`sep_fabric_inbound_aperture_cg` |Aperture base reset 0x0 and base with bits [55:32] zero; size reset and size below reset |Every test configuration uses a base with bits [55:32] non-zero and a size above reset.
|`sep_fabric_inbound_aperture_cg` |Aperture base and size classes (a size class that covers the local space, a partial size) |The spec states no legal base or size values (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`). A size class is a stimulus choice and not a specified cell.
|`sep_fabric_outbound_route_cg` |SMN catch-all class 0x0000_0000-0x0FFF_FFFF and "local-region address outside every target" |The spec gives two conflicting rules.
|`sep_fabric_outbound_route_cg` |SMC configuration with the static pair 0x4000_0000/0x4000_0000; SMU aperture set to a non-reset pair |The SMC route test holds two aperture pairs. No leaf writes the SMU aperture registers.
|`sep_fabric_outbound_route_cg` |SMC readiness before fuse sense done |The hang is a property of the SMC fabric, outside SEP.
|`sep_fabric_outbound_route_cg` |`cp_smc_cfg_class_dir` class `inside` (a marker word inside the configuration A aperture) |The `rom_fw` boot tests drive it in the `rom_boot` target, and the graded coverage run uses the default target only.
|`sep_fabric_outbound_route_cg` |Two outbound initiators in flight |The spec states no concurrency or forward-progress rule for two outbound initiators.
|`sep_fabric_outbound_route_cg` |AxUSER of the CPU master beat on the SMC or outbound port |The value of `SepSourceId` is not stated.
|`sep_fabric_inbound_aperture_cg` |Key Manager window burst; burst length classes; burst starts on past-extent words and reserved rows |`sep_fabric_deadspace_decode_test` grades bursts into the Key Manager window and every crypto window. The spec states the rule whatever the length and address, so length is a value only. A past-extent or reserved-row start answers DECERR to a single beat as well, so the burst rule cannot show there.
|`sep_remap_output_offset_cg` |Offset high bits {zero, bit 32, bit 55}; low-bit pass-through with offset bits [18:0] non-zero; seams; out-of-region class |The offset is a seeded draw. `sep_fabric_outbound_remap_filter_walk_test` grades the low-bit pass-through with a random offset, and the open walk tests grade the seams and the entry walk.
|`sep_remap_alias_cg` |Entry index; degenerate, inverted and overlapping entries; offset overflow past bit 55; reserved low bits; low-bit preservation; same-page consistency |The rejection list of `sep_fabric_alias_remap_attr_rand_test` excludes them.
|`sep_filter_match_cg` |`group_id` no-effect cells |The specification states that a transaction with group ID 0 matches whatever the field holds, and every transaction of this bench carries group ID 0, so no check can fail.
|`sep_filter_match_cg` |Cross-channel divergent verdict with both requests in flight |The owner sends the read and the write one after the other.
|`sep_filter_match_cg` |Match matrix cells with two or more failing conditions; outbound `burst_mismatch` |Each cell has one failing condition. No local master issues a multi-beat burst.
|`sep_filter_match_cg` |`cp_winner_class` cell (`in`, `perm_block`) |The inbound permission-block cells are controls of `sep_fabric_filter_match_priority_rand_test`, and `sep_fabric_inbound_filter_rule_matrix_test` grades them.
|`sep_filter_match_cg` |Source ID value classes (each of the 15 non-zero values) |The value only changes which entry matches inside the fixed rule. The class bins carry the contract.
|`sep_filter_range_granule_cg` |`cp_granule_shape_inst` cells 8B x one, 8B x equal and inbound 4K x one |Open tests grade them: `sep_fabric_inbound_filter_rule_matrix_test`, `sep_fabric_inbound_filter_window_walk_test` and `sep_fabric_outbound_remap_filter_walk_test`.
|`sep_filter_range_granule_cg` |Aligned range with no widening; inverted range; write-back transition back to one granule |The granule leg does not drive them. Its rejection list excludes the inverted range.
|`sep_filter_range_granule_cg` |Write-back fires or is suppressed |Driven by the equal and straddle shapes. The read-back is logged and not graded, because the specification states no write-back timing.
|`sep_aperture_reserved_access_cg` |Entropy pool, eFuse control and token, alias remap bank, filter bank and SEP CPU control cells; the Reserved rows 0x10A1_0280, 0x10A1_0380, 0x10A2_0400, 0x10A2_1200 and 0x10A3_2000; watchdog, scratch and SPI gap cells |Open tests grade them: `sep_entropy_pool_aperture_test`, `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test`, `sep_address_map_test` and `sep_fabric_deadspace_decode_test`.
|`sep_remap_alias_cg` |`cp_local_alias_twin` LSU classes `ext_mid`, `ext_top` and the shim twin |The extension tie-off answers an LSU access with DECERR and the CPU consequence of an error response is not stated. The DMA alias cell to `ext_top` stays.
|`sep_remap_alias_cg` |`cp_local_alias_twin` classes DMA CSR, WDT, reserved row and past extent |No static word is named for the DMA CSR and WDT, and the CPU consequence of an error response is not stated.
|`sep_fabric_port_silence_cp` |SPI access sequence silence on the SMC and extension ports |No stimulus fills the SPI RX FIFO under `no_cpu`.
|`sep_fabric_port_silence_cp` |Silence of the extension port for the reserved word 0x1FFF_FFFC and the word 0x4000_0000 |`sep_fabric_row_response_matrix_test` grades the reserved-word response, and `sep_fabric_inbound_rebase_test` grades the crossbar limit through `xbar_ext_req`. Neither asserts PR-EXT silence for them.
|`sep_filter_default_policy_cg` |Reset field values on 48 entries; enabled entry with the reset range |The reset-state leg does not read the reset fields and enables an entry with its own range.
|`sep_filter_default_policy_cg` |Reset-state probes of the extension window and of 0x4000_0100 |A healthy and a faulty filter both end in DECERR there, so no verdict can fail.
|`sep_filter_default_policy_cg` |`cp_dbg_bypass` cell `inbound_unmatched_read_admitted` |`sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test` and `sep_lcc_demote_feat_ctrl_matrix_test` grade the inbound bypass. `sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test` uses the System Interface read only as the precondition of its outbound check.
|===

[[sep-fcov-p2-mailbox-irq-pic]]
==== Mailbox, IRQ and PIC groups

These Phase 2 groups cover the AXI-Lite mailboxes, the interrupt vector, the
EL2 PIC, the NMI path and the CPU-control fault and timeout registers. They are
Phase 2 groups of <<sep-fcov-planned>>. Each Phase 2 bin of
these groups is scored by a Phase 3 owning test, so the Phase 1 contract of
"full in one `all` run" does not depend on them.

*Tool*: VCS only. Verilator compiles no covergroup. A VCS coverage run builds
target `default`, which holds the full VeeR core, so the PIC and trap-unit paths
exist in that build. The stub target removes the core and has no covergroup.

*Numbering*: bit `i` of the interrupt vector is PIC source `i+1`
(`hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc`, section "Sources and Routing"). The vector
has 43 bits (same section: slots 1 to 43 are individually assigned). Sources 40 to 43 are the token match
fault, the DMA register-path error, the DMA host-path error and the peripheral
bridge fault. `VEC[k]` in a signal column is vector bit `k`, source `k+1`.

*Excluded idle bits*: The specification states no idle level for source 13, source 15 (the Key Manager mailbox line; the reset level of its status bits is open in the specification), source 16 (which entropy source causes it carries is not stated), source 37 (`pool_low`) and source 38 (`fill_stall`). Source 39 is masked because `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` ties it to zero and `hw/sys/sep/doc/entropy_pool.adoc` says the integrity fault is also raised to the PIC, so the two files disagree. A bin that expects 0 or an exact vector on `VEC` or on a `meip` read masks `VEC[12]`, `VEC[14]`, `VEC[15]`, `VEC[36]`, `VEC[37]` and `VEC[38]` and the matching `meip` bits.

*Sampling*:

* All points sample on `clk_i`. The bark leg samples the synchronized bark
(`wdt_bark_irq_i` of `u_sep_fcov`).
* A point ignores a sample while `rst_ni` is low, except the reset points of
`sep_mbox_reset_cg`. Every point, the reset points included, samples only inside
the graded window of an owning test (<<sep-fcov-graded-window>>).
* A 4-state X on a sampled DUT value is an illegal bin where the row says so.
* Every point samples a DUT signal. No point reads log text or a scoreboard
count.
* A quiet window is a span in which the owning leaf holds `quiet_win` at 1. `quiet_win` is a
variable of the Phase 2 sampler that the leaf writes through the testbench handle of
`u_sep_fcov`, under the rules of `graded_owner` (<<sep-fcov-graded-window>>): it is a
monitor-only testbench variable that drives nothing and touches no DUT net, and it gates a
sample and is never a bin value. The leaf sets it to 1 at the end of the settle wait of each
check cycle, after `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` [7:0] and `smc_mailbox_interrupt_o`
equal the golden model, and sets it to 0 before the next access to the mailbox unit. The
specification states no completion latency (`interface.adoc`, "External Interface"), so no
clock between an access and the end of its settle wait is inside a quiet window. A bin
"after a quiet window" samples on the last clock with `quiet_win` at 1. A window that holds a
mailbox AXI beat scores no bin.
* A bin on `LSU` read data samples only the bits of the RDL fields of the register that is
read. A bit whose RDL row is `Reserved` with no access and no reset value has no stated
value: it is masked from the bin and from its illegal bins. A value on read data in a row of
this section ("reads 0", "equals 0b10", a population count, an X check) is the value of the
field bits: `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` and `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_CLEAR` bits [6:0],
`DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS` bits [1:0], `DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR` bit 0 and the mailbox `CTRL` bits [1:0]
(`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `axil_mailbox.adoc`). The read data of an offset that holds no
register keeps its full-word value where the memory map states it (a hole reads 0).
* A bin samples an alias in the testbench, an added tap, or a `sep` port that an added hook drives (see "Observation status").
An added hierarchical reference is a read-only reference from the `u_sep_fcov`
connection block into a unit that `tb_top` does not export. The sampler has one
explicit assign per mailbox index, because a genvar-indexed hierarchical
reference is not used in the tree.

*Class and reachability terms*: the cell starts with the class of the coverpoint (<<sep-fcov-parked>>), and `Stimulus:` gives the reachability.

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Status |Meaning
|Reachable |The stimulus of the owning test can produce the bin
|Walk |The owning test runs every cell of the bin set in each run; the seed picks only the order and the values inside a cell. The bin fills in one run of the owning test. A Walk bin does not depend on which values a seed draws
|Seed-dependent |The owning test draws the cell from its seed, so one run of the owning test, and one regression, can leave the bin empty. The bin is graded on a merge of regressions, as a `_rand_cg` group is (<<sep-fcov-rand>>), and the other bins of the same coverpoint keep the one-run grade. The row names the seed-dependent bins. A leaf has no run index, so no seed pins a cell
|Conditional |The bin is reachable only with the stimulus leg named in the row
|Unreachable (`ignore_bins`) |No stimulus or observation reaches the bin. It is an `ignore_bins` and is not counted as coverage
|executable |Every bin samples an alias in the testbench, an added tap or a `sep` port that an added hook drives, and no bin is blocked
|executable in part |Some bins are blocked: their stimulus needs a PROPOSED item, or they need a value that the specification does not state. `Blocked:` names the bins and the item or value. A blocked bin is not counted as coverage
|parked: <item> |Every bin is blocked. The cell names the item or value. No bin is counted as coverage
|===

A bin comes from the specification and the register description. A bin that
no stimulus reaches is not counted as coverage.

===== Signal aliases

[cols="1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Alias |Path
|`SEP_CORE` |`u_dut.u_sep`
|`VEC` |`sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o[42:0]`, the `tb_top` probe of `u_dut.u_sep.sep_internal_interrupts`
|`SMC` |`smc_mailbox_interrupt_o[7:0]`
|`XM` |External master pins `m_axi_*`: address, ID, user, prot, len, strb, `bresp`, `rresp`. The source ID of the inbound filter is the transaction source ID that the testbench master drives (the specification does not name its carrier signal)
|`LSU` |CPU-LSU bus pins on `u_sep_fcov` (`lsu_aw_*`, `lsu_ar_*`, `lsu_w_*`, `lsu_b_*`, `lsu_r_*`)
|`TRC` |`cpu_trace_valid_i`, `cpu_trace_addr_i`, `cpu_trace_interrupt_i`, `cpu_trace_exc_i`
|`RST` |`sep_reset_n_i`, `cpu_reset_n_i`
|`BARK` |`wdt_bark_irq_i`, from `u_sep.intr_wdog_timer_bark`
|`PIC` |`SEP_CORE.u_sep_cpu.u_el2_veer_wrapper.veer.pic_ctrl_inst`: `intpriority_reg[N]`, `intenable_reg[N]`, `gw_config_reg[N]`, `config_reg`, `gw_clear_reg_we[N]`, `extintsrc_req[N]`, `extintsrc_req_gw[N]`, `picm_rden`, `picm_wren`, `picm_rdaddr`, `picm_wraddr`, `picm_wr_data`, `picm_rd_data`
|`CLAIM` |`SEP_CORE.u_sep_cpu.u_el2_veer_wrapper.veer.pic_claimid[7:0]`
|`TLU` |`SEP_CORE.u_sep_cpu.u_el2_veer_wrapper.veer.dec.tlu`: `mstatus[0]`, `mie[5:0]`, `mip[5:0]`, `take_ext_int`, `take_nmi`, `take_timer_int`, `take_soft_int`
|`MB[m].P0`, `MB[m].P1` |`SEP_CORE.u_sep_system_peripherals.u_sep_axil_mailbox.gen_mailbox[m].u_axi_lite_mailbox.i_slv_port_0` (outbound aperture) and `.i_slv_port_1` (inbound aperture): `status_q`, `irqs_q`, `irqen_q`, `irqp_q`, `error_q`, `wirqt_q`, `rirqt_q`, `slv_req_i`, `irq_o`
|`MB[m].FA`, `MB[m].FB` |`...u_axi_lite_mailbox.i_mbox_0_to_1` (FIFO A) and `.i_mbox_1_to_0` (FIFO B): `usage_o`, `full_o`, `empty_o`, `push_i`, `pop_i`, `flush_i`
|`FILT` |`SEP_CORE.u_sep_system_peripherals.u_inbound_filter`: `isolate_write`, `no_write_filter_matches`, `no_read_filter_matches`, `write_filter_hit_idx`, `filters[f].{entry_enabled,allow_ns,src_id,write_allowed,locked}`
|`SKIP` |`lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, the `tb_top` probe of `SEP_CORE.feat_ctrl` (field `sep_debug`, the filter bypass input)
|`ALERTP` |`crypto_alert_pulse_probe_o[10:0]`, the added read-only testbench probe of `SEP_CORE.u_sep_crypto.crypto_alert_pulse`
|`ERRP` |`sep_cpu_ctrl_err_pulse_probe_o`, the added read-only fault pulse and clear pulse of the seven peripheral bridge latches and of the two DMA latches
|`EXT` |`sep_ext_interrupts_i` (`extintsrc_req_i` of `sep`), the boundary input of the PIC. `tb_top` ties it to 0; the owning test drives it (an added hook: top-port drive)
|`RESP` |`smn_outbound_axi_resp_i`, the `sep` input that the outbound responder `sep_outbound_mbx` drives, with the response control of the responder (an added hook: error inject): a non-OKAY response and a response hold. Without the inject the responder answers OKAY at once
|`PICRD` |`pic_rd_data_probe_o`, the added read-only copy of `picm_rd_data` at a PIC register read
|===

===== Observation status

Each alias is in the testbench, an added tap, or a port that an added hook drives. An alias in the testbench exists in
`hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` or is read by the Phase 1 sampler of
`hw/sys/sep/dv/docs/SEP_FCOV.adoc`. An added tap is a read-only probe port or
hierarchical reference that the owning test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH
observation-probe list. It drives nothing and blocks no bin. An added hook is an error inject
or a top-port drive that the owning test adds to `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).
A bin whose stimulus needs an added hook is not blocked by it.

[cols="1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Alias |Status
|`VEC`, `SMC`, `XM`, `RST`, `BARK`, `SKIP` |In the testbench: `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o`, the DUT port `smc_mailbox_interrupt_o`, the DUT port `m_axi_*`, the SEP reset probes, the direct read of `intr_wdog_timer_bark` and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`
|`LSU` |In the testbench: the post-remap CPU-LSU bus that the Phase 1 sampler reads
|`TRC` |In the testbench: the CPU trace export of `tb_top` that `SepCpuTraceMonitor` reads
|`PIC`, `CLAIM`, `TLU`, `MB[m]`, `FILT` |Added tap. Read-only hierarchical references into the CPU, the mailbox unit and the inbound filter
|`ALERTP`, `ERRP`, `PICRD` |Added tap. Read-only probe ports of `tb_top`
|`EXT`, `RESP` |Added hook. `EXT` is a top-port drive of `extintsrc_req_i` and `RESP` is an error inject on `smn_outbound_axi_resp_i`; the owning test adds each to `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. A bin samples the `sep` port
|===

===== Group definitions

Each group states its description, tool, owning tests, specification, one row
per coverpoint or cross, its illegal bins and its reachability. A cross is
written `cp_a` x `cp_b`.

====== `sep_pic_reg_map_cg`

*Description*: The PIC register map: each register group, entry position, aperture hole and reset read, through the CPU.

*Class*: executable.

*Tool*: VCS covergroup on `u_sep_fcov`. Verilator compiles no covergroup.

*Owning tests*: `sep_pic_reg_rand_test`, `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test`

*Specification*: `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/el2_pic.adoc` (section "EL2_PIC"); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/memory_map.adoc`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section "Software Handling")

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`cp_group` x `cp_dir` |Group meipl, meip, meie, mpiccfg, meigwctrl, meigwclr (picm address bits [14:12]) crossed with read, write. 11 cells. `ignore_bins`: meip write (the response is not stated; the test never writes meip). |`PIC.picm_rden`, `PIC.picm_wren`, `PIC.picm_rdaddr`, `PIC.picm_wraddr` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_entry_pos` x `cp_array` |Entry classes e0, e1, e2_42, e43, e44, e45_127, e128, e129_254, e255, crossed with arrays meipl, meie, meigwctrl, meigwclr. 36 cells. e43 is the last assigned source; e44 is the first unassigned one. |`PIC.picm_*addr[9:2]` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the anchors and at least one drawn entry in each interior range run on every run
|`cp_aperture` x `cp_dir` |Position: decoded register; hole 0x0400-0x0FFC; hole 0x1020-0x1FFC; hole 0x2400-0x2FFC; hole 0x3004-0x3FFC; hole 0x4400-0x4FFC; past extent 0x5400-0x7FFC. Crossed with read, write: 14 cells. |`PIC.picm_*addr[14:0]` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_meip_word` x `cp_bit_class` |Read of meip word 0..7 with exactly one pending bit set in the 256-bit vector (the six excluded idle bits masked), crossed with the bit class {bit 0, bits 1-30, bit 31}. 24 cells. Reachable cells: word 0 with bits 1-30 (the read with source 24 as the only pending source) and word 1 with bits 1-30 (the read with source 33 as the only pending source). 2 cells. `ignore_bins` (22 cells): word 0 bit 0 (source 0 is tied to zero); word 0 bit 31 and word 1 bit 0 (sources 31 and 32 have owners in other areas); word 1 bit 31 and words 2 to 7 (no driven source). |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[255:0]`, `PIC.picm_rd_data` at an meip read |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the single-source reads of source 24 and source 33 run on every run
|`cp_hole_live_neighbour` |hole_read_zero_with_live_neighbour: read data 0 while `intenable_reg[K]` is 1. hole_write_kept_with_live_neighbour: after a write of all ones to a hole, `intenable_reg[K]` and `intpriority_reg[K]` keep their value. K is the entry index in address bits [9:2] of the drawn hole address, and the owning test arms entry K before the hole access. 2 bins. |`PIC.picm_rd_data`, `PIC.intenable_reg[K]`, `PIC.intpriority_reg[K]` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_reset_read` |Read before the first PIC write after reset returns 0 (the six excluded idle bits of `meip` masked), one bin per group (6). Illegal bin: any X in `picm_rd_data` of such a read. |`PIC.picm_rd_data`, a flag cleared by reset and set by the first `picm_wren`; `PICRD` (illegal bin) |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable; X check needs the 4-state VCS build
|`cp_entry0_discard` |Entry 0 of meipl, meie, meigwctrl, meigwclr reads 0 after a write of a non-zero value (4 bins). Entry 1 of the same array keeps its value in the same window. |`PIC.intpriority_reg[0]`, `intenable_reg[0]`, `gw_config_reg[0]`, `picm_rd_data` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_gwclr_read0` |meigwclr read returns 0 after a non-zero write (1 bin). |`PIC.picm_rd_data` at an meigwclr read |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_gwctrl_val` |meigwctrl written value {0, 1, 2, 3} at an implemented entry (4 bins). |`PIC.gw_config_reg[N]` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the owning test writes every value 0 to 3 on every run
|`cp_priord_val` |mpiccfg.priord written {0, 1} (2 bins). |`PIC.config_reg` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_arm_state` |Post-reset arming of a drawn source: both conditions unset; enable set only; non-never priority only; both set (4 cells). Only the last cell lets a source deliver. |`PIC.intenable_reg[N]`, `PIC.intpriority_reg[N]`, `PIC.config_reg` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the drawn sources take each of the four arming cells on every run
|===

*Illegal bins*: `cp_reset_read`: any X in `picm_rd_data` of such a read

====== `sep_pic_source_map_cg`

*Description*: Each catalog interrupt source asserts alone on its own vector bit and shows in `meip`, and a boundary input shows as one pending source above source 43.

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Tool*: VCS covergroup on `u_sep_fcov`. Verilator compiles no covergroup.

*Owning tests*: `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test`

*Specification*: `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section "Sources and Routing", "SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map"); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/el2_pic.adoc` (section "meip[8]"); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (`extintsrc_req_i`)

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`cp_src_assert` |One bin per source the leaf can assert: 1-8 (mailbox), 12 (DMA alert), 14 (SPI), 18-20 (HMAC), 21-23 (KMAC), 24-27 (CSRNG), 28-29 (EDN), 30 (OTBN), 33 (crypto alert). 24 bins. Rising edge of the vector bit. |`VEC` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run drives all 24 sources, each once with its enable at 1
|`x_src_exclusive` |For each of the 24 sources: in the assert cycle, no other vector bit rises (own_only). 24 cells. The one-hot check is a control here and belongs to the crypto section; the cell records that the leaf drove it. |`VEC` and its previous-cycle value |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run drives all 24 sources, each once with its enable at 1
|`cp_meip_vs_vec` |At an meip read, the 32-bit word equals the same bits of `extintsrc_req_gw`, with a live bit set: word 0 live, word 1 live, words 2-7 zero while a live bit is set elsewhere. 3 bins. |`PIC.picm_rd_data`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_pulse_catch` |A high period of at least one clock on vector bit 11 (source 12) and on bit 32 (source 33) is seen by the sampler, and the edge gateway holds it (edge, active high). 2 bins. |`VEC`, `PIC.gw_config_reg[12]`, `PIC.gw_config_reg[33]` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_spi_cause` |Source 14 raised by the SPI error cause with `VEC[13]` equal to the `irq` field of `sep_io_spi_req_o`; the same equality after the write-one-to-clear. 2 bins. The event cause has no bin: the open SPI test grades it. |`VEC[13]`, `sep_io_spi_req_o` `irq` field (DUT port) |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: source 14 runs on every run
|`cp_fifo_empty_hold` |Source 19 (HMAC FIFO empty) and source 22 (KMAC FIFO empty) after a real FIFO set-up: the `meip` pending bit is 1 while `INTR_ENABLE.fifo_empty` is 1 after the set-up (for KMAC with `INTR_STATE.fifo_empty` 1; the HMAC `INTR_STATE` bit is graded in `sep_hmac_fifo_stream_rand_test`), and 0 after `INTR_ENABLE.fifo_empty` clears. 2 bins. The vector compare (state AND enable) belongs to `x_line_gate` of `sep_crypto_intr_cg` in the crypto section. |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[19]`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[22]`, `LSU` write address decode of the enable clear |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: sources 19 and 22 run on every run; the HMAC fill runs under the inject `hmac_fifo_drain_stall_i`, and the KMAC bin is sampled only in a seed where a firmware read shows KMAC `STATUS.fifo_full` = 1
|`cp_gw_mode_used` |Sources 12 and 33 reach pending through an edge, active-high gateway (2 bins). |`PIC.gw_config_reg[12]`, `PIC.gw_config_reg[33]`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_ext_input` |One boundary bit asserted: the number of pending sources of `meip` words 1 to 7 above source 43 is 1 and the assigned sources equal their baseline; the bit released: the number is 0; two bits asserted together: the number is 2. 3 bins. The map of boundary bits to sources is not stated, so no bin holds a source index. |`EXT`, `PIC.picm_rd_data` at an meip read |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` (CHK-EXT-IN) |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run drives one bit, releases it and drives two bits through the added hook `EXT`
|===

*Illegal bins*: none

Bins that no owning test reaches are in <<sep-fcov-mbox-irq-pic-ungraded>>.

====== `sep_pic_enable_priority_cg`

*Description*: The enable, priority and priority-order gate decides which source is delivered.

*Class*: executable.

*Tool*: VCS covergroup on `u_sep_fcov`. Verilator compiles no covergroup.

*Owning tests*: `sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test`

*Specification*: `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/el2_pic.adoc` (section "meipl[256]", "mpiccfg", "meie[256]"); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section "Software Handling")

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`cp_priord` |`priord` 0 and 1 at a delivery (2 bins). |`PIC.config_reg` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`x_level_class_order` |Level class {never, lowest usable, interior, highest usable} crossed with order {standard, reverse}. 8 cells. Never is 0 in standard order and 15 in reverse order. Lowest usable is 1 (standard) and 14 (reverse). Highest usable is 15 and 0. |`PIC.intpriority_reg[N]`, `PIC.config_reg` at a meipl write |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk for the never, lowest usable and highest usable cells: the rounds of every run hold the never value and effective priority 1 in both orders (CHK-NEVER) and effective priority 15 in both orders (CHK-WINNER, CHK-FLIP). The interior cells are Seed-dependent: they come from the drawn priorities and close on a merge of regressions. The encoding sweep is outside the graded window
|`cp_level_val` |All 16 `intpriority` encodings at a meipl write (16 bins). The rounds write the never values 0 and 15 and the effective-priority-1 values 1 and 14 on every run (CHK-NEVER), and the other encodings from the drawn effective priorities (CHK-GATED, CHK-WINNER). The encoding sweep is outside the graded window and does not fill the bin. |`PIC.intpriority_reg[N]` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: the encodings 0, 1, 14 and 15 are Walk; the encodings 2 to 13 are Seed-dependent and close on a merge of regressions
|`x_gate_phase` |Gate held closed {enable 0, never priority, mstatus.MIE 0} crossed with phase {held while pending, released and delivered}. 6 cells. Held means pending for at least one full handler time with no delivery. |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[N]`, `PIC.intenable_reg[N]`, `PIC.intpriority_reg[N]`, `TLU.mstatus[0]`, `TLU.take_ext_int` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_winner` |With 2, 3, 4 or 5 sources pending, enabled and usable (4 bins), the first delivered source has the highest effective priority. Illegal bin: first delivered source is not the highest (no ties are drawn). The reference order comes from the spec priority order, not from the PIC output. |`CLAIM`, `PIC.intpriority_reg[]`, `PIC.config_reg`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Seed-dependent: the set size of each round is drawn from 2 to 5; graded on a merge of regressions
|`cp_stay_pending` |While a handler runs, at least one other candidate stays pending (1 bin). |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw`, `TLU.take_ext_int` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_never_twin` |Enabled source at the never value does not deliver (its `meip` bit is a record and not required), for order standard (value 0) and reverse (value 15); then the same source at effective priority 1 delivers. 4 bins. |`PIC.*`, `TLU.take_ext_int`, `CLAIM` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: each order runs in at least one round, and each round runs the never corner
|`cp_order_flip` |`priord` written 0 to 1 and 1 to 0 while no source is both pending and enabled (2 bins). A write under load is not driven (the spec does not state it). |`PIC.config_reg`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw`, `PIC.intenable_reg` |`sep_pic_priority_gate_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|===

*Illegal bins*: `cp_winner`: first delivered source is not the highest (no ties are drawn)

|`cp_clr_state` |Clear written while the edge bit is pending and the input is deasserted (drops); clear written while idle (stays 0). 2 bins. |`PIC.gw_clear_reg_we[N]`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[N]` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_level_hold` |Level-high source stays pending over the hold window; the bit drops after the IP status clear. 2 bins. |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[N]` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable

====== `sep_pic_no_delivery_cg`

*Description*: Source 0, tied sources, the idle band and slotless blocks raise no delivery.

*Class*: executable.

*Tool*: VCS covergroup on `u_sep_fcov`. Verilator compiles no covergroup.

*Owning tests*: `sep_pic_reg_rand_test`, `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test`, `sep_nmi_vector_bark_rand_test`

*Specification*: `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section "Sources and Routing", "SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map", "Software Handling"); `hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (section "OpenTitan Alert Termination")

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`cp_src0` |Entry 0 of the four arrays takes all-ones writes and keeps 0 (discard); with mie.MEIE and mstatus.MIE set over the hold window `extintsrc_req_gw[0]` stays 0 and no delivery has claim id 0. 2 bins. Every write to entry 0 is discarded, so the 16 source 0 configurations collapse to one bin; the other 15 are unreachable (`ignore_bins`). |`PIC.intenable_reg[0]`, `PIC.intpriority_reg[0]`, `PIC.gw_config_reg[0]`, `PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[0]`, `CLAIM` |`sep_pic_reg_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_idle_band` |Sources 44-255 read 0 while a live source bit is 1 in the same window (1 bin); meip[1] bits 12-31 and meip[2] to meip[7] read 0 in the same window (1 bin). |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw[255:44]`, `PIC.picm_rd_data` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable (sampled through the pending band `extintsrc_req_gw[255:44]` and `meip` words 2 to 7)
|`cp_tied_sources` |Source 17 (`VEC[16]`) reads 0 while a live source is 1 (1 bin). The read of sources 16 and 39 is not graded (<<sep-fcov-mbox-irq-pic-ungraded>>). |`VEC` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_slotless_block` |OTP read; life-cycle controller read. Every vector bit except the control, the six excluded idle bits and, in the OTP read, `VEC[33]` (source 34) equals its value at the start of the operation on every clock of the operation (2 bins). The held control source 24 reads high on every clock of the window, so the sampler is live. `ignore_bins`: AES operation complete (`cp_aes_quiet` of `sep_crypto_intr_cg` in the crypto section owns it). |`VEC` (start value and current, every clock), `LSU` address decode, `LSU` response valid |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_quiet_vector_x` |The 43-bit vector holds no X after reset and during the idle window (1 bin; illegal bin: any X). |`VEC` |`sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test` |executable; X check needs the 4-state VCS build
|`cp_bark_vec_same` |The bark leaves `extintsrc_req_gw` unchanged across NMI entry while a live PIC bit is set (1 bin). Same event as `cp_vec_unchanged`. |`PIC.extintsrc_req_gw`, `TLU.take_nmi` |`sep_nmi_vector_bark_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|===

*Illegal bins*: `cp_quiet_vector_x`: any X

The bins "AES recoverable alert" and "AES fatal alert leave the vector unchanged" do not exist: an AES alert raises source 33 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (section "OpenTitan Alert Termination")).

|`cp_no_land` |Illegal bin: any change of `intpriority_reg`, `intenable_reg`, `gw_config_reg` or `config_reg` in a cycle with no CPU PIC write. 1 illegal bin plus 1 bin "external write completed with state unchanged". |`PIC.*`, `PIC.picm_wren` |`sep_pic_external_access_refuse_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable

====== `sep_nmi_vector_cfg_cg`

*Description*: The NMI vector register: value class, programming order at bark enable, and the NMI target.

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`cp_vec_class` |Vector value class at a write: ICCM 0xC000_0000-0xC003_FFFF (trampoline and hand-staged handler). 1 bin. `ignore_bins`: reset value (0xC000_0100 as address, field 0x6000_0080; no step writes it, and the reset vector is covered by `cp_order_at_enable` and `cp_nmi_target`), DCCM, reserved CPU tail and other (the owning test writes executable vectors only). |`LSU` write data of the `SEP_NMI_VEC` write |`sep_nmi_vector_bark_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk (every run writes one drawn trampoline copy and `A`, both in ICCM)
|`cp_order_at_enable` |At the moment the bark is enabled: vector at reset value; vector written and locked. 2 bins. The third bin "vector written, lock clear" has no stimulus leg in the owning test and is an `ignore_bins`. |`LSU` writes: `SEP_NMI_VEC` data, `SEP_NMI_VEC_LOCK` and the WDT enable |`sep_nmi_vector_bark_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: two bins reachable; the third bin (vector written, lock clear) is unreachable as the owning test is specified (`ignore_bins`)
|`cp_nmi_target` |First retired PC after an NMI entry equals: the reset vector 0xC000_0100; one of the four trampoline copies (4 bins); the programmed free address A of the hand-staged handler. 6 bins. |`BARK` and the first retired `TRC.addr` after it, `LSU` write data of the `SEP_NMI_VEC` write |`sep_nmi_vector_bark_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: the reset vector and `A` are Walk: every run takes both. The four trampoline-copy bins are Seed-dependent: each run takes one drawn copy, so the four bins close on a merge of regressions. The observation is the retired PC
|===

*Illegal bins*: none

Stimulus: Walk: every run faults all three blocks
|`cp_set_size` |Number of latched blocks {1, 2, 3}. 3 bins. |population count of the `LSU` read data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the first faults of every run latch one, two and then three blocks
|`x_site_resp` |Hole write of hmac, kmac and otbn with the response SLVERR. 3 cells. The SLVERR of a hole read and of a CPU access to an unmapped Secure DMA register offset are graded by `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` and have no cell. Illegal bin: OKAY or DECERR to a hole write. |`LSU` b resp |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run writes a hole of all three blocks
|`cp_status_x` |Illegal bin: X in the `LSU` read data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` or `DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS` after reset. |`LSU` read data |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable; X check needs the 4-state VCS build

====== `sep_fault_source_ack_cg`

*Description*: Clear of the DMA and peripheral bridge fault latches, and the source 43 OR.

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Tool*: VCS covergroup on `u_sep_fcov`. Verilator compiles no covergroup.

*Owning tests*: `sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test`

*Specification*: `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc` (section "DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS"); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section "SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map")

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`x_dma_clr` |Clear write {1, 0} crossed with latch state {both set, host only, reg only}. Cells: write 0 with reg only (stays set); write 0 with both set (stays); write 1 with both set (both clear); write 1 with host only (reg path stays 0). `ignore_bins`: write 1 with reg only (the clear of `reg_path_err` is graded by `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`; here it is a control). |`LSU` write data of `DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR`, `LSU` read data of `DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS`, `VEC[40]`, `VEC[41]`, `RESP` (for the cells with the host path set) |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` (CHK-DMA-CLR) |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run writes 0 with reg only, and sets the host path through the added hook `RESP` for the three cells with the host path set
|`cp_clr_read0` |DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR and PERIPH_BUS_ERR_CLEAR read 0 (2 bins). |`LSU` read data |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable
|`x_clear_matrix` |Clear bit i (0-6) crossed with status bit j in {hmac, kmac, otbn} latched before the clear. 21 cells: 3 diagonal (bit j clears) and 18 off-diagonal (bit j survives). |`LSU` write data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_CLEAR` bit i, `LSU` read data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` bit j before and after |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every clear bit 0 to 6 is written with all three block bits latched on every run
|`cp_clear_shape` |Single-bit clear; multi-bit mask clear; mask that includes an unlatched bit (state unchanged for that bit). 3 bins. |`LSU` write data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_CLEAR`, `LSU` read data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_or_follow` |Source 43 stays 1 while any bit remains; source 43 drops with the last bit. 2 bins. |`VEC[42]`, `LSU` read data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_retrigger` |A cleared block faults again and latches again; a block that is still set faults again and the other bits keep their state. 2 bins. |`LSU` read data of `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS` |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_dma_clr_alias` |One clear drops both DMA latches and both sources 41 and 42 (1 bin). |`LSU` write data of `DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR`, `VEC[40]`, `VEC[41]`, `RESP` |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` (CHK-DMA-CLR, both-set cell) |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run sets both latches, the host path through the added hook `RESP`
|`cp_race_latch` |A fault in the same cycle as the clear of its own latch still latches: peripheral block hmac, kmac or otbn (3 bins) and `host_path_err` (1 bin). 4 bins. A pair of events that misses the coincidence does not score. Illegal bin: the latch reads 0 after the coincident clear and fault. |`ERRP` (fault pulse and clear pulse in one cycle), `LSU` read data of the status register, `RESP` (for the `host_path_err` bin) |`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` (CHK-RACE) |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the sweep runs on each of the three blocks and on `host_path_err` (through the added hook `RESP`) on every run
|===

*Illegal bins*: `cp_race_latch`: the latch reads 0 after the coincident clear and fault

The `host_path_err` bin of `cp_race_latch` uses the added hook `RESP`. The reset-kind cells of this group (`cp_latch_x_reset_kind`, 4 cells) are defined in the memory, boot and reset section (<<sep-fcov-p2-periph-reset-state>>) and are owned by `sep_cpu_reset_retention_test`. The source 40 mask-only rows have no owning test (<<sep-fcov-mbox-irq-pic-ungraded>>).

The cell 0 and the cell 8 also check the whole-port value 0x00 and 0xFF. No cell holds a port bit index. |`SMC[7:0]`, `MB[k].P0.irqs_q`, `.irqen_q` |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` |executable. |`cp_out_of_extent` |Access at the wrapper extent end 0x10A0_7850 up to 0x10A0_7FFF (inbound aperture of mailbox 7), read and write (2 bins). |`XM` address and response |`sep_mailbox_access_error_inject_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk: the base injection set runs K5 once as a read and once as a write on every run

The filter-closed access (K6) is a control of the owning test and has no cell: the default-block verdict belongs to the fabric section. |`MB[m].P*.error_q`, `LSU`/`XM` response and address |`sep_mailbox_access_error_inject_test` |executable.
