====== `sep_mbox_qualification_cg`

*Description*: The mailbox consequence of the inbound filter verdict: entry type, landing of an admitted and of a blocked write, lock and bypass. The verdict classes (match terms, source ID, response code, default block) are owned by `sep_filter_match_cg` and `sep_filter_default_policy_cg` in the fabric section.

*Class*: executable.

*Tool*: VCS covergroup on `u_sep_fcov`. Verilator compiles no covergroup.

*Owning tests*: `sep_mailbox_filter_type_matrix_test`, `sep_mailbox_debug_bypass_test`

*Specification*: `hw/sys/sep/doc/mailbox.adoc` (section "Mailboxes"); `hw/ip/axi_filter/doc/index.adoc` (section "Match, Then Permit", "Blocked Transactions"); `hw/ip/axi_filter/regs/gen/adoc/filter_ctrl.adoc` (section "FILTER_CONFIG"); `hw/sys/sep/doc/fabric.adoc` (section "Fabric Topology", "Filtering and Protection", "Transaction Routing")

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`cp_type` |Entry class over a mailbox aperture: ISS (src_id 1-15); NS-only (src_id 0, one entry per window); both-NS (src_id 0, two entries, one per NS value). 3 bins. The both-NS class stands for type Regular: an entry always tests prot[1], so "no check" needs two entries. DTEE is reserved and has no bin. |`FILT.filters[f].src_id`, `.allow_ns`, `.entry_enabled` |`sep_mailbox_filter_type_matrix_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable. The owning test programs two entries, one per NS value, for type Regular
|`x_type_landing` |Type (3) crossed with landing {word lands in the FIFO and the line rises, no word and no line}. 5 cells. `ignore_bins`: (Regular, no landing), because Regular has `src_id` 0 and two enabled entries, one per `allow_ns`, so no stated access is blocked. |`FILT.filters[f].src_id`, `.allow_ns`, `.entry_enabled`, `MB[m].FB.push_i`, `MB[m].FB.usage_o`, `VEC[7:0]` |`sep_mailbox_filter_type_matrix_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`x_admit_ch_line` |Admission {admitted, blocked} crossed with inbound channel 0-7 and the line after the quiet window. Required: admitted with line 1; blocked with line 0. 16 required cells. The admitted cell of every channel comes from its admit draw, and the blocked cell of every channel comes from its no-entry leg; both run on every channel on every run. The owning test drains the mailbox and clears `IRQS` before each access, so a blocked write meets an idle line. Illegal bins: blocked with line 1; admitted with line 0. |`FILT.isolate_write`, `XM` address (channel), `VEC[7:0]` |`sep_mailbox_filter_type_matrix_test` |executable. Stimulus: Walk
|`cp_lock` |After the lock and a `src_id` write to the locked entry: a mailbox write with the old source ID is admitted; a mailbox write with the attempted new ID is rejected. 2 bins. The response of the register write to the locked entry is graded by the open `sep_fabric_remap_filter_csr_bank_test` and has no bin. |`FILT.filters[f].locked`, `FILT.filters[f].src_id`, `XM.bresp` |`sep_mailbox_filter_type_matrix_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_isolation` |An admit to mailbox m leaves the outbound STATUS and the inbound line of every other mailbox unchanged (1 bin). |`MB[j].P0.status_q`, `VEC` |`sep_mailbox_filter_type_matrix_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_bypass_accept` |With SEP_DBG set (filter bypass) a write is admitted on each of the 8 mailboxes, including a mailbox with no entry: one FIFO entry is added and the line rises. 8 bins. |`SKIP`, `MB[m].FB.push_i`, `MB[m].FB.usage_o`, `VEC[7:0]` |`sep_mailbox_debug_bypass_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_bypass_pair` |In one leaf the same mailbox holds no entry after the write before the demote and one entry after it (1 bin; CHK-DBG-ACCEPT of the owning test grades the pair on mailbox 3). The responses of the pair belong to the fabric section. |`SKIP`, `MB[m].FB.usage_o`, `MB[m].FB.push_i` |`sep_mailbox_debug_bypass_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|===

*Illegal bins*: `x_admit_ch_line`: blocked with line 1; admitted with line 0

====== `sep_cpu_ctrl_timeout_inert_cg`

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`x_reg_dir` |The 12 registers TIMEOUT_INTERRUPT, 8 counts, ENABLE, CLEAR, MODE crossed with read, write. 24 cells; `ignore_bins` (12 cells): TIMEOUT_INTERRUPT in both directions (a write is not driven), TIMEOUT_COUNT_DMA, TIMEOUT_COUNT_SYS_IN and TIMEOUT_ENABLE in both directions (graded by the open `sep_address_map_test` and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`), and TIMEOUT_CLEAR and TIMEOUT_MODE in both directions (graded by `sep_cpu_ctrl_reg_shape_test`, Phase 3). 12 cells: the six counts that `sep_address_map_test` does not write and read back, read and write. |`LSU` address and direction |`sep_cpu_ctrl_timeout_inert_rand_test` |executable
|`cp_stall_block` |A real stall while counts are 1 and ENABLE is 1, per block: system input, mailbox inbound, mailbox outbound, outbound filter, alias remap, DMA (6 bins); entropy write and entropy read (2 bins). |The stalled master `RREADY` or `BREADY`: `XM` R and B channel ready for system input and mailbox inbound, `LSU` R and B channel ready for mailbox outbound; the request on `smn_outbound_axi_req_o` and `RESP` (the response hold of the outbound responder) for outbound filter, alias remap and DMA; `LSU` R and B channel ready for entropy read and entropy write (an access to the entropy pool `STATUS` register) |`sep_cpu_ctrl_timeout_inert_rand_test` (CHK-STALL-REAL) |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run stalls all eight blocks; the outbound filter, alias remap and DMA stalls use the added hook `RESP`
|`cp_stall_inert` |For each stalled block, TIMEOUT_INTERRUPT reads 0 and the vector equals its baseline after the stall (8 bins, same split). Illegal bin: TIMEOUT_INTERRUPT bit 0 reads 1 or the vector changes. |`LSU` read data, `VEC`, `RESP` (for the three outbound blocks) |`sep_cpu_ctrl_timeout_inert_rand_test` (CHK-TO-INERT) |executable. Stimulus: Walk: every run stalls all eight blocks
|===

*Illegal bins*: `cp_stall_inert`: TIMEOUT_INTERRUPT bit 0 reads 1 or the vector changes

[cols="1,4,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled signal |Owning tests |Class and reachability
|`x_gate` |Cause bit {wtirq, rtirq} crossed with {IRQS, IRQEN} in {(1,0), (1,1), (0,1), (0,0)} and port {outbound, inbound}. 16 cells. The `eirq` cause has no cell here: the owning test never sets it (see Bins not graded), and `cp_err_line` bins the `eirq` gate. The port line is 1 only for (1,1) in at least one bit. Illegal bin: line 1 with no bit having (1,1); line 0 with a (1,1) bit after the quiet window. Samples of outbound mailbox 0 are not counted, because the open mailbox tests grade them there; mailboxes 1 to 7 fill the outbound cells. |`MB[m].P*.irqs_q`, `.irqen_q`, `.irq_o` |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_w1c_ack` |Acknowledge below threshold drops the line; acknowledge while occupancy is still above threshold re-reads 1 and the line stays. Per cause {wtirq, rtirq}. 4 bins. Samples of outbound mailbox 0 are not counted, because the open mailbox tests grade them there. |`MB[m].P*.irqs_q`, `.irq_o`, `.status_q` |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_err_line` |Empty read with IRQEN.eirq clear: line stays low and IRQS bit 2 reads 1; IRQEN.eirq set: line rises; ERROR_FLAGS read: line stays; IRQS.eirq W1C: line drops. 4 bins. |`MB[m].P*.error_q`, `.irqs_q`, `.irq_o` |`sep_mailbox_access_error_inject_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_line_iso` |While one line rises, each of the seven other inbound lines equals OR of `irqs_q` AND `irqen_q` of its own port, the Hamming distance between two consecutive quiet samples of `SMC` equals the number of outbound ports whose `irqs_q` AND `irqen_q` changed (1 bin). `VEC[14]` (KM mailbox) is one of the six masked idle bits. |`VEC`, `SMC`, `MB[*].P*.irqs_q`, `.irqen_q` |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable
|`cp_quiet_glitch` |Illegal bin: a toggle of any mailbox line while `quiet_win` is 1. The window opens after the settle wait of the check cycle (see Sampling), so a line that settles some clocks after the last beat of a push, a pop or an `IRQS` write is outside it. 1 illegal bin plus 1 bin "quiet window observed with a line high". |`VEC[7:0]`, `SMC`, `quiet_win`, mailbox AXI handshakes |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` |executable
|===

*Illegal bins*: `x_gate`: line 1 with no bit having (1,1); line 0 with a (1,1) bit after the quiet window; `cp_quiet_glitch`: a toggle of any mailbox line while `quiet_win` is 1

The width of `smc_mailbox_interrupt_o` (8) is stated by `hw/sys/sep/doc/mailbox.adoc`. It is an elaboration check of the owning test and has no bin.

|`x_clamp` |Above bit behaviour: threshold 8 or more fires at occupancy 8 and not at 7; threshold 7 fires at occupancy 8; threshold 0 fires at occupancy 1. |`x_flush` |Flush kind {write flush, read flush} crossed with port {outbound, inbound}, with the peer FIFO non-empty before the flush. 4 cells. CTRL reads 0 after each. The cell (write flush, outbound) does not count samples of outbound mailbox 0, because the open `sep_axil_mailbox_iface_rand_test` grades `wflush` there. |`MB[m].F*.flush_i`, `.empty_o`, `LSU` read data |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` |executable. Sampled at the falling edge of `sep_reset_n`, with the request handshake before it and no response handshake between them. |`RST.sep_reset_n_i`, `MB[m].F*.push_i`, `.pop_i`, mailbox AXI write to IRQS, `LSU` AW, W, AR, B and R handshakes |`sep_mailbox_reset_clear_test` |executable. Illegal bin: any of the first four false, or no inbound line or no outbound line high (a vacuous reset). |`MB[*].P*.*`, `MB[*].F*.usage_o`, `VEC[7:0]`, `SMC` |`sep_mailbox_reset_clear_test` |executable. Illegal bin: X. |`MB[m].P*.status_q`, `.irqs_q`, `.irqen_q`, `.wirqt_q`, `.rirqt_q`, `.error_q` |`sep_mailbox_reset_clear_test` |executable. Stimulus: reachable; X check needs the 4-state VCS build

[[sep-fcov-mbox-irq-pic-dropped]]
===== Group ownership

Every group of this area has at least one owning test in the verification
plan: `sep_pic_reg_map_cg`, `sep_pic_source_map_cg`,
`sep_pic_enable_priority_cg`, `sep_pic_gateway_cg`, `sep_pic_delivery_cg`,
`sep_pic_no_delivery_cg`, `sep_pic_fabric_isolation_cg`,
`sep_cpu_nonpic_irq_cg`, `sep_nmi_vector_cfg_cg`, `sep_nmi_concurrency_cg`,
`sep_fault_source_report_cg`, `sep_fault_source_ack_cg`,
`sep_mbox_transfer_cg`, `sep_mbox_qualification_cg`,
`sep_cpu_ctrl_timeout_inert_cg`, `sep_mbox_irq_line_cg`,
`sep_mbox_fifo_model_cg`, `sep_mbox_reset_cg`, `sep_pic_alert_cg` and
`sep_dma_host_fault_cg`.

[[sep-fcov-mbox-irq-pic-ungraded]]
===== Bins not graded

No owning test reaches these bins of the groups above. They are not bins of the
Phase 2 contract of this area.

[cols="2,4,3",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Bins not graded |Reason
|`sep_pic_delivery_cg` |Source state at handler return; exit path; re-entry outcome for the four sticky fault sources (8 cells) |The handlers of the owning tests do not re-enter. The specification states no re-entry rule for the level fault sources
|`sep_pic_source_map_cg` |Sources 9 to 11, 13, 15, 16, 17 (positive), 31, 32 and 34 to 40; the SPI event cause of source 14; a pending source 12 or 33 with `meie` and `meipl` 0; the shape of the DMA alert after `ALERT_TEST`; the index of the pending source of a boundary input; dual export of sources 31 and 32; the WDT delivery path; integration-variant bins; request-bus bins for these sources |The specification does not state whether `meip` shows a pending source whose enable or priority is 0 (`el2_pic.adoc`, `meip`: pending status only), nor the shape of the DMA alert, nor the map of the boundary input bits to sources 44 to 255. The owning test logs these values. `INTR_STATE.SPI_EVENT` is a status that follows the `EVENT_ENABLE` level, and the open SPI test grades the event cause. The specification states no release of the Secure DMA interrupt (sources 9 to 11). What source 13 aggregates is open in the specification (`hw/sys/sep/doc/watchdog.adoc`). Which entropy source causes raise source 16 is not stated, and the two specification files disagree on source 39; their read has no bin. Sources 31 and 32 have owners in other areas. Sources 34 to 40 have owning tests outside this area. Source 17 keeps the negative bin in `cp_tied_sources`
|`sep_pic_gateway_cg` |Pending value of an edge-triggered active-low source: inactive edge, active edge, held and cleared (4 bins); the edge/low bin of `cp_type_pol` and the two edge/low cells of `x_srcclass_cell` |`meigwctrl` states "Edge-triggered" and "Active-low" only. The active edge is not stated. The owning test logs the values
|`sep_pic_no_delivery_cg` |15 of the 16 source 0 configurations; AES alert negatives; slotless blocks other than the OTP read and the life-cycle controller read of `cp_slotless_block`; the AES operation (`cp_aes_quiet` of `sep_crypto_intr_cg` in the crypto section owns it); the WDT bite destination |Every write to entry 0 is discarded. An AES alert raises source 33. The bite leg has its own owning test
|`sep_pic_fabric_isolation_cg` |Initiators DMA, CPU DBG, System Interface and CPU IFI; masking-consequence rows of sources 39 to 42; the 16 default-block cells of target, read and write |The owning test drives only the external master and the CPU through firmware reads. The default-block regime is a control of the owning test: DECERR there comes from the default block, and the fabric section grades the default-block verdict
|`sep_cpu_nonpic_irq_cg` |Boundary stimulus 0, 1 and toggled; PIC independence of the pins |The testbench ties both pins to a constant
|`sep_fault_source_report_cg` |Command-integrity cause of the DMA host path; the misaligned-offset cause; the aes, csrng, edn and wdt bits; the DMA response at the reserved host windows and past the mailbox wrapper extent; the destination side of the DMA host-path fault |No legal stimulus drives the integrity cause. The specification does not define which access shape is a misaligned offset. It states no hole of these four blocks. It states response codes only as seen at the inbound AXI port, not for a DMA-master access. `sep_dma_basic_test` grades the destination side
|`sep_fault_source_ack_cg` |Source 40 mask-only rows; source 40 contributor subsets |No owning test drives the source 40 rows
|`sep_mbox_qualification_cg` |Type DTEE; the per-attribute rows for the confidential bit |DTEE is reserved
|`sep_mbox_qualification_cg` |The response by outcome; the source ID value classes; the match regime; the response of the register write to a locked entry |The fabric section groups `sep_filter_match_cg` and `sep_filter_default_policy_cg` own the verdict classes, and the open inbound filter and CSR bank tests grade the rest
|`sep_mbox_fifo_model_cg` |Zero fill of unstrobed lanes that carry non-zero data; the partial push after a full push (two entries, no merge) |The open AXI VIP drives unstrobed lanes to zero (see the Blocked features of the verification plan). `sep_mailbox_datapath_walk_test` grades the partial push
|`sep_cpu_ctrl_timeout_inert_cg` |Reset value and read-back shape of the timeout registers except the six counts of `x_reg_dir`; writes of `TIMEOUT_CLEAR` and `TIMEOUT_MODE` |`sep_address_map_test` and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test` (open) and `sep_cpu_ctrl_reg_shape_test` (Phase 3) grade them
|`sep_mbox_irq_line_cg` |`x_gate` cells of the `eirq` cause (8 cells: {IRQS, IRQEN} x port) |`sep_mailbox_fifo_model_rand_test` pushes only below occupancy 8 and pops only above occupancy 0, so no access sets `eirq` (`axil_mailbox.adoc`, `IRQS.eirq`: error on a full-FIFO write or an empty-FIFO read). `cp_err_line` of `sep_mailbox_access_error_inject_test` bins the `eirq` gate
|`sep_mbox_transfer_cg` |Mailbox `k` to port bit `j` of `smc_mailbox_interrupt_o` (64 cells) |The specification states no map from mailbox to port bit (Blocked features of the verification plan). `x_out_count` grades the number of high bits
|`sep_mbox_reset_cg` |The `locked` bit and `entry_enabled` of the filter entry after the reset; the rewrite of the entry after the reset |The specification states the SEP reset of the ISS source ID only. The reset source of the lock and of `entry_enabled` is open (Blocked features of the verification plan)
|`sep_mbox_transfer_cg` |Aperture isolation by aiming at another agent's space |`cp_isolation` of `sep_mbox_qualification_cg` grades it
|`sep_nmi_vector_cfg_cg` |Bin "vector written, lock clear" at bark enable; value classes DCCM, reserved CPU tail and other; the write shape of bit 0 and of the lock |The owning test has no stimulus leg for the first. It writes executable vectors only, which bars the second. `sep_cpu_ctrl_reg_shape_test` (Phase 3) owns the third
|===

===== Unreachable scenarios

[cols="3,4",options="header"]
|===
|Scenario or bin |Reason
|Timer and software pin driven 0, 1 and toggled (6 cells) |The testbench ties both pins to a constant
|15 of 16 configurations of source 0 |Entry 0 discards every write (`hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc`, section "Software Handling")
|Vector probe bits above 42 |The probe has 43 bits
|Hole cause for aes, csrng, edn and wdt |These blocks have no stated hole
|Command-integrity cause of the DMA host path |No legal stimulus drives it
|AES alert "leaves the vector unchanged" |An AES alert raises source 33
|Type Regular as a distinct bin with one entry per aperture |An entry always tests `prot[1]` (`hw/ip/axi_filter/doc/index.adoc`)
|Type Regular with verdict rejected |Regular has `src_id` 0 and two enabled entries, one per `allow_ns`, so it admits both `AxPROT[1]` values
|NMI programming order "vector written, lock clear" |The owning test has no leg for it
|Full ICCM fetch address of an arbitrary handler from the TCM tap |The tap shows a bank 0 row address only
|Source 13 delivery |What source 13 aggregates is open in the specification
|===

[[sep-fcov-p2-spi-dma]]
==== SPI and DMA groups

This section names the dense groups of the SPI host and the secure DMA. A bin
of a Phase 1 group that is in `sep_fcov.sv` is marked existing. A coverpoint
shows that an owning test drives its scenario. Bins come from the
specification and the register description only. Every coverpoint samples DUT
signals. No coverpoint reads log text or a scoreboard count. The groups are VCS
covergroups in `hw/sys/sep/dv/cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` (`ifndef VERILATOR`).
Verilator compiles no covergroup, so only a VCS run scores them. Feature
checkers stay in the tests: a bin is an interface event and not a golden
comparison.

`sep_dma_containment_cg` is executable only through `cp_dma_lock_recovery` of
the memory, boot and reset section (<<sep-fcov-parked>>). A coverpoint whose status ends in
`seed-dependent` is graded on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand>>). The
cells that the specification leaves open are in
<<sep-fcov-spi-dma-not-graded>>, and the holes are in <<sep-fcov-spi-dma-holes>>.

===== Conventions

[cols="1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Item |Rule
|Tool |VCS only. The tests also run on Verilator, which scores no coverage.
|Sampler |The existing `u_sep_fcov` sampler reads the CPU LSU request bus: register writes and reads from the `s_axi` stub and from firmware. It also reads the pad ports, the DMA done interrupt and the reset ports. Those inputs are approved. Every other input is read-only and is an added tap (<<sep-fcov-spi-dma-sampled>>). No input adds a force or a DUT port.
|Latch |A register field marked "latched" is the field of the last write of that register that the sampler saw (`wr_ev`, `wr_data`). The latch lives in the sampler and reads no DUT register. It starts at the register description reset value after each release of `sep_reset_n_i`.
|Gate |A gate is a sampler-side condition that must hold before a bin scores. A gate is built from sampled signals. A gate that compares a pad count with a register field makes the bin score only when the DUT behaves as the specification states. A gate never replaces a checker.
|Reset gate |The SPI host and the DMA reset on `sep_reset_n` (`reset_controller.adoc`, reset logic). A bin that reads state after a reset tests `sep_reset_n_i === 1`, not `rst_ni`.
|Single access |The sampler pairs one address with one data beat. An owning test does not overlap register accesses on the SPI or DMA window. A wide 8-byte write is one transaction and is decoded from `w_data_q` and `w_strb_q`.
|Randomized values |A `*_rand_cg` group samples values that the owning test draws from its seed. A coverpoint whose status ends in `, seed-dependent` depends on the seed: one regression can leave a cell empty, and the cell closes on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand>>). A coverpoint of a deterministic group that records a drawn value carries the same status. Every other coverpoint fills in one VCS `all --cov` regression.
|===

[[sep-fcov-spi-dma-status]]
===== Status codes

[cols="1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Code |Meaning
|executable; existing bin |The bin exists in `sep_fcov.sv` (Phase 1 groups only). Every sampled input is approved. The owning test adds hits.
|executable |Every sampled input is approved or an added tap. The cell fills in one VCS `all --cov` regression of the owning test.
|executable in part. Blocked: `b` needs `v` |The bins `b` need a value `v` that the register description does not state, so they are blocked. The other bins are executable.
|parked: needs `v` |Every bin of the coverpoint needs a value `v` that the register description does not state. Every bin is blocked, so the coverpoint is parked (<<sep-fcov-parked>>) and counts as no coverage.
|executable, seed-dependent |Every sampled input is approved or an added tap. At least one cell depends on a value that the owning test draws from its seed, so one regression can leave it empty. The coverpoint is graded on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand>>).
|Hole |No stimulus reaches the bin. A hole is listed in <<sep-fcov-spi-dma-holes>> and is not a coverpoint.
|Not graded |The specification does not define the value of the bin, or no stimulus can reach it. The bin is not in a group. It is listed in <<sep-fcov-spi-dma-not-graded>>.
|===

===== Citation key

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Short name |Path
|`spi.adoc`, `dma.adoc`, `cpu.adoc`, `fabric.adoc`, `interrupts.adoc`, `reset_controller.adoc`, `port_table.adoc`, `rom.adoc` |`hw/sys/sep/doc/<name>`
|`spi_controller.adoc` |`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/spi_controller/regs/gen/adoc/spi_controller.adoc`
|`spi_controller.rdl` |`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/spi_controller/regs/spi_controller.rdl`
|`secure_dma.adoc` |`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/secure_dma/regs/gen/adoc/secure_dma.adoc`
|`dma.hjson` |`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/dma/data/dma.hjson`
|===

A citation names the file and the section, register or field.

[[sep-fcov-spi-dma-sampled]]
===== Sampled signals

All hierarchical names are relative to `sep_uvm_top`. Two prefixes shorten the
table: `spi_host` is `u_dut.u_sep.u_sep_io.u_sep_ot_spi_wrap.u_spi_host` and
`dma_core` is `u_dut.u_sep.u_sep_dma_wrap.u_secure_dma`. Status `added tap`: a
read-only reference that the owning test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH
observation-probe list.

[cols="2,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Signal |Meaning |Status
|`wr_ev`, `rd_ev` |Sampler register write and register read events (OKAY response, one outstanding access) |approved (existing sampler)
|`aw_addr_q`, `ar_addr_q`, `w_data_q`, `w_strb_q` |Sampler address and full 64-bit data beat |approved (existing sampler)
|`wr_data`, `wr_strb`, `rd_data` |Sampler register data (half chosen by address bit 2) |approved (existing sampler)
|`spi_cs_n_i`, `spi_sck_i`, `spi_mosi_i` |Sampler ports on the SPI pads: chip select, shift clock, `sd[0]` |approved (existing sampler)
|`irq_dma_done_i` |Sampler port, DMA done interrupt |approved (existing sampler)
|`rst_ni`, `cpu_reset_n_i`, `sep_reset_n_i` |Sampler reset ports |approved (existing sampler)
|`SpiBase`, `SpiEnd` |Sampler SPI register window |approved (existing sampler)
|`dma_go`, `dma_opcode_w`, `dma_total_q` |Sampler decode of a `CONTROL` write and the latched `TOTAL_DATA_SIZE` |approved (existing sampler)
|`sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` index 8, 9, 10 and 13 |PIC sources 9, 10, 11 and 14: DMA done, DMA chunk done, DMA error, SPI (index = PIC source ID minus 1) |approved probe (`tb_top.sv`), read through its own sampler port
|`dma_busy_probe_o`, `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o`, `spi_tx_qd_probe_o` |DMA `STATUS.BUSY`, the SPI trigger at the DMA input, the SPI TX queue depth |approved probe (`SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`), read through its own sampler port
|`u_dut.u_sep.dma_axi_req` |DMA master AXI request after the alias remap (the sampler side of `dma_axi_req_probe_o`) |added tap
|`spi_host.active`, `tx_stall`, `rx_stall`, `tx_qd`, `rx_qd`, `cmd_qd` |SPI host activity, stall flags and queue depths |added tap
|`spi_host.tx_wm`, `tx_empty`, `tx_full`, `rx_wm`, `rx_empty`, `rx_full` |SPI host FIFO flags |added tap
|`spi_host.command_busy` |READY is the inverse |added tap
|`spi_host.reg2hw.control.{tx_watermark,rx_watermark}.q` |SPI `CONTROL` watermark fields |added tap
|`spi_host.reg2hw.error_status.{accessinval,csidinval,cmdinval,underflow,overflow,cmdbusy}.q`, `spi_host.reg2hw.error_enable.{csidinval,cmdinval,underflow,overflow,cmdbusy}.q` |SPI error status and enable |added tap
|`spi_host.reg2hw.intr_state.{error,spi_event}.q`, `spi_host.reg2hw.intr_enable.{error,spi_event}.q`, `spi_host.reg2hw.event_enable.{idle,ready,txwm,rxwm,txempty,rxfull}.q` |SPI interrupt and event registers |added tap
|`dma_core.reg2hw.status.{busy,done,chunk_done,error,sha2_digest_valid}.q` |DMA `STATUS` bits (the sampler side of `dma_status_probe_o`) |added tap
|`dma_core.reg2hw.{enabled_memory_range_base,enabled_memory_range_limit,range_valid,range_regwen}.q` |DMA range registers |added tap
|`dma_core.reg2hw.intr_state.{dma_done,dma_chunk_done,dma_error}.q` |DMA interrupt state |added tap
|`dma_core.reg2hw.control.abort.q` |DMA abort request |added tap
|`dma_core.u_secure_dma_reg.status_aborted_qs` |DMA `STATUS.ABORTED` (no `reg2hw.status.aborted` exists) |added tap
|`dma_core.u_secure_dma_reg.error_code_{src_addr,dst_addr,opcode,size,bus,base_limit,range_valid,asid}_error_qs` |DMA `ERROR_CODE` bits (no `reg2hw.error_code` exists) |added tap
|`dma_core.u_secure_dma_reg.sha2_digest_{0..15}_qs` |DMA digest registers |added tap
|===

The register fields that a coverpoint reads as "latched" (the `CONFIGOPTS`,
`CONTROL`, `COMMAND`, `SRC_CONFIG`, `DST_CONFIG`, `TRANSFER_WIDTH`,
`TOTAL_DATA_SIZE`, `CHUNK_DATA_SIZE`, `SRC_ADDR_LO`, `DST_ADDR_LO`,
`INTR_ENABLE`, `RANGE_VALID` fields) come from the register write decode and
need no row above. The sampler takes the added taps read-only. It adds no
force and no DUT port.

====== `sep_spi_fifo_rand_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Tool*: VCS. *Crosses*: `cp_txwm`, `cp_rxwm` and `cp_stall_x_speed`. *Illegal bins*: none. The owning test runs every graded TX and RX watermark class on every run, so `cp_txwm` and `cp_rxwm` are not seed-dependent. It draws one `SPEED` per seed for the stall legs, so `cp_stall_x_speed` is graded on a merge of regressions.

[cols="2,4,4,3,1,2",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Sampled DUT signal and gate |Owning tests |Status |Specification
|`cp_txwm` |`above_depth` (`TXWM` stays 1) = 1 bin. The classes `one`, `mid` and `at_depth` x crossing are graded by the open tests `sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test` and `sep_spi_ot_dma_tx_test`; the owning test runs them as controls (`OBS-SPI-WM`), so they are not bins. `zero` x crossing is not a bin |`wr_ev`, `spi_host.reg2hw.control.tx_watermark.q`, `spi_host.tx_wm`, `spi_host.tx_qd`. The class comes from `TX_WATERMARK` against the latched depth. The crossing comes from the edges of `tx_wm` |`sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test` |executable |`spi_controller.adoc`, `CONTROL.TX_WATERMARK`, `STATUS.TXWM`
|`cp_rxwm` |Class {`zero`, `one`, `mid`} x crossing {`into`, `out`}, less `zero` x `out` = 5 bins. `zero` x `out` is not a bin (<<sep-fcov-spi-dma-not-graded>>) |`wr_ev`, `spi_host.reg2hw.control.rx_watermark.q`, `spi_host.rx_wm`, `spi_host.rx_qd`. The class comes from `RX_WATERMARK`. The crossing comes from the edges of `rx_wm`: `into` is a rising edge, and `out` is a falling edge at an `RXDATA` read. CHK-SPI-WM grades `RXWM` = 0 at `RXQD` equal to the watermark minus 1 in the drain of the `one` and `mid` classes. Equality is not graded |`sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test` |executable |`spi_controller.adoc`, `CONTROL.RX_WATERMARK`, `STATUS.RXWM`
|`cp_stall_x_speed` |{`tx_stall`, `rx_stall`} x `SPEED` {0, 1, 2} = 6 cells |`spi_host.tx_stall`, `spi_host.rx_stall`, latched `COMMAND.SPEED`. Rising edge of the stall flag with the `SPEED` of the active segment |`sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test` |executable, seed-dependent |`spi_controller.adoc`, `STATUS.TXSTALL`, `STATUS.RXSTALL`, `COMMAND.SPEED`
|===

The bins `enable0_no_assert` and `held_while_condition` describe a constant `SPI_EVENT`, so they sample at each `INTR_STATE` read: `enable0_no_assert` scores when a source is high, no selected source is high and `SPI_EVENT` reads 0; `held_while_condition` scores when `SPI_EVENT` reads 1 and a selected source has stayed high since the `SPI_EVENT` rise. CHK-SPI-EVENT grades both at the state reads |`sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test` |executable |`spi.adoc`, Features (SPI event follows the selected sources); `spi_controller.adoc`, `EVENT_ENABLE`
|`cp_intr_test` |`error_force` (write 1 to `INTR_TEST` bit 0), `error_release` (write 1 to `INTR_STATE` bit 0 after a force). The `SPI_EVENT` force (write 1 to `INTR_TEST` bit 1) is not a bin: the open test grades it, and the owning entry does not write it in a graded leg |`wr_ev`, `wr_data`, `aw_addr_q`. Sample at the `INTR_TEST` and `INTR_STATE` writes |`sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test` |executable |`spi_controller.adoc`, `INTR_STATE`, `INTR_TEST`

|`cp_go_lifecycle` |`normal_set`, `normal_autoclear`, `handshake_set`, `handshake_not_autoclear`, `handshake_fw_clear` |`dma_core.reg2hw.control.go.q`, `dma_core.reg2hw.status.done.q`, `rd_ev`, `ar_addr_q`, `u_dut.u_sep.dma_axi_req`. Edges of `CONTROL.GO` and `STATUS.DONE` for `normal_set`, `normal_autoclear`, `handshake_set` and `handshake_fw_clear`. In handshake mode `GO` keeps its value and the specification states no `DONE`, so `handshake_not_autoclear` samples at each `CONTROL` read with the latched `HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE` = 1, and scores when `go.q` is 1 after the DMA has issued TOTAL_DATA_SIZE divided by the transfer width write beats since the GO rise. CHK-HS-GO grades it |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` (`normal_set`, `normal_autoclear`), `sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test` (the three handshake bins) |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `CONTROL.GO`
|`cp_busy_phase` |`idle` (`STATUS.BUSY` = 0 and `CONTROL.GO` = 0), `in_progress` (`STATUS.BUSY` = 1 with `HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE` = 0), `between_chunks` (`CHUNK_DONE` rises while `DONE` = 0). The bins `configured_go_clear` and `awaiting_handshake` are in <<sep-fcov-spi-dma-not-graded>> |`dma_core.reg2hw.status.{busy,done,chunk_done}.q`, `dma_core.reg2hw.control.go.q`, latched `CONTROL.HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE` |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.BUSY`
|`cp_done_set` |`normal_single`, `final_chunk_of_multi` |`dma_core.reg2hw.status.done.q`, latched `TOTAL_DATA_SIZE` and `CHUNK_DATA_SIZE`. Rising edge of `STATUS.DONE` with the chunk class from the sizes |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.DONE`
|`cp_done_clear_path` |`hw_autoclear_on_new_transfer` |`dma_core.reg2hw.status.done.q`, `wr_ev`. `DONE` falls after a GO write with no write of 1 to `STATUS.DONE` since the previous `DONE` rise. A `STATUS` write that sets only `CHUNK_DONE`, as the owner does before each GO of Leg B, does not block the bin. CHK-DMA-DONE-CLR grades it |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.DONE`
|`cp_chunk_done_class` |`single_chunk_not_raised`, `multi_chunk_raised`, `handshake_not_raised` |`dma_core.reg2hw.status.chunk_done.q`, latched `TOTAL_DATA_SIZE`, `CHUNK_DATA_SIZE`, `CONTROL.HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE`. Rising edge of `CHUNK_DONE` and the class at the transfer end (the `DONE` rise) for `single_chunk_not_raised` and `multi_chunk_raised`. A handshake run has no `DONE` that the specification states, so `handshake_not_raised` samples at the `CONTROL` read that scores `cp_go_lifecycle` bin `handshake_not_autoclear` (`rd_ev`, `ar_addr_q`), and scores when `chunk_done.q` has not risen since the GO rise. CHK-HS-NOCHUNK grades it |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` (`single_chunk_not_raised`, `multi_chunk_raised`), `sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test` (`handshake_not_raised`) |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.CHUNK_DONE`; `dma.adoc`, Completion Reporting
|`cp_chunk_done_clear` |`hw_clear_at_next_chunk_start` |`dma_core.reg2hw.status.chunk_done.q`, `wr_ev`. Falling edge of `CHUNK_DONE` before the `DONE` rise with no `STATUS` write since the `CHUNK_DONE` rise. The re-GO rule of the owner writes no `STATUS` bit, so under both chunk readings the fall comes from the hardware. CHK-DMA-CHUNKRISE grades it |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.CHUNK_DONE`
|`cp_chunk_decomp` |{`single_chunk`, `multi_exact`} x `normal` x `WRAP` {0, 1} = 4 cells |Latched `SRC_CONFIG.wrap`, `DST_CONFIG.wrap`, `TOTAL_DATA_SIZE`, `CHUNK_DATA_SIZE`, `CONTROL.HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE`. At the rise of `CONTROL.GO` |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test` |executable |`secure_dma.adoc`, `CHUNK_DATA_SIZE`, `SRC_CONFIG.WRAP`
|===

===== Randomized-value groups

The `_rand_cg` groups below record values that a test draws from its seed.
The Phase 1 group `sep_dma_rand_cg` gains the points listed above. A coverpoint
whose status ends in `seed-dependent` is graded on a merge of regressions
(<<sep-fcov-rand>>), and no claim says that one regression fills it.
The other coverpoints of these groups fill on every run of their owners.

[cols="1,1,2",options="header"]
|===
|Covergroup |Owner |Coverpoint and bins
|`sep_spi_seg_rand_cg` |`sep_spi_pad_speed_dir_matrix_test` |`cp_len_x_kind`, `cp_csaat_x_dir`, `cp_clkdiv_x_speed`
|`sep_spi_pad_rand_cg` |`sep_spi_pad_timing_cfg_rand_test` |`cp_mode8`, `cp_mode_x_dir`, `cp_clkdiv_class`, `cp_csn_class`, `cp_idle_level`, `cp_cpha_edge`
|`sep_spi_fifo_rand_cg` |`sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test` |`cp_txwm`, `cp_rxwm`, `cp_stall_x_speed`
|`sep_spi_misc_rand_cg` |`sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test`, `sep_spi_cold_reset_values_rand_test` |`cp_event_src_x_en_x_cond`, `cp_intr_en_x_state`, `cp_clear_form`, `cp_swrst_midseg`, `cp_rst_entry_midseg`
|`sep_dma_xfer_rand_cg` |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test`, `sep_dma_abort_recovery_rand_test` |`cp_mode_x_width`, `cp_mode_x_chunkcount`, `cp_wrap_independence`, `cp_abort_phase`
|`sep_dma_rand_cg` (added points) |`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test`, `sep_dma_irq_error_lock_rand_test` |`cp_len` bins `b4` to `b16384`, `cp_irq_x_enable`
|===

===== Reachability notes

[cols="2,4",options="header"]
|===
|Point |Condition
|`sep_spi_fifo_rand_cg.cp_txwm` (`above_depth`) |`TX_WATERMARK` has 8 bits. If the TX depth is 255, depth + 1 is not writable and the owning test skips it, so the cell is a hole for that depth. For any other depth the cell fills on every run
|`sep_dma_xfer_rand_cg.cp_abort_phase` |The owning test runs a mid-chunk abort on every seed and draws the class of a second abort. It repeats a run until the beat count at the ABORT write, read on `dma_axi_req_probe_o`, lies in the class of the run. The three drawn classes close on a merge of regressions
|`sep_spi_seg_rand_cg.cp_len_x_kind` (`max` x `dummy`) |`LEN` = 0xFFFFF at `CLKDIV` 0 takes 2,097,152 core clocks. The owning test draws it with a weight of 1 in 4, so one regression of four seeds leaves the cell empty with a probability of about 32 % ((3/4)^4). The cell closes on a merge of regressions
|Coverpoints marked `parked: needs ...` |The bins need a value that the register description does not state. The coverpoint scores nothing
|Rx bit content at the pad (not a bin) |The TB drives one response lane and the specification does not state which line carries the Rx bit, so no test grades the Rx content. No coverage bin depends on it
|===

[[sep-fcov-spi-dma-dropped]]
===== Groups that no owning test hits

Each group of this section has at least one coverpoint with an owning test: `sep_spi_command_segment_cg`, `sep_spi_seg_rand_cg`,
`sep_spi_illegal_access_cg`, `sep_spi_pad_interface_cg`, `sep_spi_pad_rand_cg`,
`sep_spi_fifo_status_cg`, `sep_spi_fifo_rand_cg`, `sep_spi_interrupt_cg`,
`sep_spi_misc_rand_cg`, `sep_spi_error_status_cg`, `sep_periph_reset_state_cg`,
`sep_spi_dma_use_case_cg`, `sep_dma_transfer_cg`, `sep_dma_xfer_rand_cg`,
`sep_dma_endpoint_cg`, `sep_dma_completion_cg`, `sep_dma_range_config_cg`,
`sep_dma_hash_cg`, `sep_dma_config_fault_cg`, `sep_dma_error_terminal_cg` and
`sep_dma_containment_cg`.

The bins and cells below belong to those groups and are not in the plan,
because no owning test hits them.

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Bins and cells not in the plan
|`sep_spi_command_segment_cg` |Segment role axis (opcode and address as separate roles; see <<sep-fcov-spi-dma-holes>>).
|`sep_spi_seg_rand_cg` |Length class `max` x data kind in `cp_len_x_kind` (a 1 MiB data segment is not driven).
|`sep_spi_illegal_access_cg` |`TXDATA` write and `RXDATA` read while `SPIEN` = 0 (2 cells). `SPIEN` cleared mid-segment (1).
|`sep_spi_pad_interface_cg` |`CLKDIV` 0xFFFF class and its x `SPEED` cells. Sampling mode x `bidirectional` for the 7 modes other than 000. `CSNIDLE` x spacing x `CSAAT` cells with `CSAAT` = 1 or single-command spacing (9 of 12). `OUTPUT_EN` x line group x active cells with `OUTPUT_EN` = 0. The pad enable vector at idle against `OUTPUT_EN` (the specification names no member or polarity, and the open interrupt test grades the `OUTPUT_EN` read-back). Sd output and input enable x segment class (16). Direction turnaround (2).
|`sep_spi_fifo_status_cg` |RX back-to-back drain form (3 cells). Partial final word (its packing depends on the byte order, which the specification leaves open). RX threshold at depth (`RX_WATERMARK` cannot exceed the depth).
|`sep_spi_interrupt_cg` |Externally driven interrupt (hole). `INTR_TEST` with the real condition present (4 cells). `INTR_ENABLE` reserved bits 31:2. The reserved-bit read-back of `INTR_ENABLE` (the register description states no read value).
|`sep_periph_reset_state_cg` |SPI reset values after an idle reset and the non-zero and safe-idle bins (left to the reset tests). DMA reset values, non-zero values and fail-secure defaults (left to the reset groups). `SW_RST` domains CDC FIFOs and shift register (hole). `SW_RST` entry condition `block idle`. `SW_RST` released with a non-empty FIFO (3): the test waits for both FIFOs to be empty.
|`sep_spi_dma_use_case_cg` |Sensor use case. Flash class x4 use. Short frequent frames. Boot path destinations (the open ROM boot tests run SRAM then ICCM). 23 of the 24 matrix cells. Boot image size and traffic shape. Image smaller than one chunk. Local peripheral inventory (left to the fabric groups).
|`sep_dma_transfer_cg` |Transfer mode memory-to-peripheral. Peripheral streaming mirror direction and 6 further cells. Transfer mode x hashing x chunking: 9 cells beyond the 2 listed and the `cp_peripheral_stream` cell.
|`sep_dma_endpoint_cg` |All endpoint cells beyond SRAM to SRAM and SPI to SRAM (left to the fabric groups). Alias form cells. CSR aperture cells.
|`sep_dma_completion_cg` |Handshake cells of `cp_chunk_decomp`. `DONE` software clear (graded by the open DMA test `sep_dma_basic_test`). `CHUNK_DONE` software clear.
|`sep_dma_range_config_cg` |`RANGE_VALID` = 0 bin. `LIMIT` = `BASE` bin. Clearing-source configuration for source 0 (no owning test drives the interrupt clear write) and for sources 1 to 10 (hole) and the tuple and concurrency cells for sources 1 to 10.
|`sep_spi_error_status_cg` |`ACCESSINVAL` raised and cleared (hole). Two or more classes by one offending access. `RXDATA` read on an empty FIFO in the back-to-back and active forms. Partial byte-enable `TXDATA` write. Whole-beat zero strobe cells (5 cells; the AXI master emits no beat without strobes).
|`sep_dma_config_fault_cg` |Misalignment cells (12): the tests use 4-aligned addresses. Short final chunk and `CHUNK_DATA_SIZE` greater than `TOTAL_DATA_SIZE` (the specification does not state the behavior). `INITIAL_TRANSFER` misuse (2).
|`sep_dma_error_terminal_cg` |Error classes `BUS`, `RANGE_VALID`, `BASE_LIMIT`, `OPCODE`, `ASID` and multi-error. Abort phases idle, stalled on a peripheral and awaiting a handshake. SoC-interface abort class. `DONE` and `SHA2_DIGEST_VALID` must-not-set cells. `BUSY` and `DONE` after an error or an abort (the specification states no rule). `OPCODE` exclusions.
|`sep_dma_containment_cg` |The lock while the DMA is busy (the specification does not name it). Straddling transfers (2 cells). `RANGE_VALID_ERROR` and `BASE_LIMIT_ERROR` cells. Interrupt-clearing target cells (33). Illegal endpoint cells (5). CSR aperture fault cells (12).
|===

[[sep-fcov-spi-dma-not-graded]]
===== Bins not graded

The specification and the register description do not define the value of
these bins, or no stimulus can reach them. They are not in a group. A stimulus
that touches them in a test is logged and is not scored.

[cols="2,3,4",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Bin or coverpoint |Reason
|`sep_spi_fifo_rand_cg` |`cp_rxwm_reset` (`RX_WATERMARK` at the reset value 0x7F, crossing into and out of `RXWM`) |A crossing at 0x7F needs an RX depth above 127. The specification does not state the RX depth (`spi.adoc`, Features; `spi_controller.adoc`, `CONTROL.RX_WATERMARK`).
|`sep_spi_fifo_rand_cg` |`cp_rxwm` cell `zero` x `out` |At `RX_WATERMARK` 0 the out crossing is at `RXQD` equal to the watermark, where `CONTROL.RX_WATERMARK` (the depth "reaches" the watermark) and `STATUS.RXWM` (the depth "exceeds" it) differ (`spi_controller.adoc`).
|`sep_spi_fifo_status_cg` |`cp_depth_counter` cells {`TXQD`, `RXQD`} x `ACTIVE` = 1 x {`one`, `mid`} |While `ACTIVE` = 1, `RXQD` may be an underestimate and `TXQD` may be an overestimate (`spi_controller.adoc`, `STATUS.RXQD`, `STATUS.TXQD`), so no value is stated. The owning tests compare `TXQD` and `RXQD` only with `ACTIVE` = 0, and the stall checks grade the `zero` and `max` cells.
|`sep_spi_interrupt_cg` |`cp_intr_test` bin `event_release` (write 0 to `INTR_TEST` bit 1) |The release of a forced `SPI_EVENT` is not stated (`spi_controller.adoc`, `INTR_TEST`).
|`sep_spi_misc_rand_cg` |`cp_masked_kind` (a kind injected with its `ERROR_ENABLE` bit clear) |The register description does not state whether a masked error sets its status bit or `INTR_STATE.ERROR` (`spi_controller.adoc`, `ERROR_ENABLE`, `ERROR_STATUS`).
|`sep_spi_error_status_cg` |`cp_csid_out_of_range` bin `csid_one` (CSID = 1) |`spi_host.hjson` (`ERROR_ENABLE.CSIDINVAL`) raises the error when CSID exceeds NumCS, and CSID = 1 does not exceed NumCS = 1, while the CSID field text selects `cio_csb_o[CSID]` and `sep_io` has one chip select (`spi.adoc`, Features). The owning test logs the cell as `OBS-SPI-CSID1 LOG`.
|`sep_spi_error_status_cg` |`cp_csid_out_of_range` bin `csid_max` (CSID = 0xFFFF_FFFF) |The open test `sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test` drives the value with the graded window closed (<<sep-fcov-graded-window>>), and the owning leg draws v from 2 to 0xFFFF_FFFE.
|`sep_dma_config_fault_cg` |`cp_addr_error_attribution` (`SRC_ADDR_ERROR` and `DST_ADDR_ERROR` for a region outside the range) |The register description does not state which `ERROR_CODE` bit a region outside `ENABLED_MEMORY_RANGE_BASE` to `LIMIT` raises (`secure_dma.adoc`, `ERROR_CODE`).
|`sep_dma_containment_cg` |`cp_out_of_range` (`src_below`, `src_above`, `dst_below`, `dst_above`) |Same reason.
|`sep_dma_error_terminal_cg` |`cp_error_x_aggregate` cells `SRC_ADDR_ERROR` and `DST_ADDR_ERROR` |Same reason.
|`sep_dma_completion_cg` |`cp_busy_phase` bins `configured_go_clear` and `awaiting_handshake` |The specification names no observable state between the configuration writes and `GO` (`secure_dma.adoc`, `CONTROL.GO`). `STATUS.BUSY` "may be set" while a hardware handshake is active and triggered, so no value is stated while the DMA waits for a trigger (`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.BUSY`).
|`sep_dma_completion_cg` |`cp_intr_state_seen` (`dma_done`, `dma_chunk_done`) |`INTR_STATE` with the enable clear and its release are not stated (`secure_dma.adoc`, `INTR_STATE`).
|`sep_dma_completion_cg` |`cp_chunk_done_clear` bin `state_at_final_recorded` |The register description does not state the value of `CHUNK_DONE` at the `DONE` rise (`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.CHUNK_DONE`).
|`sep_dma_hash_cg` |`cp_digest_valid_lifecycle` bin `held_clear_in_accumulation` |The value of `SHA2_DIGEST_VALID` between the transfers of a chain is not stated (`secure_dma.adoc`, `STATUS.SHA2_DIGEST_VALID`).
|`sep_spi_pad_rand_cg` |`cp_cpha_edge` cell `CPHA` = 1 with `FULLCYC` = 1 |The shift edge in `spi_controller.adoc` (`CONFIGOPTS`) differs from the general `CPHA` text. The cell stays in `cp_mode8` as a stimulus cell and has no edge bin.
|`sep_spi_pad_rand_cg` |`cp_csn_class` bin `CSNIDLE` `zero` |The owning test raises the graded `CSNIDLE` until the stated minimum gap exceeds the chip-select-high gap of its control run, and that gap is at least (0 + 1) x (`CLKDIV` + 1) core clocks (`spi_controller.adoc`, `CONFIGOPTS.CSNIDLE`). So a graded run never has `CSNIDLE` 0. The control run with `CSNIDLE` 0 runs with the coverage window closed.
|===

[[sep-fcov-spi-dma-holes]]
===== Holes

A hole stays in the plan so that the gap is visible. It is not a coverpoint.

[cols="1,2,3",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Bin |Reason
|`sep_spi_error_status_cg` |`ACCESSINVAL` raised and cleared |A zero-strobe write completes with OKAY and no TL-UL request (`spi.adoc`, Features). No AXI stimulus reaches the core with zero strobes.
|`sep_periph_reset_state_cg` |`SW_RST` domain CDC FIFOs |No register or specification-named signal shows the CDC clear (`spi_controller.adoc`, `CONTROL.SW_RST`).
|`sep_periph_reset_state_cg` |`SW_RST` domain shift register |Same reason.
|`sep_spi_interrupt_cg` |Externally driven interrupt |The SPI host is inside SEP. Its interrupt is an output member of the pad struct and feeds the PIC internally (`spi.adoc`, Features). No `spi_irq_i` input exists.
|`sep_dma_range_config_cg` |Clearing sources 1 to 10 (44 cells less source 0) |Handshake inputs 1 to 10 are tied low (`dma.adoc`, OCAH Modifications). No stimulus raises them.
|`sep_dma_completion_cg` |Handshake triggered by sources 1 to 10 |Same reason.
|`sep_spi_command_segment_cg` |Segment role instruction and address |The role is a firmware label. The segment fields show only `SPEED`, `DIRECTION`, `LEN` and `CSAAT`.
|===

===== Contract notes

The specification and the register description decide the bins. These points
differ from a plain reading of the feature list.

* `COMMAND.LEN` is 20 bits wide (`spi_controller.adoc`, `COMMAND.LEN`). The maximum is 0xFFFFF. The bin `max` uses 0xFFFFF.
* Every `ERROR_STATUS` bit is RW1C (`spi_controller.adoc`, `ERROR_STATUS`). The expected result of the clear forms follows RW1C.
* `INTR_STATE.ERROR` is a latched event that only a write of 1 clears (`spi.adoc`, Features; `spi_controller.adoc`, `INTR_STATE`). The bins `held_after_status_cleared` and `cleared_by_intr_state_w1c` record this. There is no aggregation-deassert bin.
* `INTR_ENABLE` reserves bits 31:2 (`spi_controller.adoc`, `INTR_ENABLE`).
* `cp_intr_en_x_state` has 16 cells, not 32. The other cells need PIC source 14 high with no enabled state bit, which the gate forbids. Five of the 16 cells are open-test controls, so 11 cells are bins.
* `INTR_STATE.SPI_EVENT` follows the level of the sources that `EVENT_ENABLE` selects (`spi.adoc`, Features). The cell axis is the level of each source.
* `sep_spi_flash_cg` and `sep_dma_completion_route_cg` keep their present contracts.
* `sep_sram_req_o` is a memory request struct. It has no `AxLEN`, `AxSIZE` or master identity, so the single-beat bins sample `u_dut.u_sep.dma_axi_req`, the sampler side of the observation tap `dma_axi_req_probe_o`. The width axis comes from `TRANSFER_WIDTH`, because the specification does not state the `AxSIZE` of a narrow DMA transaction.
* The address-class bins of `cp_src_x_dst_endpoint` and `cp_mode_hash_chunk` come from the latched source and destination address registers, not from a beat.

===== Sampler caveats

* The sampler pairs one outstanding single-beat transaction. A test that overlaps two register accesses scores neither.
* `wr_ev` and `rd_ev` score only OKAY responses. A write with an error response is invisible to them. No SPI or DMA coverpoint needs an error response on the register bus.
* A no-CPU test drives the LSU request bus through the `s_axi` stub. A test that drives the SMN inbound port (`m_axi`) is not seen by the register decode. The owning tests use `s_axi` or firmware.
* The sampler decodes a 4-byte register from the half that address bit 2 selects. A wide write to an 8-byte aligned pair uses `w_data_q` and `w_strb_q` for both halves.
* The existing `in_reset` follows `rst_ni` only. A bin that reads a register after a reset does not use it.

[[sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset]]
==== Memory, boot, reset, WDT and clock groups

The groups cover the memory apertures, the boot release,
the reset sources and scopes, the watchdog and wakeup timers, the clock
domains, the `sep_cpu_ctrl` registers and the CPU debug and trace paths. Each
group is specified as a VCS `covergroup` for `cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` under
`ifndef VERILATOR`. Verilator runs the same tests as checker evidence and
collects nothing. Bins come from the specification and the register
description only. A bin that the specification does not define, or that no
stimulus can reach, is not in a group (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>).

*Rules for every coverpoint*

* The tool is VCS only.
* A coverpoint samples a DUT net, a DUT port, or the AXI response and read data
that the DUT returns. It does not read log text, a checker verdict or a
scoreboard count. A stimulus value that has no DUT net (a drawn offset, a flip
mask) is not a sampled signal.
* A Phase 1 group does not change. A bin that extends a Phase 1 topic is in a
Phase 2 group.
* A reset-edge coverpoint samples in a block that the Phase 1 `in_reset` gate
does not hold off (`always @(negedge rst_ni)` and similar). It still samples only
inside the graded window of an owning test (<<sep-fcov-graded-window>>), so the
power-on reset, the bring-up reset of a leaf and a reset between legs fill no
bin. The Phase 1 `in_reset` gate stays on the bus-decoded coverpoints only.
* A sub-cycle coverpoint compares `$realtime` of two edges. The sampler carries
`` `timescale 1ps/1fs ``, the value of `tb/tb_top.sv`.
* A bus-decoded coverpoint follows the single-outstanding pairing rule of
<<sep-fcov-collection>>. A coverpoint that needs a refused response (SLVERR,
DECERR) uses the read-completion event and a write-completion event. The
OKAY-only read and write events of Phase 1 do not carry it.
* A tolerance (a synchronizer allowance, a window length, a settle time) is a
choice of the environment, and the row says so. The specification gives none.
A bin never takes an expected value from a measurement of the DUT.
* A bin whose notes say "seed-dependent" records a cell that the owning leaf
draws from its seed. It is graded on a merge of regressions, as
<<sep-fcov-rand>> states, and is never claimed from one regression. Every
other bin of the group fills in one VCS `all --cov`.

*Citation tags.* A spec citation is a path relative to the repository root and a
section or register name.

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Tag |Path
|`doc/` |`hw/sys/sep/doc/`
|`regs/` |`hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/` (`regs/blocks/` holds the per-block register references, `regs/memory_map.adoc` the address map)
|`aon_timer.adoc` |`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/aon_timer/regs/gen/adoc/aon_timer.adoc`
|`secure_dma.adoc` |`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/secure_dma/regs/gen/adoc/secure_dma.adoc`
|`SEP_CORE` |The hierarchy `u_dut.u_sep` in `tb/tb_top.sv`
|===

*Notes terms.* The Notes cell of each row starts with the class of the coverpoint (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Code |Meaning
|executable; existing bin |The bin exists in `sep_fcov.sv` (Phase 1 groups only). The owning test adds hits.
|executable |Every bench drive and inject of every bin is approved (in the closed set of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` or in `tb/tb_top.sv`) or an added hook (<<sep-fcov-parked>>). Every sampled input is approved or an added tap (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-paths>>).
|executable in part |Some bins are blocked: a bench drive of those bins is PROPOSED (a drive, force or deposit of a net inside the `sep` module for a purpose other than error injection, outside the closed set of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`), or the bins need a value that the specification does not state. `Blocked:` names the bins and the item. A blocked bin does not score and counts as no coverage.
|parked: <item> |Every bin needs a PROPOSED drive or an unstated value, which the cell names. No bin scores.
|Not graded |The specification does not define the value of the bin, or no stimulus can reach it. The bin is not in a group. It is listed in <<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>.
|===

[[sep-fcov-p2-reset-source]]
===== `sep_reset_source_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: Every reset source, how the primary and always-on resets cross
into their domains, and the boot-release terms. Tool: VCS.

[cols="2,6,4,3,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signals |Owning tests |Notes
|`cp_source_edge` |source {`rst_ni`, `wdt_rst_ni`, `dbg_rstb_i`, `scan_rst_ni`, `jtag_trst_ni`, TAP Test-Logic-Reset} x edge {assert, release} = 12 cells. A cell samples only the edge that the graded stimulus of its owner places: the `rst_ni` fall and rise of the graded pass of `sep_cold_reset_phase_test` (CHK-COLD-DEFAULT, CHK-RST-SYNC), and the `wdt_rst_ni` fall and rise of a WDT_PULSE of the WDT sequence, the overlap leg or the DMA leg of `sep_cpu_reset_retention_test` (CHK-RETAIN, CHK-AGG-SURVIVE, CHK-OVL, CHK-DMA-CPURST), and the `dbg_rstb_i` fall and rise of a DBG_PULSE of the DBG sequence or the overlap leg of the VCS leaf of `sep_cpu_reset_retention_test` (CHK-RETAIN, CHK-AGG-SURVIVE, CHK-OVL), the `scan_rst_ni` fall and rise of the control A pulse and of the leg B pulse of `sep_scan_reset_override_test` (CHK-SCAN-IDLE, CHK-SCAN-REPLACE), and the `jtag_trst_ni` fall and rise and the Test-Logic-Reset entry and exit of the TAP resets of `sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` (CHK-RSTVEC-TAPRST, CHK-TRST-SCOPE). The bring-up reset and the control pass of `sep_cold_reset_phase_test` run with the graded window closed and fill no cell (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>). Test-Logic-Reset is five consecutive `jtag_tms_i` = 1 on `jtag_tck_i` with `jtag_trst_ni` high. Spec: `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Reset Architecture; `doc/reset_controller.adoc`, SEP Reset Controller; `doc/port_table.adoc`, port list; `doc/cpu.adoc`, Reset Vector |None |`rst_ni`, `wdt_rst_ni`, `dbg_rstb_i`, `scan_rst_ni`, `jtag_trst_ni`, `jtag_tms_i`, `jtag_tck_i` |`sep_cold_reset_phase_test` (`rst_ni`); `sep_cpu_reset_retention_test` (`wdt_rst_ni`, `dbg_rstb_i`); `sep_scan_reset_override_test` (`scan_rst_ni`); `sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` (`jtag_trst_ni`, Test-Logic-Reset) |executable. The `rst_ni`, `wdt_rst_ni` and `dbg_rstb_i` cells have approved inputs. The `scan_rst_ni` cells use the `scan_rst_ni` and `test_en_i` drives, and the `jtag_trst_ni` and Test-Logic-Reset cells use the TAP pin drive and the TAP state tracker of <<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-sampler>>; these drives are added hooks. Every owner runs its cells in every leaf
|`cp_rst_release_sync` |phase of the `rst_ni` rise {0-33 %, 33-67 %, 67-100 %} x `sep_reset_n` rise aligned to a `clk_i` rising edge (offset at most 1 ps; the 1 ps is a choice of the environment). Spec: `doc/port_table.adoc`, port list (`rst_ni`); `doc/reset_controller.adoc`, SEP Reset Controller |`sep_reset_n` rises with an offset above 1 ps |`rst_ni`, `clk_i`, `sep_reset_n` |`sep_cold_reset_phase_test` |executable. Seed-dependent: the owning leaf draws the third of its one graded pass, so the three bins fill on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand>>). The control pass releases at 10 percent of the `clk_i` period after an edge and runs with the graded window closed, so it fills no third
|`cp_aon_release_sync` |`clk_wdt_i` phase at the `rst_ni` release {0-33 %, 33-67 %, 67-100 % of one `clk_wdt_i` period} x `aon_rst_n` rise aligned to a `clk_wdt_i` rising edge (offset at most 1 ps). Spec: `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Reset Architecture; `doc/reset_controller.adoc`, SEP Reset Controller |`aon_rst_n` rises with an offset above 1 ps |`rst_ni`, `clk_wdt_i`, `SEP_CORE.u_sep_wdt_wrap.rst_wdt_n` |`sep_cold_reset_phase_test` |executable. The start phase and period of `clk_wdt_i` are an approved cocotb clock drive. Seed-dependent: the owning leaf places its one graded release in the drawn third of the `clk_wdt_i` period, so the bins fill on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand>>)
|`cp_release_order` |arrival of `ext_boot_seq_done_i` as the last release term, after a held window. The held window is `rst_ni` = 1, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` = 1, `ext_boot_seq_done_i` = 0 and `sep_reset_n` = 0 for K consecutive `clk_i` cycles. K is an environment choice from 401 to 514 cycles: above the longest delay after sensing of the H = no epoch (400 cycles) and at most the shortest hold of the H = yes epoch (2 T, with T at least 257 cycles). Bins: {held window of K cycles, `sep_reset_n` rises after the `ext_boot_seq_done_i` rise that ends a held window} = 2 bins. An arrival before the `rst_ni` rise, in the `clk_i` cycle of the `rst_ni` rise, after the `rst_ni` rise and before the `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rise, or within K cycles after the `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rise opens no held window and is not a bin (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>). Spec: `doc/reset_controller.adoc`, SEP Reset Controller; `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Reset Architecture; `doc/port_table.adoc`, port list (`ext_boot_seq_done_i`) |In the interval from a `rst_ni` rise to the next `rst_ni` fall: `sep_reset_n` = 1 while `ext_boot_seq_done_i` = 0 or `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` = 0, with the override off (`jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i` = 0) and no SEC_DIS release. The interval from a `rst_ni` fall to the next `rst_ni` rise is not sampled: `sep_reset_n` falls only after the eFuse-wrapper synchronizer, and the specification states no assertion timing for it (`doc/reset_controller.adoc`) |`ext_boot_seq_done_i`, `rst_ni`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `sep_reset_n`, `jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i` |`sep_boot_release_terms_test` |executable. Only the H = yes epoch (CHK-REL-HOLD) opens a held window, and every leaf runs it, so both bins fill in one regression. The calibration boot runs with the graded window closed
|`cp_scan_bypass` |`scan_rst_ni` {0, 1} x `test_en_i` {0, 1} = 4 cells, sampled at each change of either input. Spec: `doc/port_table.adoc`, port list (`test_en_i`, `scan_rst_ni`); `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Reset Architecture |None |`scan_rst_ni`, `test_en_i` |`sep_scan_reset_override_test` |executable. Control A (CHK-SCAN-IDLE) fills (0, 0), leg B (CHK-SCAN-REPLACE) fills (0, 1) and (1, 1), and the `test_en_i` toggles of the independence steps (CHK-TEST-EN-PINS) fill (1, 0) and (1, 1), in every leaf. `test_en_i` and `scan_rst_ni` are added hooks (top-port drives)
|`cp_scan_net_effect` |net {`sep_reset_n`, `sep_cpu_reset_n`, `ip_rst_n[1]` to `ip_rst_n[6]`, `aon_rst_n`, Key Manager cold conditioner output, Key Manager warm conditioner output} (11 nets) x case {`scan_rst_ni` = 0 with `test_en_i` = 0: the net keeps its value from before the event; `scan_rst_ni` = 0 with `test_en_i` = 1: the net is low at the last `clk_i` rising edge before the `scan_rst_ni` rise} = 22 cells, with a `scan_rst_ni` low width of at least 4 `clk_i` cycles (a choice of the environment), plus two release cells: the rise of `sep_reset_n` and the rise of `aon_rst_n` have an offset above 1 ps to a `clk_i` edge and to a `clk_wdt_i` edge respectively (the 1 ps is a choice of the environment; the synchronizer is bypassed). The specification names a clock for these two nets only, so the rise of the other nets is not a cell. Spec: `doc/reset_controller.adoc`, SEP Reset Controller; `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Reset Architecture |None |`scan_rst_ni`, `test_en_i`, `sep_reset_n`, `sep_cpu_reset_n`, `ip_rst_n[6:1]`, `SEP_CORE.u_sep_wdt_wrap.rst_wdt_n`, `SEP_CORE.u_sep_crypto.u_key_manager_s3c_scan.rst_cold_aasd_n`, `SEP_CORE.u_sep_crypto.u_key_manager_s3c_scan.rst_warm_sync_n`, `clk_i`, `clk_wdt_i` |`sep_scan_reset_override_test` |executable. CHK-SCAN-IDLE fills the `test_en_i` = 0 cells, and CHK-SCAN-REPLACE fills the `test_en_i` = 1 cells and the two release cells, in every leaf. The test releases the Key Manager before the check, because the Key Manager warm net is low while `SW_RESET_N.km_sw_rst_n` = 0
|`cp_jtag_mech` |mechanism {`jtag_trst_ni` assertion, TAP walked into Test-Logic-Reset} x TAP-resident state {at default, holding a programmed value} = 4 cells. The state is read from `dmi_rst_vec` against the Boot ROM base. Spec: `doc/cpu.adoc`, Reset Vector |None |`jtag_trst_ni`, `jtag_tms_i`, `jtag_tck_i`, `SEP_CORE.u_sep_cpu.u_el2_veer_wrapper.dmi_rst_vec` |`sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` |executable. The four TAP reset sub-legs of the owner (CHK-RSTVEC-TAPRST, CHK-TRST-SCOPE) fill the four cells in every leaf. The coverpoint samples the reset-vector register itself, so an inert register shows as an empty cell, which the owning test reports as a specification-versus-build finding
|`cp_mpc_sync_reset` |while `dbg_rstb_i` = 0, `mpc_run_sync` does not follow a toggle of `mpc_reset_run_req_i` (at least one toggle in the window); after the `dbg_rstb_i` release, `mpc_run_sync` equals `mpc_reset_run_req_i` within the two-flop synchronizer latency plus an environment allowance (`doc/cpu.adoc`, CPU: `mpc_reset_run_req_i` passes a two-flop synchronizer) = 2 bins. The bins claim no held value: the spec states that the pulse resets the synchronizer and gives no reset value. Spec: `doc/reset_controller.adoc`, SEP Reset Controller; `doc/cpu.adoc`, Processor Core |None |`dbg_rstb_i`, `mpc_reset_run_req`, `SEP_CORE.u_sep_cpu.mpc_reset_run_req_sync` |`sep_cpu_reset_retention_test` |executable. The VCS full-CPU build has the net; the Verilator stub has none
|===

[[sep-fcov-p2-clk-domain]]
===== `sep_clk_domain_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: A register access that crosses from `clk_i` into an exception
clock domain, and the phase of that clock at the crossing. Tool: VCS.

[cols="2,6,4,3,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signals |Owning tests |Notes
|`cp_domain_crossing` |exception domain {`clk_wdt_i`, `clk_ref_i`, `jtag_tck_i`} against the primary `clk_i` = 3 cells. A cell hits at a completed access that crosses into that domain: a watchdog-window access, a `REFERENCE_COUNTER` access, a TAP shift. The exception clock toggles in the periods before the access. The `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` cell belongs to the crypto and entropy groups. Spec: `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Clock Distribution |None |`clk_i`, `clk_wdt_i`, `clk_ref_i`, `jtag_tck_i`, LSU bus completion |`sep_cold_reset_phase_test` and `sep_timer_clock_contract_test` (the `clk_wdt_i` cell), `sep_timer_clock_contract_test` (the `clk_ref_i` cell), `sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` (the `jtag_tck_i` cell) |executable. The TDR shifts of the redirect and rewrite steps of `sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` (CHK-RSTVEC) fill the `jtag_tck_i` cell, through the TAP pin drive (added hook). The `clk_wdt_i` and `clk_ref_i` cells have approved inputs. The `WKUP_THOLD_LO` reads of the retention routine of `sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` fill no `clk_wdt_i` cell
|`cp_xdomain_phase` |domain {`clk_ref_i`} x phase quartile of the `clk_ref_i` period at the `clk_i` edge that completes a `REFERENCE_COUNTER` read {Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4} = 4 cells. The owning test walks the issue gap of the reads from 0 to k - 1 `clk_i` cycles at the ratio k = 5, so the completing edge takes five positions 0.2 of a period apart and every quartile fills in every leaf. The owning test grades each phase-walk read (CHK-REFCNT-PHASE). Spec: `doc/clk_rst.adoc`, Clock Distribution |None |`clk_i`, `clk_ref_i` |`sep_timer_clock_contract_test` |executable. The start phase and period of `clk_ref_i` are an approved cocotb clock drive
|===

[[sep-fcov-p2-boot-milestone]]
===== `sep_boot_milestone_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: The fuse-sense milestone, the pre-sense window, and the first
instruction fetch after reset. Tool: VCS.

[cols="2,6,4,3,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins and crosses |Illegal bins |Sampled signals |Owning tests |Notes
|`cp_smc_status_follow` |`smc_fuse_sense_done_i` {0, 1} stable for an environment-chosen settle time x `SMC_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` readback {0, 1}: cells (0, 0) and (1, 1). Spec: `regs/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `SMC_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` |Cells (0, 1) and (1, 0) |`smc_fuse_sense_done_i`, LSU read data |`sep_boot_release_terms_test` |executable. The testlist entry `sep_boot_release_terms_test_smc_hold` (the bench plusarg `+sep_smc_fuse_sense_hold`, a time-0 bench configuration that the seed cannot select) fills (0, 0) and the entry `sep_boot_release_terms_test` fills (1, 1), on every regression. The test sends no outbound SMC request, so no outbound-safety cell exists
|`cp_presense_window` |window = `rst_ni` = 1 and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` = 0. The cells are the shadow-map read response {OKAY, SLVERR} x `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` {0, 1} at the response handshake, with `security_disable_o` = 0 (the cells with `security_disable_o` = 1 are not bins of this test). Bins: (SLVERR, 0) at a window probe (CHK-PRESENSE); (OKAY, 1) at the read-back of a word whose read answered (SLVERR, 0) earlier in the same boot, with no `rst_ni` fall between the two reads (CHK-PRESENSE-CTL). An (OKAY, 1) read of a word with no SLVERR answer before it in the same boot is not a bin: every read after sensing gives it, so it does not show the window (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>). The cell (SLVERR, 1) is not a bin: the owning test scores SLVERR only with sense done low, and logs a response that arrives with sense done high. The `lc_state_o` layout is not stated, so no bin reads it (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>). Spec: `doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, OTP Fuse Controller and Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing |OKAY with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` = 0 and `security_disable_o` = 0 |`sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o`, `rst_ni`, LSU read address and response |`sep_boot_release_terms_test` |executable. Both epochs of every leaf fill both bins. The calibration boot runs with the graded window closed
|`cp_first_fetch` |first retired instruction after a `sep_cpu_reset_n` rise, with `mpc_reset_run_req_i` = 1 (the port default) {address = Boot ROM base 0x1004_0000, any other address}. The "any other address" bin needs a redirected reset vector. The value 0 of `mpc_reset_run_req_i` is not driven, because the specification gives no value-to-action map (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>). Spec: `doc/cpu.adoc`, Reset and Boot and Reset Vector; `doc/rom.adoc`, Boot ROM; `doc/port_table.adoc`, port list (`mpc_reset_run_req_i`) |A retired instruction before `sep_cpu_reset_n` = 1 |`sep_cpu_reset_n`, `cpu_trace_valid_o`, `cpu_trace_addr_o` |`sep_cpu_reset_entry_test` |executable. CHK-RSTVEC-DEFAULT fills the Boot ROM base bin and CHK-RSTVEC fills the other-address bin (the redirect through the TAP pin drive, an added hook), in every leaf
|===
