[[sep-fcov-p2-efuse-lifecycle]]
==== eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS and locked-field groups

These groups are the Phase 2 groups for the eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS
and locked-field tests. They are named in <<sep-fcov-planned>>. In the VPLAN
the owning tests are Phase 3 tests. The credit rule and the fill rules of
<<sep-fcov-planned>> apply to them.

Every group is VCS only. A covergroup does not compile under Verilator
(`cov/sv/sep_fcov.sv` is `ifndef VERILATOR`), so a Verilator run of an owning
test grades by checker only.

Names of specification and register description files are relative to the
`hw` directory of the repository. Short names: `lifecycle_controller.adoc`,
`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, `token_processing.adoc`, `security_disable.adoc`,
`test_mode.adoc`, `reset_controller.adoc`, `interrupts.adoc` and
`port_table.adoc` are in `sys/sep/doc/`. `sep_efuse_map.adoc` and
`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc` are in `sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/`.
`memory_map.adoc` is in `sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/`. `efuse_mmr.adoc`,
`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` and `architecture.adoc` are in
`ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/` (the first two) and `ip/efuse/doc/` (the last). The
RTL is read only to resolve a signal path. It never defines a bin.

===== Rules for every group

* Every coverpoint samples DUT signals (nets, ports or hierarchical taps) at a
  clock edge. No coverpoint samples log text, a scoreboard count or a test
  variable. A test-side expected value never enters a sampler. The one
  test-side input is the graded-window gate of <<sep-fcov-graded-window>>: it
  enables a sample and is never a sampled value.
* A cell lists its legal outcome. An outcome that the specification or the RDL
  forbids is an `illegal_bins` entry. A cell that no stimulus can fill, or whose
  value the specification does not define, is not declared. It is listed in
  <<sep-fcov-p2-efuse-lifecycle-notdeclared,Bins not graded>> with its reason.
* Sample guards: not in reset (`rst_ni`); not `+skip_fuse_sense` (the guard of
  the Phase 1 sampler); `fuse_sense_done_i` high for every coverpoint that reads
  shadow-derived state. The coverpoints that the sample event of
  `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` states with sense
  done low are the exception: `cp_match_phase`, `cp_stops_sense` and
  `cp_reset_values_hold`.
* Sample events:
** *Frontdoor completion*: completion of a frontdoor access, address decoded
   with the generated constants.
** *Sense done*: rising edge of `fuse_sense_done_i`.
** *Feature change*: any change of `feat_ctrl_i`, of a DIS word of the shadow
   probe, of `demote_1_i` / `demote_2_i`, of `sec_dis_i` or of `lc_sigint_i`,
   sampled once these inputs are stable. The specification gives no decode
   latency, so the settle rule belongs to the sampler code.
** *Token change*: change of a token status code or of a hash start pulse,
   sampled once the status code, `sec_dis_i`, the sticky fault bits and the
   interrupt line have been stable for N clocks (N is a sampler parameter). The
   specification gives no latency between the result code, the sticky bit and the
   interrupt, so a cell that compares two of these signals samples only at that
   settled point.
** *Program done*: rising edge of `efuse_program_done_o`.
* Lifecycle raw class from the sensed nibble (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`,
  section "Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile"): TEST_DEV `0000`, PROD `0001`,
  PROD_END `1000`, RMA_SIP `001X`, RMA_CHIPLET `011X`, INVALID every other value.
* Disable vector groups (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section "Disable Vector
  Format (SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS)"): DBG_1 `[23:0]`, DBG_2 `[47:24]`, Function
  `[63:48]`. Named bits: SEP_DBG 0, CHIPLET_DBG 1, SEP_FUSE_DBG 2,
  SMC_FUSE_DBG 3, SIP_DBG 24.
* Lock slots (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, sections "LOCKS" to "LOCKS_SPARE"): slot n
  has its write-lock at `LOCKS` bit 2n and its read-lock at bit 2n+1 for slots 0
  to 31. For slots 32 to 40 the bits are in `LOCKS_SPARE` at 2(n-32) and
  2(n-32)+1. Slot 0 is `LC_STATE`. The slot-to-field table comes from the RDL
  field list.
* Status in each group. A cell with no mark fills from the stimulus of the
  owning tests. The marks are:
** *Constraint*: the cell fills only when the owning test applies
   the stimulus constraint written under the group.
** *Blocked*: the cell needs a PROPOSED internal drive (<<sep-fcov-parked>>)
   or a value that the specification does not state. It is not counted. An added
   hook (an error inject or a top-port drive) and an added tap block no cell
   (<<sep-fcov-p2-unresolved-efuse-lifecycle,Unresolved signal paths>>). The Class column of each table gives the class of the coverpoint
   (<<sep-fcov-parked>>): `executable`, `executable in part. Blocked: <cells>
   <item>` or `parked: <item>`, followed by `seed-dependent` when the owning
   test draws the cells.
** *Not graded*: the cell is not declared. The reason is in
   <<sep-fcov-p2-efuse-lifecycle-notdeclared,Bins not graded>>.
** *Seed-dependent*: the owning test draws the cell from the leaf seed. A leaf
   has one t=0 image, so a cell that needs its own image, strap or leaf is a
   draw, and one regression can leave the cell empty. The coverpoint is graded
   on a merge of regressions, as <<sep-fcov-rand>> states for the `_rand_cg`
   groups, and the owning VPLAN entry states the expected closure. No statement
   says that one regression fills it. Every coverpoint that is not marked
   seed-dependent must fill in one VCS `all --cov` regression. A seed-dependent
   coverpoint can also carry the mark Constraint or Blocked, as its status
   says.

===== Sampled signals

Each group samples the signals below. The disposition column states whether the
sampler has the signal, whether the bench probe is approved (it exists in
`tb_top.sv` or is listed in `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`), which inputs are added taps and
which items are added hooks. An *Added tap* is a read-only reference outside that
closed set that the owning test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list;
it blocks no cell. An *added hook* is an error inject or a top-port drive outside
that closed set (<<sep-fcov-parked>>); it blocks no cell. Each absent input is listed in <<sep-fcov-p2-unresolved-efuse-lifecycle,Unresolved signal paths>>.

|`jtag_filt_sigint_i` |`jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o`, the integrity-error flag at the decoder output of the JTAG lifecycle filter |integrity-error state of the JTAG policy |Added tap. `lc_sigint_err_o` stays 0 under a filter-side injection, so `lc_sigint_i` is not the sample of the JTAG integrity-error state
|`token_cmp_fault_inject_i`, `token_cmp_fault_sel_i`, `lc_sigint_inject_i`, `token_digest_test_en_inject_i`, `jtag_ic_reset_*` |bench hooks |stimulus |Approved bench inputs. They are never a coverpoint input
|`jtag_id_i` drive, comparator copy select, `efuse_sense_status_inject_i`, `efuse_sense_status_word_i`, `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i`, `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`, test-enable inject of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS latches |bench hooks |stimulus |Added hooks: the drive of `jtag_id_i` is a top-port drive, and the others are error injects. They are never a coverpoint input
|OTP image preload and direct OTP word read |bench |image staging at t=0 and a word read through the frontdoor read interface |Approved test-side checks. No coverpoint samples them
|`otp_bank_probe_o` |OTP bank array, read only |image snapshot, bit diff and per-clock sample |Added tap. The bank loads once at t=0 and the bench has no rewrite hook. No coverpoint samples it
|===

Hierarchy roots in `tb_top.sv`: `SEP_CORE` is `u_dut.u_sep`. `EFUSE_CTRL` is
`SEP_CORE.u_sep_crypto.u_sep_efuse_wrapper.u_efuse_interface_controller`.
`TOKEN_PROC` is `EFUSE_CTRL.gen_mmr_reg.u_efuse_token_processing`.

A refusal coverpoint or check on `efuse_read_cmd_req_valid_o` or
`efuse_program_cmd_req_valid_o` always passes, because the guard drops a refused
command after those taps. Every "no program command at the shim" cell uses
the added tap `shim_cmd_i`. The flag bits of `ext_debug_bus_o` that the guard
raises have no definition in the specification, so no bin uses them.

===== `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: The eFuse window decodes to the specified extents, response codes
and register offsets.

*Basis*: `memory_map.adoc` (address map rows for the eFuse map, the interface
control, the token registers and the reserved ranges); `sep_efuse_map.adoc`
(section "SEP eFuse Map Address Map"); `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (section
"eFuse Interface Control Address Map"); `efuse_mmr.adoc` (section "eFuse Token
Registers Address Map").

*Sample event*: frontdoor completion in `0x1093_0000` to `0x1093_FFFF`.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`cp_window` |shadow `[0x1093_0000, 0x1093_03FF]`; ctrl_in `[0x1093_0400, 0x1093_041B]`; ctrl_past `[0x1093_041C, 0x1093_04FF]`; tok_in `[0x1093_0500, 0x1093_0573]`; rsvd `[0x1093_0600, 0x1093_FFFF]`. The span `0x1093_0574` to `0x1093_05FF` is not declared: the response differs between two documents |executable
|`cp_rsvd_zone` |low `[0x1093_0600, 0x1093_0FFF]`; high `[0x1093_1000, 0x1093_FFFF]` |executable
|`x_win_op_resp` |Window x op x response class {OKAY, not OKAY}, sampled only while `fuse_sense_done_i` is high, because before sense done every access to the shadow window answers SLVERR (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section "Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing"). 5 legal cells: (shadow, rd, OKAY); (ctrl_in, rd and wr, OKAY); (tok_in, rd and wr, OKAY). The ctrl_past and rsvd windows have no cell (see Bins not graded): `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades the response past the interface-control extent and the owning test only logs it, and no cited source states the CPU-side response of the reserved span. `illegal_bins`: an in-extent window with a non-OKAY response, except the `ignore_bins`. `ignore_bins`: (shadow, wr); (shadow, rd) while the read-lock bit of the field at the address is set in the LOCKS words of `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, because the response of a blocked read is not stated; (ctrl_in, wr) at `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, whose read-only fields make the response of a write not stated. The rule for the other registers: an access that the register access type of the register description does not offer is ignored, because the response is not stated. So every write to a register with access R is ignored, which are `EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` at `0xC`, `EFUSE_READ_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` at `0x10`, the three `*_TOKEN_MATCH` registers at `0x64`, `0x68` and `0x6C`, and `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` at `0x70`; and the read of the write-only `TOKEN_EOP` at `0x60` is ignored |executable
|`cp_shadow_field` |9 bins, one per graded narrow register of `sep_efuse_map.adoc` (the ten 32-bit rows except `LOCKS_SPARE`: `LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`), decoded by byte offset, on an OKAY 32-bit read of the graded used-bit leg. The word-by-word read of all 43 rows is a control of the owning test and fills no bin; the open owners of that compare keep it |executable
|`cp_edge_addr` |Last shadow word `0x1093_03FC`; last control register `0x1093_0418`; first past-extent word `0x1093_041C`; first reserved word `0x1093_0600` |executable
|`x_ctrl_reg_op` |10 cells: `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` (read and write each, 6 cells); `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` and `EFUSE_READ_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` (read, 4 cells). Offsets `0x0` to `0x18`. The write cells of the two timeout registers are not declared (see Bins not graded) |executable
|`x_token_blk_op` |10 cells: three token arrays (read and write, 6); three match registers (read, 3); `TOKEN_EOP` (write, 1). Offsets per `efuse_mmr.adoc`. A write to a read-only register of the block is not a cell. The `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` read is not declared (see Bins not graded) |executable
|`cp_jtag_id_class` |Nonzero, sampled on sense done from the DUT port `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, which the owning test drives with a drawn non-zero ID (the added top-port drive of `jtag_id_i`). 1 bin. The zero value is not declared (see Bins not graded) |executable
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test` (`cp_window`,
`cp_rsvd_zone`, `x_win_op_resp`, `cp_edge_addr`, `x_ctrl_reg_op`, the token
array cells of `x_token_blk_op`); `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test`
(`cp_shadow_field`, `cp_jtag_id_class`);
`sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test` (the `TOKEN_EOP` write cell and the three
match read cells of `x_token_blk_op`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Every coverpoint of the group samples the frontdoor or the
sensed-shadow probe, which are approved. Constraint for `cp_rsvd_zone`,
`cp_edge_addr` and `x_ctrl_reg_op`: `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`
pins the four edge addresses, draws one reserved address per reserved zone, and
reads `EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` and `EFUSE_READ_INTERFACE_READ_DATA`
(`0xC`, `0x10`). `cp_jtag_id_class` samples the DUT port that the owning test
drives in every leaf.

===== `sep_efuse_field_value_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: Narrow eFuse fields are sensed with their edge values.

*Basis*: `sep_efuse_map.adoc` (sections "SBOOT_DIS", "STATUS_RPT", "ROM_CTL",
"SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ", "CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE", "REQUIRED_SIGNERS", "REQUIRED_ALGS",
"BL1_VERSION").

*Sample event*: sense done, from the sensed shadow words (`efuse_shadow_probe_o`).

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`cp_sboot_dis` |0, 1 (`disable_secure_boot`, bit 0) |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_required_signers` |0, 1, 2, 3 (bits 1:0) |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_algs_owner_value` |Owner chiplet `[3:0]`, SiP `[7:4]`, system `[11:8]` x value of bits 1:0 in {0, 1, 2, 3}. 12 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_status_rpt` |0, 1, 2, 3 (`rpt` bits 1:0) |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_rom_ctl` |Endianness 0, 1; swap field `[5:1]` in {0, each single bit (5), all five set, other}. 10 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_sysclk_class` |1, 2 to 2046, 2047. The field is 11 bits. Value 0 is "ignored" and is not drawn |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_revoke_class` |0, one bit set, bits 25:0 all set, other nonzero |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_version_bit` |Field {BL1_VERSION, BL2_VERSION} x burned bit index class {0, 1 to 254, 255}, sampled on the first sense done after a program, from the new set bit of the sensed shadow words (`efuse_shadow_probe_o`). 6 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test` (all coverpoints except
`x_version_bit`); `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test` (`x_version_bit`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint and seed-dependent for every coverpoint of
the group. A leaf has one fuse image, so one leaf fills one class per
coverpoint. Uniform values seldom fall in an edge class, so each owner draws
the class first, with equal weight per class, and then a value inside it.
`sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test` draws in this way `SBOOT_DIS`, `STATUS_RPT`,
`REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, the three nibbles of `REQUIRED_ALGS`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`
(1, 2047 or a middle value), the `ROM_CTL` endianness and swap class, and the
`CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` class, and these coverpoints close in about 4
regressions. `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test` draws the pair (version
field, burned index class) in its chain leaves, and the 6 cells of
`x_version_bit` close in about 18 regressions.

===== `sep_lc_feat_ctrl_profile_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: The feature control profile that the lifecycle controller
produces for each sensed state, SEC_DIS value and demotion.

*Basis*: `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections "Debug and Functional Feature
Control", "Disable Vector Format (SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS)", "Combining Rule: OR then
Invert", "When Each DIS Vector Applies", "Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS)",
"Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile").

*Sample event*: feature change. Inputs: `efuse_lc_raw_i`, `sec_dis_i`,
`demote_1_i`, `demote_2_i`, the DIS words of `efuse_shadow_probe_o`,
`feat_ctrl_i`.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`cp_state_class` |TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_profile_row` |17 rows of the profile table: TEST_DEV x {00, 10, 01, 11} demotion with SEC_DIS 0, and SEC_DIS 1 (5); PROD, the same (5); PROD_END x SEC_DIS {0, 1} (2); RMA_SIP x SEC_DIS {0, 1} (2); RMA_CHIPLET (1); INVALID x SEC_DIS {0, 1} (2). Demotion is ignored where the table shows no entry |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_row_group_class` |17 rows x group {DBG_1, DBG_2, Function} x observed class {all ones, all zeros, `~SIP_DIS` of the group, `~(SIP_DIS or SYS_DIS)` of the group}. 51 legal cells, one class per cell as the table gives. Every other class is an `illegal_bins` entry. Sample guard per group: the SIP_DIS group slice is nonzero, SYS_DIS has a bit outside SIP_DIS, and the OR is neither zero nor all ones. Without the guard two classes can be equal |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_or_class` |Per group: OR of both vectors all zero (walk start), mixed (filter) |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_rma_dis_nonzero` |RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET sensed with a nonzero DIS word. 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` (all coverpoints).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint for `x_row_group_class`: in
every group the owning test makes SIP_DIS nonzero, gives SYS_DIS a bit outside
SIP_DIS, and keeps the OR away from zero and all ones. Every coverpoint of the
group is seed-dependent: the owning test draws one leaf plan (follow-on leg,
raw state code and strap) per leaf, so the group closes over a merge of
regressions.

===== `sep_lc_feature_bit_semantics_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: Named feature bits follow the disable vectors and the demotion
registers.

*Basis*: `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections "Disable Vector Format
(SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS)", "Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile", "In-Field Debug
Demotion").

*Sample event*: feature change.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_named_bit_value` |{SEP_DBG, CHIPLET_DBG, SEP_FUSE_DBG, SMC_FUSE_DBG, SIP_DBG} x {enabled (feature bit 1), disabled (0)}. 10 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_closed_bit_owner` |Bit {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 23, 24, 25, 47, 48, 63} x owner whose shadow bit rose {SIP_DIS, SYS_DIS}. Sampled on a fall of `feat_ctrl_i[b]` with the OR bit newly 1 and no change of state, demotion, SEC_DIS or sigint in the same window. 22 cells. The SYS_DIS cells need a state where SYS_DIS binds (TEST_DEV, PROD) |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_demote_group` |Demotion {DEMOTE_1, DEMOTE_2} x state {TEST_DEV, PROD}: groups whose feature bits changed. Legal: DEMOTE_1 changes DBG_1 only, DEMOTE_2 changes DBG_2 only. `illegal_bins`: a change in Function, or in the other debug group. 4 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`
(`x_named_bit_value` from the DFT-port leg and the owner walk,
`x_closed_bit_owner`, `x_demote_group`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint for `x_closed_bit_owner`: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`
fixes the owner of bits 0 to 3, 4, 23, 24, 25, 47, 48 and 63 (SIP_DIS in
TEST_DEV, SYS_DIS in PROD) and gives these bits double weight among the 16
positions that its walk draws. Every coverpoint of the group is
seed-dependent (one leaf plan per leaf) and closes over a merge of
regressions.

===== `sep_dtp_gating_ladder_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: The two fuse DFT gate ports follow the debug-bit ladder. Scope:
the fuse DFT gate ports only.

*Basis*: `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section "Debug and Test Port Path
Gating"); `port_table.adoc` (rows `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` and
`smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`).

*Sample event*: feature change.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_sep_port` |Case 3 true (SIP_DBG and CHIPLET_DBG and SEP_DBG) x SEP_FUSE_DBG x value of `sep_fuse_dft_disable_i` relative to its value in the all-open cell of the same leaf {same, other}. 4 legal cells: (Case 3, SEP_FUSE_DBG) same; the other three combinations other. The four opposite pairs are `illegal_bins` (the port is Case 3 AND SEP_FUSE_DBG). No bin says which value is the active level |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_smc_port` |Case 2 true (SIP_DBG and CHIPLET_DBG) x SMC_FUSE_DBG x value of `smc_fuse_dft_disable_i` relative to its value in the all-open cell of the same leaf {same, other}. 4 legal cells: (Case 2, SMC_FUSE_DBG) same; the other three combinations other. The four opposite pairs are `illegal_bins` (the port is Case 2 AND SMC_FUSE_DBG). No bin says which value is the active level |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_nesting` |Case 1 only, Case 2 not Case 3, Case 3. No `illegal_bins` entry: Case 3 implies Case 2 by the definition of the cases, so no sample can show the reverse |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` (the DFT-port leg).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: The specification does not state the polarity of the two gate ports, so no bin
and no `illegal_bins` entry names a level, and the port value enters only as
"same as" or "other than" its value in the all-open cell, which every leg E leaf
runs first (see Bins not graded). The check `CHK-DTP-PORT` of the owning test
compares the port values of the cells with each other by case class. Every
coverpoint is seed-dependent: a leg E leaf of the owning test walks six of the
32 combinations, one for each count of set bits, from all open to all closed.
`x_sep_port`, `x_smc_port` and `cp_nesting` close in about 6 regressions. The
32 combinations are not a coverpoint (see Bins not graded).

===== `sep_lc_sigint_failclosed_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: A lifecycle word that fails the integrity check closes the
feature control.

*Basis*: `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections "Lifecycle States", "Overrides
and Final Gates (SEC_DIS)", "Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile");
`otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section "Fuse Fields"); `port_table.adoc` (rows
`lc_state_o` and `lc_sigint_err_o`).

*Sample event*: sense done (`x_raw16_sigint`); change of `lc_sigint_i`, `sec_dis_i` or `feat_ctrl_i` while `lc_sigint_i` is high (`x_sigint_state_secdis_out`). The lifecycle byte is
word 3 of `efuse_shadow_probe_o`.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_raw16_sigint` |Complementary byte `{~r, r}` with r in 0 to 15 (16 bins) x `lc_sigint_i` 0, sampled on sense done. 16 legal cells. `lc_sigint_i` 1 at sense done is an `illegal_bins` entry |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_sigint_state_secdis_out` |State class of the sensed nibble `efuse_lc_raw_i` {TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET} x SEC_DIS {0, 1} x output class of `feat_ctrl_i` {all zeros, all ones, other}. Legal: SEC_DIS 0 gives all zeros, SEC_DIS 1 gives all ones. `illegal_bins`: other. 10 legal cells |executable; seed-dependent
|===

INVALID is not declared in `x_sigint_state_secdis_out`: its feature control is
all zeros with and without the integrity logic, so no check can fail on it.

*Owning tests*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` (`x_raw16_sigint`);
`sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test` (`x_sigint_state_secdis_out`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Seed-dependent for `x_raw16_sigint`: every leaf of
`sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` senses one drawn raw code, and the 16
cells close in about 25 regressions. Constraint and seed-dependent
for `x_sigint_state_secdis_out`: legs A and B of `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`
inject the error with the bench input `lc_sigint_inject_i` in one nibble per
leaf, drawn from the seven nibbles `{0000, 0001, 0010, 0011, 0110, 0111, 1000}`,
and the seven nibbles close in about 4 regressions. Leg A at the nibble `0111` is the cell that `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test`
injects, so it runs as a control of an open test, and the cells of that nibble still
fill in leg B of `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`.
PROD and PROD_END give a nonzero profile when the Function group has an open
bit, and the control epoch needs that. The injected value is fixed (both rails
of the byte at 0), so no corruption-distance cell is declared. INVALID is
excluded.

===== `sep_lc_illegal_transition_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: A token match updates the `LC_STATE` shadow through the transient
path only when the transient enable and the channel gate hold. No cell of this
group is a frontdoor write to `LC_STATE` (see <<sep-fcov-p2-efuse-lifecycle-notdeclared,Cells that are not declared>>).

*Basis*: `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section "Fuse Fields", row
`TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`); `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section "Lifecycle States");
`token_processing.adoc` (sections "Token Matching Mechanism and Attack
Countermeasures", "Token Submission", "Post Token Matching Handling");
`sep_efuse_map.adoc` (section "TRANSIENT_RMA_EN").

*Sample event*: a match code on `tok_sip_i` or `tok_chp_i`, with the old class
from `efuse_lc_raw_i` at the match rise. The outcome is sampled later: at the first
change of the `LC_STATE` word of `efuse_shadow_probe_o` after the match (updated),
or at the end of a window of N clocks after the match with no change (unchanged).
N is a sampler parameter and is not longer than the window W of the owning test.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_transient` |`TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` {0, 1} (word 5 bit 0) x matched channel {SIP, CHIPLET} x old class {TEST_DEV, PROD, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID} x outcome {updated, unchanged}, sampled as the sample event states. 20 legal cells. Updated is legal for: enable 1 and SIP from TEST_DEV or PROD; enable 1 and CHIPLET from RMA_SIP. The other 17 legal cells show unchanged. The 20 other cells are `illegal_bins` |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`.

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint and seed-dependent for `x_transient`:
`sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test` draws per leaf the pair (enable, start
state) and the script (SIP only, CHIPLET only, or a two-token order), so the 20
cells of (enable, channel, start state) close over a merge of regressions, in
about 16.

===== `sep_efuse_lock_policy_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: Write-locks refuse programs, read-locks block reads, and
the exempt words follow their own rules, per lock slot.

*Basis*: `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections "Shadow Register and JTAG Access
Control", "Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization", "Fuse Fields");
`sep_efuse_map.adoc` (sections "SEP eFuse Map Address Map", "LOCKS",
"LOCKS_SPARE"); `token_processing.adoc` (section "Post Token Matching
Handling").

*Sample event*: for a program, the frontdoor read of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`
that ends the program wait W (a refused program can end without program done);
the frontdoor read of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`
that ends a read-interface request; frontdoor completion on a shadow read or write.
The program result comes from the post-guard shim command (`shim_cmd_i`), not
from the interface status.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_slot_prog` |Slot 1 to 40 x write-lock {0, 1} x outcome {landed (program command at the shim), refused (none)}. 80 legal cells: (0, landed), (1, refused). (1, landed) and (0, refused) are `illegal_bins` |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_slot_read` |Slot 1 to 40 x read-lock {0, 1}. 80 legal cells: with the lock clear, a frontdoor read of the field returns the sensed shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`; with the lock set, a read-interface request on a bit of the field sets `efuse_req_error`, sampled from the frontdoor read of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` that ends the request, with the field taken from `efuse_addr` of the `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go write (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section "Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control": a read-lock blocks software reads of the fuse field and its shadow; `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, field `efuse_req_error`). `illegal_bins`: lock clear with read data that differs from the sensed word; lock set with a request that ends with `efuse_req_error` 0. The response and the data of a frontdoor read that a read-lock blocks are not bins: the specification does not state them |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_kind_op` |Lock kind {none, write, read, write and read} x op {program, read}. 8 legal cells: a write-lock refuses a program and not a read; a read-lock blocks a read and not a program. A read cell of a read-locked kind is a read-interface request that sets `efuse_req_error`; a read cell of another kind is a frontdoor read that returns the sensed word |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_neighbor_free` |Target slot unlocked while slot minus 1 or slot plus 1 holds a lock {previous, next} x op {program landed, read not blocked}. 4 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_exempt` |Frontdoor OKAY read whose data equals the sensed word of the register at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, for {`LC_STATE`, `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`}, while every assigned lock bit of the `LOCKS` words at the probe is set. 3 bins. A write or program of `LOCKS` or `LOCKS_SPARE` is not declared: with every assigned lock bit set no new assigned bit can be written, and bits 31:18 of `LOCKS_SPARE` are reserved (`SPARE_LOCK_RSVD`) |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_sense_fills_locked` |At sense done, a read-locked field whose sensed words are nonzero: secret field {CHIPLET_UID, SIP_UID, SYS_UID, CLASS_KEY}, other field. 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_shadow_write_lock` |Shadow-write field {SIP_DIS (slot 3), SYS_DIS (slot 4), REQUIRED_SIGNERS (slot 21), REQUIRED_ALGS (slot 22)} x write-lock {0, 1} x outcome {changed (OR), unchanged}. 8 legal cells. The write data is the shadow value OR one new bit, so the open write class of REQUIRED_SIGNERS does not change the outcome |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_lock_order` |Write before lock lands; write after lock blocked; zero write after lock keeps the lock. 3 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_prog_visibility` |After a landed program the shadow word equals its value before the program (1 bin). At the next sense done the word has the programmed bit and every earlier bit (1 bin) |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_round_count` |Round index k = the number of consecutive sense-done events, up to and including this one, at each of which the sensed shadow words (`efuse_shadow_probe_o`) gained at least one set bit against the previous sense-done; a sense-done with no gain resets k to 0. Sampled at sense done, bins k = 1, 2, 3, 4 (each round ends with a cold-reset sense). 4 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_lc_prog_gate` |`LC_STATE` bit {1, 2} x token state {own channel matched, other channel matched only} x outcome of the program at the shim. Legal: own match lands, other match is refused. 3 legal cells: (bit 1, own), (bit 1, other only), (bit 2, own). (bit 2, other channel matched only) is not a bin: it is a control of the owner, and `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` grades that refusal (see Bins not graded) |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test` (every coverpoint
except `x_lc_prog_gate`); `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`
(`x_lc_prog_gate`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: `x_slot_prog`, the program cells of `x_kind_op` and
`x_neighbor_free`, `cp_prog_visibility` and `x_lc_prog_gate` sample the program
command at the bank port at the added tap `shim_cmd_i`. Constraint
for `x_slot_prog`, `x_slot_read` and `x_neighbor_free`: in a
lock leaf of `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test` the drawn target slot takes
the lock kind write and read with both neighbours unlocked, every other data
slot of 3 to 40 takes a drawn lock kind, and the leaf reads every slot of 1 to
40. Constraint for `cp_round_count`: the chain leaf draws the
round count R in 1 to 4, so the round index 1 to R is sampled at each
post-program sense. Every coverpoint of the group is seed-dependent (one leaf
kind and one image per leaf) and closes over a merge of regressions:
`x_slot_read` in about 4, `x_slot_prog` in about 45,
and `x_exempt` in about 3. The lock kind
read alone is not stimulated on slots 11, 13 and 15 (the UID fields), because the
RDL and the field table differ on whether a read-lock removes writability, so the
(read-lock, program) cell of `x_kind_op` is filled by the other slots and the UID
slots fill their `x_slot_prog` cells with the kinds none, write and write and read.

===== `sep_lc_demotion_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: The volatile demotion registers combine with each lifecycle
state, reset to zero and export their codeword. The write source is not
declared: the owning tests write from the frontdoor only.

*Basis*: `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections "Lifecycle States",
"Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile", "In-Field Debug Demotion", "Reset
Behavior"); `port_table.adoc` (rows `lcc_demote_state_1_o` and
`lcc_demote_state_2_o`); `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section "RTL
Integration").

*Sample event*: feature change; sense done.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_state_combo` |State class (6) x demotion combination {00, 10, 01, 11}. 24 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_assert_order` |DEMOTE_1 set first; DEMOTE_2 set first |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_reset_vector` |Before a cold reset, the vector (demote 1 as bit 3, lock 1, demote 2, lock 2 as bit 0): values 1 to 15. After the reset release and sense done all four read 0. 15 cells. `ignore_bins`: vector 0, because a reset of a clear state has nothing to clear |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_otp_invariant` |State class (6: TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID) x demotion combination (4): the lifecycle raw nibble and the `LC_STATE` word are unchanged across the demotion. 24 cells. A program command at the shim is not part of the cell (CHK-DEMOTE-OTP grades that statement) |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_export_codeword` |`lcc_demote_state_1_o` and `lcc_demote_state_2_o` x pair class {complementary, non-complementary}. 2 legal cells: complementary. The non-complementary class (`2'b00` and `2'b11`) is an `illegal_bins` entry. The specification states no polarity, so no bin names a value |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_export_changes` |Per register: the pair after the demote write differs from the pair before it. 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` (every coverpoint).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint for
`cp_reset_vector` and `x_otp_invariant`: the demotion leg takes the first cold reset
from a clean state after a scripted path that writes only the bits of the vector,
demote before lock. Each leg B leaf draws the vector v (1 to 15) and the raw
state code of its image, so each of the six base state classes runs the four
demotion combinations over a merge of regressions. Every coverpoint of the
group is seed-dependent (one leaf plan per leaf), and `cp_reset_vector` closes
in about 30 regressions.

===== `sep_lc_demote_lock_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: A demotion register accepts writes until its lock bit is set, and
the two registers are independent.

*Basis*: `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections "Lifecycle States", "In-Field
Debug Demotion", "Programming and Locking"); `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`
(sections "SEP Lifecycle Control Address Map", "DEMOTE_1").

*Sample event*: completion of a frontdoor write to `DEMOTE_1` or `DEMOTE_2`. Old
and new (demote, lock) come from `demote_1_i`, `demote_2_i`, `demote_lock_1_i`
and `demote_lock_2_i`.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_reg_lock_write` |Register {1, 2} x old lock {0, 1} x write {set demote (data `0x1`), set lock (`0x2`), zero} x result {changed, unchanged}. 12 legal cells: with lock 0, set demote and set lock change and zero is unchanged; with lock 1 every write is unchanged. A change with lock 1 is an `illegal_bins` entry. Guard for set demote: old demote 0 |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_committed_next` |Register x committed demote {0, 1} (lock set) x next write {set demote, zero}. 8 legal cells, all unchanged |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_independence` |Register 1 locked, then a register 2 write is accepted; register 2 locked, then a register 1 write is accepted. 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_profile_unchanged` |A write to a register whose old lock is 1, with `feat_ctrl_i` equal before and after, the other register's demote state unchanged and the shadow DIS word of the group that the register governs nonzero. Per register: 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` (the demotion leg).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint for `x_reg_lock_write` and `x_committed_next`: the
leg runs the forced block in every leg B leaf on the drawn register A and on
the other register B (lock then demote and demote then lock each on its own
register, a zero write after each set, and a later set and a later zero write on
a committed demote of 0 and of 1), and the draw of A runs each register as A and
as B over a merge of regressions. Every coverpoint of the group is
seed-dependent, because a leg B leaf is one of the drawn leaf plans.

===== `sep_token_channel_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: Each token channel (RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS) returns the
specified status code, keeps its words, and stays independent of the other
channels.

*Basis*: `token_processing.adoc` (sections "Token Matching Mechanism and Attack
Countermeasures", "Token Submission"); `security_disable.adoc` (section
"Implementation Requirements"); `efuse_mmr.adoc` (sections
"SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[8]", "RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH").

*Sample event*: token change.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_ch_verdict` |Channel {RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS} x first non-idle code after a go {Match `0x15`, Mismatch `0x2a`}. 6 cells |executable
|`cp_code` |Every observed status value in {`0x00`, `0x15`, `0x2a`, `0x3f`}. The other 60 six-bit values are `illegal_bins` |executable
|`x_ch_shape` |Channel x shape of the presented words {all zeros, all ones, other channel's token}, classified from the token word registers. Legal verdict: Mismatch. 8 cells. Excluded on SEC_DIS: all zeros (the bound SEC_DIS token is zero and that token matches). The flip, swap and byte-swap shapes are not bins, because they need the bench token vectors, which are test-side values; the checker of the owning test grades them |executable
|`x_rma_go_phase` |RMA channel {SIP, CHIPLET} x go while `fuse_sense_done_i` is low (code stays `0x00` until sense done) / go after sense done (Match). 4 bins |executable
|`cp_independence` |Go on channel c {SIP, CHIPLET, SEC_DIS} while both other channels hold a non-idle code and keep it. 3 bins |executable
|`cp_hold_word_write` |A token word write with a non-idle code held on the channel and the code unchanged. 3 bins (per channel) |executable
|`cp_go_idle_first` |A `token_go_i` pulse of the channel while its code is non-idle, after which the code of the channel equals `0x00` on at least one clock before it next takes a non-idle value. 3 bins (per channel). The sampler reads `token_go_i` and the codes `tok_sip_i`, `tok_chp_i` and `tok_sdis_i` on every clock, so the bin does not depend on the read cadence of the leaf (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: a start clears digest validity, so the comparison returns idle while the new digest is unavailable) |executable
|`cp_retention` |Cold reset with an RMA_SIP Match held: token words equal before and after (1); code `0x00` before sense done (1); code `0x15` after sense done with no new go (1). 3 bins |executable
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test` (every coverpoint; bins
`0x00`, `0x15` and `0x2a` of `cp_code`); `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test`
(bin `0x3f` of `cp_code`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: The status codes of RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET sample the
existing debug-bus path (`tok_sip_i`, `tok_chp_i`). The SEC_DIS cells sample
the added tap `tok_sdis_i`; `x_ch_shape`, the word-write cell `cp_hold_word_write`
and bin 1 of `cp_retention` sample the added tap `tok_words_i`; and
`cp_go_idle_first` samples the added tap `token_go_i`.
Constraint for `x_ch_shape` and `x_rma_go_phase`: the test uses 7
mismatch shapes, and every leaf walks every legal (shape, channel) cell, with
digest-as-token as one shape; it never uses the
all-zero, swap or byte-swap shape on SEC_DIS; and it runs the go before sense done
on RMA_CHIPLET as well as on RMA_SIP.

===== `sep_token_fault_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: A fault in one comparator copy sets a sticky bit, drives the
interrupt line, and gives no SEC_DIS match. A test enable at the digest latch of
a channel freezes the retained digest and its valid bit.

*Basis*: `token_processing.adoc` (sections "Token Matching Mechanism and Attack
Countermeasures", "Matching Logic Countermeasures");
`efuse_mmr.adoc` (section "TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT"); `interrupts.adoc` (section
"SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map").

*Sample event*: change of the sticky bits (`tok_fault_i`), of a status code, or
a status flip with no go. Copy and kind are classified from `cmp_match_p_i` and
`cmp_match_n_i`.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_ch_kind_copy` |Channel (3) x kind {collapsed pair (`match_p[i] == match_n[i]`), one copy dissents} x copy i {0, 1, 2}. 14 cells. Copy 0: 2 cells (one copy dissents, on RMA_CHIPLET and on SEC_DIS). The copy-0 collapsed-pair cells of the three channels and the copy-0 dissent cell of RMA_SIP are not bins: the owner does not draw them (see Bins not graded). Copies 1 and 2: 12 cells, which need the added comparator copy select |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_ch_common_flip` |RMA_CHIPLET x unanimous legal flip with no go {Match to Mismatch, Mismatch to Match}. No sticky bit rises. 2 cells. The RMA_SIP flip is not a cell of this group (see Bins not graded) |executable
|`x_ch_forced_err` |Channel x (code `0x3f` with the channel's sticky bit set and `irq_token_fault_i` high). 3 cells. `0x3f` without the sticky bit is an `illegal_bins` entry |executable
|`x_ch_retry` |After a fault, a valid go gives {Match, Mismatch} while the channel's sticky bit and the interrupt line stay high. 5 cells: the RMA channels with both verdicts, SEC_DIS with Match |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_no_clear` |A write to `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (`0x1093_0570`) with the channel's bit set, sticky bit unchanged at the next sample. 3 bins |executable
|`cp_reset_clear` |Cold reset: the sticky bit and the interrupt line fall to 0. 3 bins (per channel). Token word retention over a cold reset is `cp_retention` of `sep_token_channel_cg`; the owner of this group logs the words and does not grade them |executable
|`cp_secdis_off_under_fault` |SEC_DIS fault: `sec_dis_i` stays 0. 1 bin |executable
|`x_ch_latch_freeze` |Channel {RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS} x test enable at the digest latch {asserted, released} x token operation. Legal: under the freeze the sticky digest and the valid bit keep their values (2 cells); after the release the digest is replaced (2 cells). 4 cells. A change under the freeze is an `illegal_bins` entry. No cell names a result code under the freeze, because the specification states no effect on a match in progress. The RMA_SIP cells are not bins (see Bins not graded) |executable
|`cp_irq_line` |`irq_token_fault_i` equals the OR of the sticky bits for the single-channel values 001, 010, 100. 3 bins. Multi-channel values are not stimulated and are not declared |executable
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test`.

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: The sticky bits sample the added tap `tok_fault_i`, and the copy and
kind sample the added taps `cmp_match_p_i` and `cmp_match_n_i` (the comparator
rails). `cp_irq_line` compares the line with the sticky bits. The twelve cells of
copies 1 and 2 of `x_ch_kind_copy` need the added comparator copy select. The
injection input `token_cmp_fault_inject_i` and the copy select are stimulus and
never a sampler input. For `x_ch_retry`,
the owning test draws the held phase (match or mismatch) of the fault cell of
each RMA channel in every leaf, and the retry presents the token of the opposite verdict
(a retry that does not run then leaves the held verdict and fails), so each
(channel, verdict) retry cell fills over a merge of regressions. `x_ch_kind_copy`
and `x_ch_retry` are seed-dependent, because each leaf draws one fault cell per
channel. The other coverpoints fill on every seed: every leaf has a new fault
cell on each channel (the owner draws no copy-0 collapse cell and no copy-0
dissent cell of RMA_SIP) and runs the two common-mode cells and the three freeze
cells. The SEC_DIS channel holds a mismatch only, so
its retry gives a match.
`x_ch_latch_freeze` samples the added latch taps of RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS,
whose freeze needs the added test-enable inject.

===== `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: SEC_DIS activates on a token match at each phase, forces the
feature control to all ones, persists through a cold reset, and clears after a
mismatch.

*Basis*: `security_disable.adoc` (sections "Token Activation", "Fuse Sensing and
the Shadow Map", "Reset Persistence"); `token_processing.adoc` (sections "Token
Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures", "Token Submission");
`lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections "When Each DIS Vector Applies",
"Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS)"); `architecture.adoc` (section "Security
Disable Bypass Mechanism"); `reset_controller.adoc` (section "Isolation and
Reset Sequencing").

*Sample event*: change of `sec_dis_i`; token change on `tok_sdis_i`; rise of
`rst_ni`. Reset classes other than cold reset are not declared. `cp_match_phase`
samples with `fuse_sense_done_i` low before sense, so the sense-done guard does
not apply to it.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`cp_match_phase` |Before sense (`sec_dis_i` high at reset release, sense not done); during sense (rises with `fuse_sense_done_i` low, no reset); after sense (rises with `fuse_sense_done_i` high). 3 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_verdict_out` |SEC_DIS code {Match, Mismatch, Error} x `sec_dis_i` {0, 1}. Legal: (Match, 1), (Mismatch, 0), (Error, 0). The other 3 are `illegal_bins` |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_state_demote_forced` |`sec_dis_i` 1 with `feat_ctrl_i` all ones x state x demotion. TEST_DEV and PROD each x 4 combinations (8 cells); PROD_END, RMA_SIP, INVALID with any demotion (3 cells). 11 cells. The RMA_CHIPLET cell is not a bin (see Bins not graded). `feat_ctrl_i` not all ones is an `illegal_bins` entry |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test` (`cp_match_phase` before and during sense);
`sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test` (`x_verdict_out`, Error cells);
`sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` (`cp_match_phase` after sense,
`x_state_demote_forced`, `x_verdict_out` Match and Mismatch cells).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: `x_verdict_out` samples the SEC_DIS code at the added tap `tok_sdis_i`. Its Match and Mismatch cells fill in leg C of `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`, in a state other than RMA_CHIPLET, where CHK-FEAT-CELL grades `security_disable_o` high at the `0x15` match and low after the `0x2a` mismatch. The before-sense and during-sense bins of `cp_match_phase` fill in `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`, and the after-sense bin fills in the zero-token step of `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`. Constraint for
`x_state_demote_forced`: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` runs the match
and the dropping mismatch at each demotion cell of its state. Seed-dependent:
the before-sense and during-sense bins of `cp_match_phase` (one class of
`sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test` per leaf), the after-sense bin and the Match and Mismatch cells of `x_verdict_out` (a leg C leaf of
`sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`) and `x_state_demote_forced` (drawn
states).

===== `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: A replayed digest never matches, SEC_DIS stops fuse sensing, and
the reset values hold while SEC_DIS is active.

*Basis*: `token_processing.adoc` (section "Token Submission");
`security_disable.adoc` (sections "Token Activation", "Fuse Sensing and the
Shadow Map"); `test_mode.adoc` (sections "Test Mode Entry", "Protection While
Active"); `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section "Overrides and Final Gates
(SEC_DIS)").

*Sample event*: token change on `tok_*_i` (`x_digest_replay`); change of `sec_dis_i` or `feat_ctrl_i`
(`x_state_strap_feat`); each clock while `sec_dis_i` is high and `fuse_sense_done_i`
is low (`cp_stops_sense`, `cp_reset_values_hold`). The guard
"`fuse_sense_done_i` high" does not apply to the last two coverpoints, because they
sample the SEC_DIS-held window in which sense done stays low.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_digest_replay` |Channel (3) x go with the token words equal to the channel's expected digest (RMA: the OTP digest words; SEC_DIS: the gate constant) x code {Mismatch legal, Match `illegal_bins`}. 3 legal cells |executable
|`cp_stops_sense` |`sec_dis_i` 1 and `fuse_sense_done_i` 0 for a bound of 3 times the sense time measured in the run (the interval from the first reset release to sense done), which is the window W of `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`. Phase {match during sense: the sensed shadow words (`efuse_shadow_probe_o`) stay unchanged from the rise of `sec_dis_i`; match before sense: the sensed shadow words stay at their reset values from reset release}. 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|`x_state_strap_feat` |State {TEST_DEV, PROD, RMA_SIP} x strap {0, 1}, sampled on a feature change with `sec_dis_i` 0 (the value comparison belongs to the checker). 6 cells |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_reset_values_hold` |`sec_dis_i` 1 and `fuse_sense_done_i` 0 after a reset: every shadow word except the `LC_STATE` word reads 0. The `LC_STATE` word is excluded: the specification and the RDL differ on its reset value |executable
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test` (`x_digest_replay`);
`sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test` (`cp_stops_sense`,
`cp_reset_values_hold`); `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`
(`x_state_strap_feat`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: `cp_stops_sense` needs a
completed control sense in the run: a run with no completed sense records no
hit. Constraint for `x_digest_replay`: digest-as-token is one of
the mismatch shapes of `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`. It samples the added
taps `tok_words_i` and `sec_disable_expected_i`. Seed-dependent: the two phase bins of
`cp_stops_sense` (one class of `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test` per leaf) and
`x_state_strap_feat` (drawn states). `cp_reset_values_hold` fills in both
classes, on every seed.

===== `sep_efuse_if_status_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: Each refused or invalid eFuse interface request sets the
specified status flags, and each request that the specification blocks issues
no command at the shim.

*Basis*: `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (sections "EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS",
"EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL", "EFUSE_READ_CTRL"); `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections
"OTP Fuse Controller", "Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control", "Fuse
Programming and RMA Authorization", "Fuse Fields": the fuse map is exactly
8192 bits, end address `0x400`); `architecture.adoc` (section "Command-path
ownership").

*Sample event*: a go on `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` or `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, then a sample
once the interface is idle (`efuse_read_busy_o` and `efuse_program_busy_o`
low). For the case `both_enables` the read may never complete, so that case is
sampled a fixed number of clocks after the go (a sampler parameter) or at the next
reset, whichever comes first. `cp_sense_status_word` and `x_prog_lifecycle` have their own
sample events, given in their rows.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`cp_case` |pre_sense_prog (go while `fuse_sense_done_i` is low); read_no_enable; read_oob (address of 8192 or more); prog_oob; prog_wlocked (the program address lies in a field whose write-lock bit is set in the LOCKS words of `efuse_shadow_probe_o`); read_rlocked (the read address lies in a field whose read-lock bit is set there); both_enables; prog_data0. 8 bins |executable
|`cp_flags` |Observed `{read_status, read_addr_error, program_addr_error, req_error}`: 0000, 1000, 1100, 0010, 0001, sampled only for read_no_enable, read_oob, prog_oob and prog_wlocked, and 0000 for the accepted twins. Every other value in those cases is an `illegal_bins` entry. Not sampled for pre_sense_prog, prog_data0, both_enables and read_rlocked |executable
|`x_case_flags` |read_no_enable gives 1000; read_oob gives 1100; prog_oob gives 0010; prog_wlocked gives 0001; read_rlocked gives `req_error` = 1 and the other flags are not graded; 5 legal cells. pre_sense_prog, prog_data0 and both_enables: the RDL does not state the flags (`architecture.adoc` states only that a read under `program_enable` does not reach the bank), so these cells are `ignore_bins`. The flags come from the added status taps `rd_addr_err_i`, `pg_addr_err_i` and `req_err_i` |executable
|`x_case_cmd` |Case x commands at the shim during the window {none, read, program}. Legal: no program command for pre_sense_prog (window from reset release to sense done); no read command for read_rlocked (window from the go to the end of W) and both_enables (window from the go to the cold reset). pre_sense_prog allows read commands (sense). read_no_enable, read_oob and prog_oob are not declared: no document states that these requests issue no command (the register description states only their flags). prog_wlocked and prog_data0 are not cells (see Bins not graded). 3 cells |executable
|`cp_flag_clear` |Write 1 to a clear bit: `efuse_read_addr_error` falls, `efuse_program_addr_error` falls, `efuse_req_error` falls, the other flags unchanged. 3 bins |executable
|`cp_sense_status_word` |A sense response with its status bit set at a word w of 10 to 254. The response is a clock with `shim_resp_i.valid` and `shim_resp_i.status` both 1 while `fuse_sense_done_i` is 0. w is bits [12:5] of `shim_cmd_i.address` of the READ command that the response answers, the last READ command with `shim_cmd_i.valid` 1 before the response (the address is a bit address: `interface.adoc`, section Fuse Command Protocol). The bin scores at the next rise of `fuse_sense_done_i` when word w of `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equals its reset value `0x0`. 1 bin. Words 0 to 9 and 255 are not declared (see Bins not graded) |executable
|`x_prog_lifecycle` |Program request with the program timeout disabled x phase {response withheld, response released}. The request is a PROGRAM command at the shim (`shim_cmd_i.valid` 1, command `2'b01`) while `program_req_timeout_enable` is 0: no frontdoor write to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` since reset (reset value 0), or the last such write, decoded from `lsu_aw_*` and `lsu_w_*`, wrote it 0. Withheld: sampled N clocks after the command (N a sampler parameter) when `shim_resp_i.valid` has stayed 0 since the command; legal is `efuse_program_busy_o` high and `efuse_program_done_o` low. Released: only for a command that scored the withheld cell, sampled N clocks after the first rise of `efuse_program_done_o` that follows the rise of `shim_resp_i.valid`; legal is done high, `efuse_program_error_o` low and busy low. 2 cells. The outcome with the timeout enabled is not a bin |executable
|`cp_accepted_twin` |A valid read (`read_done` high, `read_status` low); a landed program (program command at the shim, flags 0). 2 bins |executable
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test` (every coverpoint
except `cp_sense_status_word` and `x_prog_lifecycle`);
`sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test` (`cp_sense_status_word`,
`x_prog_lifecycle`).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: The owning test runs all eight cases in every leaf. The
out-of-range bound is 8192 bits (`hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`: 256 words of
32 bits). `x_case_cmd` and the program bin of `cp_accepted_twin` sample
the added tap `shim_cmd_i`, and the flag coverpoints (`cp_flags`, `x_case_flags`,
`cp_flag_clear`) sample the added address-error taps. The stimulus of
`cp_sense_status_word` and `x_prog_lifecycle` is the pair of added error injects
`efuse_sense_status_inject_i` and `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i`; both fill on every
seed. The two coverpoints sample the added tap `shim_resp_i` with `shim_cmd_i`,
`fuse_sense_done_i`, `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the program interface outputs; the
injects are stimulus only.

===== `sep_efuse_secure_tm_cg`

*Class*: executable.

*Description*: With `secure_tm_i` high the eFuse interface blocks protected
writes and programs, masks the secret fields, and keeps the frontdoor read.

*Basis*: `test_mode.adoc` (sections "Test Mode Entry", "Entry and Exit",
"Protection While Active"); `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections "OTP Fuse
Controller", "Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection");
`lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section "Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS)");
`architecture.adoc` (section "Secure Test Mode (secure_tm_i)"); `port_table.adoc`
(rows `secure_tm_o` and `secure_tm_req_i`); `test_mode.adoc` (section "Entry and
Exit": the chiplet `TEST_EN` strap drives `secure_tm_req_i`). `test_en_strap_i`
is the `tb_top` name of the strap signal.

*Sample event*: frontdoor completion of a
shadow read (`x_secret_read_kept`); a program go, then a sample
once the interface is idle (`efuse_program_busy_o` low) (`x_prog_block`);
frontdoor completion of a read
of a secret field, sampled together with the sensed-shadow output of that field at
the same edge (`x_secret_probe`).

*Tool*: VCS.

The group declares no cell for the JTAG write of a protection shadow (see Bins
not graded). The memory, boot and
reset groups cover the strap latch: `cp_stm_event_x_strap` holds the early
capture before sense done, and `cp_secure_tm_state` holds the cold-reset clear.
The value after the fuse-sense completion sample has no bin, because
`sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` grades it (<<sep-fcov-p2-mem-boot-reset-unowned>>).
This group declares no cell for the strap latch.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`x_prog_block` |Program class {256-bit field, 32-bit field, DIS bit, `LOCKS` bit, spare word outside the five protection shadows} x strap x program command at the shim {seen, none}. Legal: strap 0 seen, strap 1 none. 10 legal cells |executable
|`x_secret_probe` |Secret field {CHIPLET_UID, SIP_UID, SYS_UID, CLASS_KEY} at strap 1 x sensed-shadow output class {zero, nonzero}, with the stored value nonzero at the frontdoor read. Legal: zero. `illegal_bins`: nonzero. 4 legal cells. The strap-0 cells and a strap-1 value that only differs from the staged value are not declared: `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` grades them, and the owning test credits only the exact zero |executable
|`x_secret_read_kept` |Strap 1, frontdoor OKAY read of an unlocked secret field with nonzero data, per field. 4 bins |executable
|===

*Owning test*: `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`.

*Status*: `x_prog_block` samples the added tap `shim_cmd_i`. The sensed-shadow probe is the masked output, so under `secure_tm_i` it
shows zero for secret words; `x_secret_probe` reads the stored value at the
frontdoor. The Key Manager consumer view of the masked fields belongs to
`sep_km_otp_interface_rand_test` and is not a cell of this group.
Every coverpoint fills in every leaf.

===== `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg`

*Class*: executable (<<sep-fcov-parked>>).

*Description*: The JTAG AXI-Lite port allows the token and match registers and
denies the shadow and control registers, per lifecycle state.

*Basis*: `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section "Shadow Register and JTAG Access
Control"); `architecture.adoc` (section "Lifecycle State (LC_STATE) Effects");
`lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section "Debug and Test Port Path Gating");
`efuse_mmr.adoc` (sections "eFuse Token Registers Address Map",
"SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[8]"); `memory_map.adoc` (address map rows for the eFuse
window).

*Sample event*: completion of a JTAG AXI-Lite access (B or R handshake on
`jtag_req_i` and `jtag_resp_i`). The class comes from the address, decoded with
the generated constants.

[cols="2,5,3",options="header"]
|===
|Coverpoint |Bins |Class
|`cp_state` |PROD; RMA_SIP; integrity error (`jtag_filt_sigint_i` high, in TEST_DEV) |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_class` |Shadow word; interface-control register; token word; `TOKEN_EOP`; match register. `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` is not declared: the specification does not state the JTAG policy for it |executable
|`cp_resp` |OKAY, not OKAY. The specification states only "an error response" for a denied access, so the code is not a bin |executable
|`x_state_class_op` |State (3) x class and op: allowed (token word read, token word write, `TOKEN_EOP` write, match read) with response OKAY; denied (shadow read, shadow write, control read, control write) with a response that is not OKAY. 14 legal cells for PROD and RMA_SIP: (PROD, token word read) and (PROD, token word write) are `ignore_bins`, because the open test `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test` grades them and the owning test only logs them. A denied class with OKAY, or an allowed class with an error, is an `illegal_bins` entry. `TOKEN_EOP` read and match write are not declared. The integrity-error state adds 8 cells of the same form (the allowed classes answer OKAY and the denied classes answer not OKAY, `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section "Debug and Test Port Path Gating", row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path), so the coverpoint has 22 legal cells. The integrity-error cells need the added error inject `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i` at the JTAG lifecycle filter and sample the added tap `jtag_filt_sigint_i` |executable; seed-dependent
|`cp_gate_independent_of_feat` |State {PROD, RMA_SIP} x an allowed access that completes OKAY while `feat_ctrl_i` has every DBG_1 and DBG_2 bit closed. 2 bins |executable; seed-dependent
|===

*Owning tests*: `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test` (PROD and RMA_SIP cells);
`sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test` (integrity-error cells).

*Tool*: VCS.

*Status*: Constraint for `cp_gate_independent_of_feat`: in RMA_SIP the owning
test stages `SIP_DIS` with every DBG_1 and DBG_2 bit set. Constraint
for `x_state_class_op`: the specification states no response for
`TOKEN_EOP` read or match write, so the test draws `TOKEN_EOP` as write only and
the match registers as read only, and draws both operations for the four denied
classes. The JTAG port is live only in the `lsu_stub_all_live` target. The specification
does not state the JTAG policy of the other states, so the PROD and
RMA_SIP cells come from `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test`, and the
integrity-error cells come from leg C of `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`,
which drives the added error inject `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i` and samples
`jtag_filt_sigint_i`. Leg C covers all eight classes of the integrity-error state. The
approved `lc_sigint_inject_i` acts at the lifecycle decoder, and `lc_sigint_i` stays
low under the filter-side injection, so it is not the sample of this state.
`cp_state`, `x_state_class_op` and `cp_gate_independent_of_feat` are
seed-dependent: each leaf of `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test` draws one state,
PROD or RMA_SIP, and leg C runs only in a leaf of
`sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test` that draws the nibble `0000`. `cp_class` and
`cp_resp` fill in every leaf.
